========== Backend\src\app.js ==========

import express from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import helmet from "helmet";

import env from "./config/env.js";

import healthRoutes from "./routes/health.routes.js";
import collegeRoutes from "./routes/college.routes.js";
import authRoutes from "./routes/auth.routes.js";
import resourceRoutes from "./routes/resource.routes.js";
import academicStructureRoutes from "./routes/academicStructure.routes.js";

import {
  notFoundHandler,
  errorHandler,
} from "./middleware/error.middleware.js";

const app = express();

app.disable("x-powered-by");

app.use(helmet());

app.use(
  cors({
    origin: env.FRONTEND_URL,
    methods: ["GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"],
    allowedHeaders: ["Content-Type", "Authorization"],
    credentials: true,
  })
);

app.use(express.json({ limit: "100kb" }));

app.use("/api/v1", healthRoutes);
app.use("/api/v1/colleges", collegeRoutes);
app.use("/api/v1/auth", authRoutes);
app.use("/api/v1/academic-structures", academicStructureRoutes);
app.use("/api/v1", resourceRoutes);

app.use(notFoundHandler);
app.use(errorHandler);

export default app;


========== Backend\src\config\database.js ==========
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import env from "./env.js";

export async function connectDatabase() {
  const mongoUri = env.MONGODB_URI.trim();

  if (!mongoUri) {
    throw new Error("MONGODB_URI is not configured.");
  }

  await mongoose.connect(mongoUri, {
    serverSelectionTimeoutMS: 5000
  });

  console.log("MongoDB connected successfully.");
}

export async function disconnectDatabase() {
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState !== 0) {
    await mongoose.disconnect();
  }
}

export function isDatabaseConnected() {
  return mongoose.connection.readyState === 1;
}

========== Backend\src\config\env.js ==========
import "dotenv/config";
import { z } from "zod";

const envSchema = z.object({
  NODE_ENV: z
    .enum(["development", "test", "production"])
    .default("development"),

  PORT: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(1)
    .max(65535)
    .default(5000),

  FRONTEND_URL: z.url().default("http://localhost:5173"),

  MONGODB_URI: z.string().default(""),

  JWT_SECRET: z.string().min(64).optional(),
  SESSION_SECRET: z.string().min(32).optional(),
  // Email configuration
  EMAIL_HOST: z.string().default("smtp.gmail.com"),

  EMAIL_PORT: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(1)
    .max(65535)
    .default(465),

  EMAIL_SECURE: z
    .enum(["true", "false"])
    .default("true")
    .transform((value) => value === "true"),

  EMAIL_USER: z.email().optional().or(z.literal("")),

  EMAIL_APP_PASSWORD: z.string().default(""),

  // Google OAuth
  GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: z.string().default(""),
  GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET: z.string().default(""),

  // GitHub OAuth
  GITHUB_CLIENT_ID: z.string().default(""),
  GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET: z.string().default(""),

  // OAuth callback URLs
  GOOGLE_CALLBACK_URL: z
    .url()
    .default("http://localhost:5000/api/v1/auth/google/callback"),

  GITHUB_CALLBACK_URL: z
    .url()
    .default("http://localhost:5000/api/v1/auth/github/callback"),
  
    // Backblaze B2 S3-compatible storage
  B2_ENDPOINT: z.url().optional(),
  B2_REGION: z.string().default("us-east-005"),
  B2_BUCKET_NAME: z.string().default(""),
  B2_KEY_ID: z.string().default(""),
  B2_APPLICATION_KEY: z.string().default(""),

  MAX_PDF_SIZE_MB: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(25).default(25),
  MAX_PDF_PARTS: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(10).default(10),
  SIGNED_URL_EXPIRY_SECONDS: z.coerce.number().int().min(60).max(900).default(300)
  
});

const parsedEnv = envSchema.safeParse(process.env);

if (!parsedEnv.success) {
  console.error("Invalid environment configuration:");
  console.error(parsedEnv.error.flatten().fieldErrors);
  process.exit(1);
}

const env = parsedEnv.data;

if (
  env.B2_ENDPOINT &&
  (!env.B2_BUCKET_NAME || !env.B2_KEY_ID || !env.B2_APPLICATION_KEY)
) {
  console.error("Backblaze B2 configuration is incomplete.");
  process.exit(1);
}

if (!env.MONGODB_URI.trim()) {
  console.warn(
    "MONGODB_URI is not configured. Database-dependent functionality will be unavailable."
  );
}

if (!env.JWT_SECRET) {
  console.warn(
    "JWT_SECRET is not configured. Token generation may be unavailable."
  );
}

if (!env.EMAIL_USER || !env.EMAIL_APP_PASSWORD) {
  console.warn(
    "Email credentials are not configured. Email delivery will be unavailable."
  );
}

if (!env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID || !env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET) {
  console.warn("Google OAuth is not configured.");
}

if (!env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID || !env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET) {
  console.warn("GitHub OAuth is not configured.");
}

export default env;

========== Backend\src\config\password.js ==========
import passport from "passport";
import { Strategy as GoogleStrategy } from "passport-google-oauth20";
import { Strategy as GitHubStrategy } from "passport-github2";

import env from "./env.js";

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| GOOGLE OAUTH STRATEGY
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

if (env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID && env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET) {
  passport.use(
    "google",
    new GoogleStrategy(
      {
        clientID: env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID,
        clientSecret: env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET,
        callbackURL: env.GOOGLE_CALLBACK_URL,
        scope: ["profile", "email"],
        state: true
      },
      (accessToken, refreshToken, profile, done) => {
        try {
          const googleData = profile._json ?? {};

          const email = profile.emails?.find(
            (entry) => entry.verified === true
          )?.value;

          const emailVerified = googleData.email_verified === true;

          if (!profile.id || !email || !emailVerified) {
            return done(null, false, {
              message:
                "A verified Google email address is required to sign in."
            });
          }

          return done(null, {
            provider: "google",
            providerUserId: profile.id,
            name: profile.displayName?.trim() || "",
            email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
            emailVerified: true
          });
        } catch (error) {
          return done(error);
        }
      }
    )
  );
} else {
  console.warn(
    "Google OAuth is disabled because its client credentials are not configured."
  );
}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| GITHUB OAUTH STRATEGY
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

if (env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID && env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET) {
  passport.use(
    "github",
    new GitHubStrategy(
      {
        clientID: env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID,
        clientSecret: env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET,
        callbackURL: env.GITHUB_CALLBACK_URL,
        scope: ["read:user", "user:email"],
        state: true
      },
      async (accessToken, refreshToken, profile, done) => {
        try {
          if (!profile.id) {
            return done(null, false, {
              message: "Unable to identify your GitHub account."
            });
          }

          /*
           * GitHub's profile email may be missing or unverified.
           * Query its authenticated email endpoint instead.
           */
          const response = await fetch(
            "https://api.github.com/user/emails",
            {
              method: "GET",
              headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
                Accept: "application/vnd.github+json",
                "X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28",
                "User-Agent": "OpenNotes"
              },
              signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000)
            }
          );

          if (!response.ok) {
            return done(
              new Error(
                "Unable to verify the email address associated with GitHub."
              )
            );
          }

          const emails = await response.json();

          if (!Array.isArray(emails)) {
            return done(
              new Error("GitHub returned an invalid email response.")
            );
          }

          const verifiedEmail = emails.find(
            (entry) =>
              entry.verified === true && entry.primary === true
          );

          if (!verifiedEmail?.email) {
            return done(null, false, {
              message:
                "A verified primary GitHub email address is required."
            });
          }

          return done(null, {
            provider: "github",
            providerUserId: String(profile.id),
            name:
              profile.displayName?.trim() ||
              profile.username?.trim() ||
              "",
            email: verifiedEmail.email.trim().toLowerCase(),
            emailVerified: true
          });
        } catch (error) {
          return done(error);
        }
      }
    )
  );
} else {
  console.warn(
    "GitHub OAuth is disabled because its client credentials are not configured."
  );
}

export default passport;

========== Backend\src\config\session.js ==========
import session from "express-session";
import MongoStore from "connect-mongo";

import env from "./env.js";

const SESSION_MAX_AGE_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export function createSessionMiddleware() {
  if (!env.MONGODB_URI.trim()) {
    throw new Error(
      "MONGODB_URI must be configured before session middleware can start."
    );
  }

  if (!env.SESSION_SECRET || env.SESSION_SECRET.length < 32) {
    throw new Error(
      "SESSION_SECRET must contain at least 32 characters."
    );
  }

  return session({
    name: "opennotes.sid",

    secret: env.SESSION_SECRET,

    resave: false,

    saveUninitialized: false,

    rolling: true,

    store: MongoStore.create({
      mongoUrl: env.MONGODB_URI,
      collectionName: "opennotes_sessions",
      ttl: SESSION_MAX_AGE_MS / 1000,
      autoRemove: "native"
    }),

    cookie: {
      httpOnly: true,
      secure: env.NODE_ENV === "production",
      sameSite: "lax",
      maxAge: SESSION_MAX_AGE_MS
    }
  });
}

========== Backend\src\config\storage.js ==========
import {
  S3Client,
  HeadBucketCommand
} from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";

import env from "./env.js";

const storageConfigured = Boolean(
  env.B2_ENDPOINT &&
  env.B2_BUCKET_NAME &&
  env.B2_KEY_ID &&
  env.B2_APPLICATION_KEY
);

export const storageClient = storageConfigured
  ? new S3Client({
      endpoint: env.B2_ENDPOINT,
      region: env.B2_REGION,
      credentials: {
        accessKeyId: env.B2_KEY_ID,
        secretAccessKey: env.B2_APPLICATION_KEY
      },
      forcePathStyle: true
    })
  : null;

export const STORAGE_BUCKET = env.B2_BUCKET_NAME;

export async function verifyStorageConnection() {
  if (!storageClient) {
    throw new Error("Backblaze B2 is not configured.");
  }

  await storageClient.send(
    new HeadBucketCommand({
      Bucket: STORAGE_BUCKET
    })
  );

  return true;
}

========== Backend\src\controllers\academicStructure.controller.js ==========

import mongoose from "mongoose";
import AcademicStructure from "../models/AcademicStructure.js";

export async function listAcademicStructuresController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { collegeId, stream, branch } = req.query;

    if (!collegeId || !mongoose.isValidObjectId(collegeId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "A valid collegeId is required.",
      });
    }

    const query = {
      collegeId,
      isActive: true,
    };

    if (stream) query.stream = stream;
    if (branch) query.branch = branch;

    const structures = await AcademicStructure.find(query)
      .select(
        "collegeId stream branch programName programCode academicYears semesters subjects"
      )
      .sort({ stream: 1, branch: 1, programName: 1 })
      .lean();

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      count: structures.length,
      data: structures,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
}


========== Backend\src\controllers\auth.controller.js ==========
import {
  registerSchema,
  loginSchema
} from "../validators/auth.validator.js";

import {
  registerUser,
  loginUser,
  verifyUserEmail,
  resendVerificationEmail,
  requestPasswordReset,
  resetPassword,
  revokeAllUserSessions
} from "../services/auth.service.js";

import User from "../models/User.js";


function sendValidationError(res, message, issues) {
  return res.status(400).json({
    success: false,
    message,
    errors: issues.map((issue) => ({
      field: issue.path.join("."),
      message: issue.message
    }))
  });
}


/* =========================================================
   REGISTER
========================================================= */

export async function registerController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const validation = registerSchema.safeParse(req.body);

    if (!validation.success) {
      return sendValidationError(
        res,
        "Invalid registration details.",
        validation.error.issues
      );
    }

    const user = await registerUser(validation.data);

    return res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message:
        "Registration successful. Email verification is required before account activation.",
      data: user
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   LOGIN
========================================================= */

export async function loginController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const validation = loginSchema.safeParse(req.body);

    if (!validation.success) {
      return sendValidationError(
        res,
        "Invalid login details.",
        validation.error.issues
      );
    }

    const result = await loginUser(validation.data);

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Login successful.",
      data: result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   CURRENT USER
========================================================= */

export async function getCurrentUserController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const user = await User.findOne({
      _id: req.auth.userId,
      isActive: true
    }).select(
      "name email role collegeId collegeAffiliationStatus emailVerified teacherApprovalStatus profile authProvider createdAt"
    );

    if (!user) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Your account is unavailable. Please log in again."
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: user
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   VERIFY EMAIL
========================================================= */

export async function verifyEmailController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { token } = req.body ?? {};

    if (
      typeof token !== "string" ||
      !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(token)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "A valid verification token is required."
      });
    }

    const result = await verifyUserEmail(token);

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Email verified successfully.",
      data: result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   RESEND VERIFICATION EMAIL
========================================================= */

export async function resendVerificationEmailController(
  req,
  res,
  next
) {
  try {
    const email = req.body?.email;

    if (
      typeof email !== "string" ||
      !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Please provide a valid email address."
      });
    }

    const result = await resendVerificationEmail({
      email
    });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      ...result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   FORGOT PASSWORD
========================================================= */

export async function requestPasswordResetController(
  req,
  res,
  next
) {
  try {
    const email = req.body?.email;

    if (
      typeof email !== "string" ||
      !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Please provide a valid email address."
      });
    }

    const result = await requestPasswordReset({
      email
    });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      ...result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   RESET PASSWORD
========================================================= */

export async function resetPasswordController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { token, newPassword } = req.body ?? {};

    if (
      typeof token !== "string" ||
      !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(token)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "A valid password reset token is required."
      });
    }

    if (
      typeof newPassword !== "string" ||
      newPassword.length < 12 ||
      newPassword.length > 128
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Password must contain between 12 and 128 characters."
      });
    }

    const result = await resetPassword({
      token,
      newPassword
    });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      ...result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   LOGOUT ALL DEVICES
========================================================= */

export async function logoutAllController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const result = await revokeAllUserSessions(
      req.auth.userId
    );

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      ...result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

========== Backend\src\controllers\college.controller.js ==========

import {
  searchColleges,
  getCollegeById
} from "../services/college.service.js";

import {
  collegeSearchSchema,
  collegeIdSchema
} from "../validators/college.validator.js";

export async function searchCollegesController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const validation = collegeSearchSchema.safeParse(req.query);

    if (!validation.success) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid college search parameters.",
        errors: validation.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
          field: issue.path.join("."),
          message: issue.message
        }))
      });
    }

    const { q, limit } = validation.data;
    const colleges = await searchColleges(q, limit);

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      count: colleges.length,
      data: colleges
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}

export async function getCollegeController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const validation = collegeIdSchema.safeParse(req.params.id);

    if (!validation.success) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid college ID."
      });
    }

    const college = await getCollegeById(validation.data);

    if (!college) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: "College not found."
      });
    }

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: college
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return next(error);
  }
}


========== Backend\src\controllers\health.controller.js ==========
import { isDatabaseConnected } from "../config/database.js";

export function getHealth(req, res) {
  return res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: "OpenNotes API is running.",
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
  });
}
//Readiness: confirms that the application can use its required database.
export function getReadiness(req, res) {
  if (!isDatabaseConnected()) {
    return res.status(503).json({
      success: false,
      message: "Service is not ready.",
      dependencies: {
        database: "disconnected"
      }
    });
  }

  return res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: "OpenNotes API is ready.",
    dependencies: {
      database: "connected"
    }
  });
}

========== Backend\src\controllers\myuploads.controller.js ==========

import { getMyUploads } from "../services/myuploads.service.js";

export async function myUploadsController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { type = "all", status, page = 1, limit = 10 } = req.query;

    if (!["all", "notes", "pyqs"].includes(type)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Type must be all, notes, or pyqs.",
      });
    }

    if (
      status &&
      !["pending", "approved", "rejected"].includes(status)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid upload status.",
      });
    }

    const parsedPage = Number(page);
    const parsedLimit = Number(limit);

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(parsedPage) ||
      parsedPage < 1 ||
      !Number.isInteger(parsedLimit) ||
      parsedLimit < 1 ||
      parsedLimit > 50
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid pagination values.",
      });
    }

    const result = await getMyUploads(req.auth.userId, {
      type,
      status,
      page: parsedPage,
      limit: parsedLimit,
    });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      ...result,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
}


========== Backend\src\controllers\resource.controller.js ==========
import {
  createResource,
  browseResources,
  getResource,
  getDownloadLink,
  getPendingResources,
  moderateResource
} from "../services/resource.service.js";

import {
  createNoteSchema,
  browseNoteSchema,
  resourceIdSchema
} from "../validators/note.validator.js";

import {
  createPYQSchema,
  browsePYQSchema
} from "../validators/pyq.validator.js";

function sendValidationError(res, validation, message) {
  return res.status(400).json({
    success: false,
    message,
    errors: validation.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
      field: issue.path.join("."),
      message: issue.message
    }))
  });
}

export async function createNoteController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const validation = createNoteSchema.safeParse(req.body);

    if (!validation.success) {
      return sendValidationError(
        res,
        validation,
        "Invalid note details."
      );
    }

    const note = await createResource({
      type: "notes",
      userId: req.auth.userId,
      data: validation.data,
      files: req.files
    });

    return res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Note uploaded successfully and is awaiting approval.",
      data: note
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
}

export async function downloadResourceController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { type, id, partId } = req.params;

    if (!["notes", "pyqs"].includes(type)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid resource type."
      });
    }

    const idValidation = resourceIdSchema.safeParse(id);
    const partValidation = resourceIdSchema.safeParse(partId);

    if (!idValidation.success || !partValidation.success) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid resource or PDF part ID."
      });
    }

    const result = await getDownloadLink({
      type,
      resourceId: id,
      partId
    });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
}

export async function pendingResourcesController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const type = req.query.type;

    if (!["notes", "pyqs"].includes(type)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Resource type must be notes or pyqs."
      });
    }

    const page = Math.max(1, Number(req.query.page) || 1);
    const limit = Math.min(
      50,
      Math.max(1, Number(req.query.limit) || 20)
    );

    const result = await getPendingResources(type, page, limit);

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      ...result
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
}

export async function approveResourceController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const idValidation = resourceIdSchema.safeParse(req.params.id);
    const { type } = req.params;

    if (!idValidation.success || !["notes", "pyqs"].includes(type)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid resource type or ID."
      });
    }

    const resource = await moderateResource({
      type,
      resourceId: req.params.id,
      adminId: req.auth.userId,
      action: "approve"
    });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Resource approved successfully.",
      data: resource
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
}

export async function rejectResourceController(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const idValidation = resourceIdSchema.safeParse(req.params.id);
    const { type } = req.params;
    const reason = req.body?.rejectionReason;

    if (!idValidation.success || !["notes", "pyqs"].includes(type)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid resource type or ID."
      });
    }

    if (
      typeof reason !== "string" ||
      !reason.trim() ||
      reason.trim().length > 1000
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "A rejection reason between 1 and 1000 characters is required."
      });
    }

    const resource = await moderateResource({
      type,
      resourceId: req.params.id,
      adminId: req.auth.userId,
      action: "reject",
      rejectionReason: reason
    });

    return res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: "Resource rejected successfully.",
      data: resource
    });
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
}

========== Backend\src\middleware\auth.middleware.js ==========
import User from "../models/User.js";
import { verifyAccessToken } from "../utils/token.js";


const JWT_ERROR_NAMES = new Set([
  "JWTExpired",
  "JWTClaimValidationFailed",
  "JWSSignatureVerificationFailed",
  "JWSInvalid",
  "JWTInvalid",
  "JWTMalformed",
  "JWKSNoMatchingKey"
]);

const JWT_ERROR_CODES = new Set([
  "ERR_JWT_EXPIRED",
  "ERR_JWS_INVALID",
  "ERR_JWT_INVALID",
  "ERR_JWT_CLAIM_VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "ERR_JWS_SIGNATURE_VERIFICATION_FAILED"
]);


/* =========================================================
   AUTHENTICATE USER
========================================================= */

export async function authenticateUser(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const authorization = req.headers.authorization;

    if (
      typeof authorization !== "string" ||
      !authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")
    ) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required."
      });
    }

    const token = authorization
      .slice("Bearer ".length)
      .trim();

    if (!token) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required."
      });
    }

    // Verify JWT signature, expiry, issuer and audience.
    const payload = await verifyAccessToken(token);

    if (
      typeof payload.sub !== "string" ||
      !/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i.test(payload.sub)
    ) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Invalid authentication token."
      });
    }

    // Fetch live user data from MongoDB.
    // sessionVersion is select:false in the User schema,
    // so explicitly select it here.
    const user = await User.findById(payload.sub)
      .select("_id role isActive +sessionVersion");

    if (!user || !user.isActive) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Your account is unavailable. Please log in again."
      });
    }

    const tokenSessionVersion =
      typeof payload.sv === "number" &&
      Number.isInteger(payload.sv) &&
      payload.sv >= 0
        ? payload.sv
        : 0;

    const currentSessionVersion =
      Number.isInteger(user.sessionVersion) &&
      user.sessionVersion >= 0
        ? user.sessionVersion
        : 0;

    // Exact comparison prevents tokens with unexpected
    // session versions from being accepted.
    if (tokenSessionVersion !== currentSessionVersion) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Your session has expired. Please log in again."
      });
    }

    // Always use the current role from MongoDB.
    req.auth = {
      userId: user._id.toString(),
      role: user.role
    };

    return next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      JWT_ERROR_NAMES.has(error?.name) ||
      JWT_ERROR_CODES.has(error?.code) ||
      error?.name === "CastError"
    ) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "Invalid or expired authentication token."
      });
    }

    return next(error);
  }
}


/* =========================================================
   ROLE AUTHORIZATION
========================================================= */

export function requireRole(...allowedRoles) {
  return function roleAuthorization(req, res, next) {
    if (!req.auth) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: "Authentication required."
      });
    }

    if (!allowedRoles.includes(req.auth.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          "You do not have permission to access this resource."
      });
    }

    return next();
  };
}

========== Backend\src\middleware\error.middleware.js ==========
export function notFoundHandler(req, res) {
  return res.status(404).json({
    success: false,
    message: "API endpoint not found."
  });
}

export function errorHandler(err, req, res, next) {
  if (res.headersSent) {
    return next(err);
  }

  const statusCode =
    Number.isInteger(err.statusCode) &&
    err.statusCode >= 400 &&
    err.statusCode <= 599
      ? err.statusCode
      : 500;

  if (statusCode >= 500) {
    console.error("Internal server error:", err.message);
  }

  return res.status(statusCode).json({
    success: false,
    message:
      statusCode >= 500
        ? "An unexpected server error occurred."
        : err.message
  });
}

========== Backend\src\middleware\upload.middleware.js ==========
import multer from "multer";
import { fileTypeFromBuffer } from "file-type";
import env from "../config/env.js";

const MAX_FILE_SIZE = env.MAX_PDF_SIZE_MB * 1024 * 1024;

const upload = multer({
  storage: multer.memoryStorage(),

  limits: {
    fileSize: MAX_FILE_SIZE,
    files: env.MAX_PDF_PARTS,
    fields: 20,
    parts: env.MAX_PDF_PARTS + 20
  },

  fileFilter(req, file, callback) {
    if (file.mimetype !== "application/pdf") {
      const error = new Error("Only PDF files are accepted.");
      error.statusCode = 400;
      return callback(error);
    }

    callback(null, true);
  }
});

export const uploadPdfFiles = upload.array(
  "files",
  env.MAX_PDF_PARTS
);

export async function validatePdfFiles(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const files = req.files;

    if (!Array.isArray(files) || files.length === 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: "At least one PDF file is required."
      });
    }

    if (files.length > env.MAX_PDF_PARTS) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Maximum ${env.MAX_PDF_PARTS} PDF parts are allowed.`
      });
    }

    for (const file of files) {
      const detected = await fileTypeFromBuffer(file.buffer);

      if (
        !detected ||
        detected.ext !== "pdf" ||
        detected.mime !== "application/pdf"
      ) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          success: false,
          message: `${file.originalname} is not a valid PDF file.`
        });
      }
    }

    next();
  } catch (error) {
    next(error);
  }
}

export function handleUploadError(error, req, res, next) {
  if (error instanceof multer.MulterError) {
    const message =
      error.code === "LIMIT_FILE_SIZE"
        ? `Each PDF must be smaller than ${env.MAX_PDF_SIZE_MB} MB.`
        : error.code === "LIMIT_FILE_COUNT"
          ? `Maximum ${env.MAX_PDF_PARTS} PDF parts are allowed.`
          : "Invalid file upload.";

    return res.status(400).json({
      success: false,
      message
    });
  }

  if (error.statusCode === 400) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      success: false,
      message: error.message
    });
  }

  next(error);
}

========== Backend\src\models\AcademicStructure.js ==========
//	Store streams, branches, academic years, semesters, and subjects.
import mongoose from "mongoose";

const academicStructureSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    collegeId: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "College",
      required: [true, "College reference is required."],
      index: true
    },

    stream: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, "Stream is required."],
      trim: true,
      maxlength: [100, "Stream cannot exceed 100 characters."]
    },

    branch: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, "Branch is required."],
      trim: true,
      maxlength: [150, "Branch cannot exceed 150 characters."]
    },

    programName: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, "Program name is required."],
      trim: true,
      maxlength: [150, "Program name cannot exceed 150 characters."]
    },

    programCode: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, "Program code is required."],
      trim: true,
      uppercase: true,
      maxlength: [30, "Program code cannot exceed 30 characters."]
    },

    academicYears: {
      type: [
        {
          type: String,
          trim: true,
          enum: ["1st Year", "2nd Year", "3rd Year", "4th Year", "5th Year"]
        }
      ],
      required: true,
      validate: {
        validator(years) {
          return years.length > 0 &&
            years.length === new Set(years).size;
        },
        message: "Academic years must be non-empty and unique."
      }
    },

    semesters: {
      type: [
        {
          type: Number,
          min: 1,
          max: 12
        }
      ],
      required: true,
      validate: {
        validator(semesters) {
          return semesters.length > 0 &&
            semesters.length === new Set(semesters).size;
        },
        message: "Semesters must be non-empty and unique."
      }
    },

    subjects: {
      type: [
        {
          subjectName: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
            maxlength: 150
          },

          subjectCode: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
            uppercase: true,
            maxlength: 30
          },

          semester: {
            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 1,
            max: 12
          }
        }
      ],
      default: []
    },

    isActive: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: true
    }
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
    versionKey: false
  }
);

// Prevent duplicate programs for the same college and branch.
academicStructureSchema.index(
  { collegeId: 1, programCode: 1 },
  { unique: true }
);

// Optimize academic browsing and filtering.
academicStructureSchema.index({
  collegeId: 1,
  stream: 1,
  branch: 1,
  isActive: 1
});

academicStructureSchema.index({
  collegeId: 1,
  "subjects.subjectCode": 1
});

const AcademicStructure = mongoose.model(
  "AcademicStructure",
  academicStructureSchema
);

export default AcademicStructure;

========== Backend\src\models\College.js ==========
import mongoose from "mongoose";

const collegeSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, "College name is required."],
      trim: true,
      minlength: [2, "College name must contain at least 2 characters."],
      maxlength: [200, "College name cannot exceed 200 characters."]
    },

    nameKey: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true
    },

    domains: {
      type: [
        {
          type: String,
          lowercase: true,
          trim: true
        }
      ],
      default: [],
      validate: {
        validator(domains) {
          return (
            domains.length ===
            new Set(domains.map((domain) => domain.toLowerCase())).size
          );
        },
        message: "Duplicate email domains are not allowed."
      }
    },

    verificationStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["pending", "verified", "rejected"],
      default: "pending",
      required: true
    },

    isActive: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: true,
      required: true
    },

    address: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: [500, "Address cannot exceed 500 characters."],
      default: ""
    },

    website: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: ""
    },

    createdBy: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      default: null
    }
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
    versionKey: false
  }
);

// Efficient college name searches.
collegeSchema.index({ name: 1 });

// Efficient lookup of colleges by institutional email domain.
collegeSchema.index({ domains: 1 });

// Keep the normalized name synchronized with the display name.
collegeSchema.pre("validate", function () {
  if (this.name) {
    this.nameKey = this.name.trim().toLowerCase();
  }
});

const College = mongoose.model("College", collegeSchema);

export default College;

========== Backend\src\models\Note.js ==========
//	Store note metadata, PDF parts, uploader, and approval status.
import mongoose from "mongoose";

const pdfPartSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    partNumber: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1
    },

    originalFileName: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 255
    },

    storageKey: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true
    },

    contentType: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["application/pdf"],
      default: "application/pdf",
      required: true
    },

    sizeBytes: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1
    },

    checksum: {
      type: String,
      default: ""
    }
  },
  {
    _id: true,
    versionKey: false
  }
);

const noteSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    collegeId: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "College",
      required: true,
      index: true
    },

    academicStructureId: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "AcademicStructure",
      required: true,
      index: true
    },

    stream: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 100
    },

    branch: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 150
    },

    academicYear: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: ["1st Year", "2nd Year", "3rd Year", "4th Year", "5th Year"]
    },

    semester: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1,
      max: 12
    },

    subjectName: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 150
    },

    subjectCode: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      uppercase: true,
      maxlength: 30
    },

    moduleNumber: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1,
      max: 50
    },

    title: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      minlength: 3,
      maxlength: 200
    },

    description: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 2000,
      default: ""
    },

    hasMultipleParts: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
      required: true
    },

    parts: {
      type: [pdfPartSchema],
      required: true,
      validate: {
        validator(parts) {
          if (parts.length === 0) return false;

          const numbers = parts.map((part) => part.partNumber);

          return (
            new Set(numbers).size === numbers.length &&
            numbers.every((number) => Number.isInteger(number) && number > 0)
          );
        },
        message: "At least one PDF part with unique positive part numbers is required."
      }
    },

    uploadedBy: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      required: true,
      index: true
    },

    approvalStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["pending", "approved", "rejected"],
      default: "pending",
      required: true,
      index: true
    },

    reviewedBy: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      default: null
    },

    reviewedAt: {
      type: Date,
      default: null
    },

    rejectionReason: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 1000,
      default: ""
    },

    isDeleted: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
      required: true
    }
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
    versionKey: false
  }
);

// Common filters for Browse Notes.
noteSchema.index({
  collegeId: 1,
  branch: 1,
  academicYear: 1,
  semester: 1,
  subjectCode: 1,
  approvalStatus: 1,
  createdAt: -1
});

// Efficient uploader dashboard queries.
noteSchema.index({
  uploadedBy: 1,
  createdAt: -1
});

const Note = mongoose.model("Note", noteSchema);

export default Note;

========== Backend\src\models\OAuthIdentity.js ==========
import mongoose from "mongoose";

const oauthIdentitySchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    userId: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      required: true,
      immutable: true
    },

    provider: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["google", "github"],
      required: true,
      immutable: true
    },

    // Stable, unique identifier supplied by the OAuth provider.
    // Do not use an email address as the provider identity.
    providerUserId: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      immutable: true
    },

    providerEmail: {
      type: String,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 254,
      default: null
    },

    providerEmailVerified: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
      required: true
    }
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
    versionKey: false
  }
);

// One provider identity can be connected to only one OpenNotes account.
oauthIdentitySchema.index(
  { provider: 1, providerUserId: 1 },
  {
    unique: true,
    name: "oauth_provider_identity_unique"
  }
);

// Quickly retrieve all linked providers for an account.
oauthIdentitySchema.index(
  { userId: 1, provider: 1 },
  {
    name: "oauth_user_provider"
  }
);

const OAuthIdentity = mongoose.model(
  "OAuthIdentity",
  oauthIdentitySchema
);

export default OAuthIdentity;

========== Backend\src\models\PYQ.js ==========
//Store question-paper metadata, CIE/SEE type, examination year, PDF parts, uploader, and approval status.
import mongoose from "mongoose";

const pdfPartSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    partNumber: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1
    },

    originalFileName: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 255
    },

    storageKey: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true
    },

    contentType: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["application/pdf"],
      default: "application/pdf",
      required: true
    },

    sizeBytes: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1
    },

    checksum: {
      type: String,
      default: ""
    }
  },
  {
    _id: true,
    versionKey: false
  }
);

const pyqSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    collegeId: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "College",
      required: true,
      index: true
    },

    academicStructureId: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "AcademicStructure",
      required: true,
      index: true
    },

    stream: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 100
    },

    branch: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 150
    },

    academicYear: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: ["1st Year", "2nd Year", "3rd Year", "4th Year", "5th Year"]
    },

    semester: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1,
      max: 12
    },

    subjectName: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 150
    },

    subjectCode: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      uppercase: true,
      maxlength: 30
    },

    examType: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: ["CIE", "SEE"]
    },

    examinationYear: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1950,
      max: 2200
    },

    title: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      minlength: 3,
      maxlength: 200
    },

    description: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 2000,
      default: ""
    },

    hasMultipleParts: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
      required: true
    },

    parts: {
      type: [pdfPartSchema],
      required: true,
      validate: {
        validator(parts) {
          if (parts.length === 0) return false;

          const numbers = parts.map((part) => part.partNumber);

          return (
            new Set(numbers).size === numbers.length &&
            numbers.every(
              (number) => Number.isInteger(number) && number > 0
            )
          );
        },
        message:
          "At least one PDF part with unique positive part numbers is required."
      }
    },

    uploadedBy: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      required: true,
      index: true
    },

    approvalStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["pending", "approved", "rejected"],
      default: "pending",
      required: true,
      index: true
    },

    reviewedBy: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "User",
      default: null
    },

    reviewedAt: {
      type: Date,
      default: null
    },

    rejectionReason: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 1000,
      default: ""
    },

    isDeleted: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
      required: true
    }
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
    versionKey: false
  }
);

// Optimize Browse PYQs filters.
pyqSchema.index({
  collegeId: 1,
  branch: 1,
  academicYear: 1,
  semester: 1,
  subjectCode: 1,
  examType: 1,
  examinationYear: -1,
  approvalStatus: 1
});

// Optimize My Uploads queries.
pyqSchema.index({
  uploadedBy: 1,
  createdAt: -1
});

const PYQ = mongoose.model("PYQ", pyqSchema);

export default PYQ;

========== Backend\src\models\User.js ==========
import mongoose from "mongoose";

const userSchema = new mongoose.Schema(
  {
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, "Full name is required."],
      trim: true,
      minlength: [2, "Name must contain at least 2 characters."],
      maxlength: [100, "Name cannot exceed 100 characters."]
    },

    email: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, "Email is required."],
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: [254, "Email cannot exceed 254 characters."],
      match: [
        /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/,
        "Please provide a valid email address."
      ]
    },

    // Never returned by default. Always select explicitly with "+passwordHash".
    passwordHash: {
      type: String,
      default: undefined,
      select: false
    },

    role: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["student", "teacher", "admin"],
      required: true,
      default: "student"
    },

    collegeId: {
      type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: "College",
      default: null,
      index: true
    },

    collegeAffiliationStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: [
        "not_provided",
        "pending",
        "verified",
        "rejected"
      ],
      default: "not_provided",
      required: true
    },

    emailVerified: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
      required: true
    },

    emailVerificationTokenHash: {
      type: String,
      default: null,
      select: false
    },

    emailVerificationExpiresAt: {
      type: Date,
      default: null,
      select: false
    },

    passwordResetTokenHash: {
      type: String,
      default: null,
      select: false
    },

    passwordResetExpiresAt: {
      type: Date,
      default: null,
      select: false
    },

    teacherApprovalStatus: {
      type: String,
      enum: [
        "not_applicable",
        "pending",
        "approved",
        "rejected"
      ],
      default: "not_applicable",
      required: true
    },

    // Reason stored when an admin rejects a teacher application.
    teacherApprovalRejectionReason: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: [500, "Rejection reason cannot exceed 500 characters."],
      default: ""
    },

    isActive: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: true,
      required: true
    },

    /*
     * Increment this counter to invalidate all previously issued JWTs.
     * Any JWT that carries a sessionVersion lower than the current value
     * is treated as revoked. This enables:
     *   - Logout from all devices
     *   - Forced sign-out after a password change or security event
     */
    sessionVersion: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
      min: 0,
      required: true,
      select: false
    },

    profile: {
      phone: {
        type: String,
        trim: true,
        default: ""
      },

      academicYear: {
        type: String,
        enum: [
          "",
          "1st Year",
          "2nd Year",
          "3rd Year",
          "4th Year",
          "5th Year"
        ],
        default: ""
      },

      about: {
        type: String,
        trim: true,
        maxlength: [
          1000,
          "About section cannot exceed 1000 characters."
        ],
        default: ""
      }
    },

    /*
     * Retained for backward-compatibility with existing code.
     * This is NOT the authoritative list of linked login providers.
     * Use OAuthIdentity documents for multi-provider identity management.
     */
    authProvider: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["local", "google", "github"],
      default: "local",
      required: true
    },

    lastLoginAt: {
      type: Date,
      default: null
    }
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
    versionKey: false
  }
);

// Each normalized email must belong to only one account.
userSchema.index(
  { email: 1 },
  { unique: true, name: "user_email_unique" }
);

userSchema.index(
  { role: 1, isActive: 1 },
  { name: "user_role_active" }
);

userSchema.index(
  { role: 1, teacherApprovalStatus: 1 },
  { name: "teacher_approval_queue" }
);

const User = mongoose.model("User", userSchema);

export default User;
