========== Backend\src\server.js ==========
import app from "./app.js";
import env from "./config/env.js";
import {
  connectDatabase,
  disconnectDatabase
} from "./config/database.js";

let server;

async function startServer() {
  try {
    await connectDatabase();

    server = app.listen(env.PORT, () => {
      console.log(`OpenNotes API listening on port ${env.PORT}`);
      console.log(`Environment: ${env.NODE_ENV}`);
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to start OpenNotes API:", error.message);
    process.exitCode = 1;
    await disconnectDatabase();
  }
}

async function shutdown(signal) {
  console.log(`${signal} received. Shutting down gracefully.`);

  if (server) {
    server.close(async (error) => {
      if (error) {
        console.error("Error closing HTTP server:", error.message);
        process.exitCode = 1;
      }

      try {
        await disconnectDatabase();
      } catch (disconnectError) {
        console.error(
          "Error closing MongoDB connection:",
          disconnectError.message
        );
        process.exitCode = 1;
      }

      process.exit();
    });
  } else {
    await disconnectDatabase();
    process.exit();
  }
}

process.on("SIGTERM", () => shutdown("SIGTERM"));
process.on("SIGINT", () => shutdown("SIGINT"));

startServer();

========== Backend\src\services\api.js ==========

const API_BASE_URL = (
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || "http://localhost:5000/api/v1"
).replace(/\/$/, "");

const TOKEN_KEY = "openNotes-access-token";

// JWT token management
export function getAccessToken() {
  return sessionStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
}

export function setAccessToken(token) {
  if (token) {
    sessionStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);
  } else {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  }
}

// Centralized API request handler
export async function apiRequest(path, options = {}) {
  const { headers = {}, body, ...rest } = options;

  const token = getAccessToken();
  const isFormData =
    typeof FormData !== "undefined" && body instanceof FormData;

  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_BASE_URL}${path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`}`,
    {
      ...rest,
      body,
      headers: {
        ...(body && !isFormData
          ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
          : {}),
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
        ...headers,
      },
    }
  );

  let payload;

  try {
    payload = await response.json();
  } catch {
    payload = null;
  }

  if (!response.ok || payload?.success === false) {
    const error = new Error(
      payload?.message || `Request failed (${response.status}).`
    );

    error.status = response.status;
    error.payload = payload;

    throw error;
  }

  return payload;
}

// Authentication APIs
export const authApi = {
  register: (data) =>
    apiRequest("/auth/register", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }),

  login: (data) =>
    apiRequest("/auth/login", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }),

  me: () => apiRequest("/auth/me"),

  logoutAll: () =>
    apiRequest("/auth/logout-all", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    }),
};

// Notes and PYQ APIs
export const resourceApi = {
  notes: (params = {}) =>
    apiRequest(`/notes${toQuery(params)}`),

  pyqs: (params = {}) =>
    apiRequest(`/pyqs${toQuery(params)}`),

  uploadNotes: (formData) =>
    apiRequest("/notes", {
      method: "POST",
      body: formData,
    }),

  uploadPYQ: (formData) =>
    apiRequest("/pyqs", {
      method: "POST",
      body: formData,
    }),

  download: (type, id, partId) =>
    apiRequest(`/${type}/${id}/download/${partId}`),

  myUploads: (params = {}) =>
    apiRequest(`/resources/mine${toQuery(params)}`),
};

// College APIs
export const collegeApi = {
  search: (q, limit = 10) =>
    apiRequest(`/colleges/search${toQuery({ q, limit })}`),

  get: (id) =>
    apiRequest(`/colleges/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
};

// Query string helper
function toQuery(params) {
  const query = new URLSearchParams();

  Object.entries(params).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (
      value !== undefined &&
      value !== null &&
      value !== ""
    ) {
      query.set(key, String(value));
    }
  });

  const result = query.toString();

  return result ? `?${result}` : "";
}


========== Backend\src\services\auth.service.js ==========
import User from "../models/User.js";
import College from "../models/College.js";

import {
  hashPassword,
  verifyPassword
} from "../utils/password.js";

import { generateAccessToken } from "../utils/token.js";

import {
  generateEmailVerificationToken,
  hashEmailVerificationToken
} from "../utils/emailverification.js";

import {
  generatePasswordResetToken,
  hashPasswordResetToken
} from "../utils/passwordReset.js";

import {
  sendVerificationEmail,
  sendPasswordResetEmail
} from "./email.service.js";


function createError(message, statusCode) {
  const error = new Error(message);
  error.statusCode = statusCode;
  return error;
}


/* =========================================================
   REGISTER USER
========================================================= */

export async function registerUser({
  name,
  email,
  password,
  collegeId
}) {
  const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

  const existingUser = await User.exists({
    email: normalizedEmail
  });

  if (existingUser) {
    throw createError(
      "An account with this email already exists.",
      409
    );
  }

  let selectedCollege = null;

  if (collegeId) {
    selectedCollege = await College.findOne({
      _id: collegeId,
      isActive: true,
      verificationStatus: "verified"
    })
      .select("_id")
      .lean();

    if (!selectedCollege) {
      throw createError(
        "The selected college does not exist or is not verified.",
        400
      );
    }
  }

  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);

  const {
    token,
    tokenHash,
    expiresAt
  } = generateEmailVerificationToken();

  let user;

  try {
    user = await User.create({
      name,
      email: normalizedEmail,
      passwordHash,

      // Public registration can only create students.
      role: "student",

      collegeId: selectedCollege?._id ?? null,

      collegeAffiliationStatus: selectedCollege
        ? "pending"
        : "not_provided",

      emailVerified: false,

      emailVerificationTokenHash: tokenHash,
      emailVerificationExpiresAt: expiresAt,

      teacherApprovalStatus: "not_applicable",

      authProvider: "local",
      sessionVersion: 0
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      error.code === 11000 &&
      error.keyPattern?.email
    ) {
      throw createError(
        "An account with this email already exists.",
        409
      );
    }

    throw error;
  }

  try {
    await sendVerificationEmail({
      email: user.email,
      token
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Verification email delivery failed:",
      error.message
    );

    // The account remains unverified.
    // The raw verification token is never returned.
    throw createError(
      "Your account was created, but the verification email could not be sent. Please try again later.",
      503
    );
  }

  return {
    id: user._id,
    name: user.name,
    email: user.email,
    role: user.role,
    collegeId: user.collegeId,
    collegeAffiliationStatus:
      user.collegeAffiliationStatus,
    emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
    message:
      "Registration successful. Please check your email to verify your account."
  };
}


/* =========================================================
   LOGIN USER
========================================================= */

export async function loginUser({ email, password }) {
  const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

  const user = await User.findOne({
    email: normalizedEmail
  }).select("+passwordHash +sessionVersion");

  // Use the same error for unknown email and wrong password.
  if (
    !user ||
    !user.passwordHash ||
    !(await verifyPassword(password, user.passwordHash))
  ) {
    throw createError(
      "Invalid email or password.",
      401
    );
  }

  if (!user.isActive) {
    throw createError(
      "This account is inactive.",
      403
    );
  }

  if (!user.emailVerified) {
    const error = createError(
      "Please verify your email address before logging in.",
      403
    );

    error.code = "EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED";

    throw error;
  }

  // Important: sessionVersion is explicitly selected above.
  // The generated JWT must contain the actual database value.
  const accessToken = await generateAccessToken(user);

  user.lastLoginAt = new Date();

  await user.save();

  return {
    accessToken,
    tokenType: "Bearer",
    expiresIn: 900,

    user: {
      id: user._id,
      name: user.name,
      email: user.email,
      role: user.role,
      collegeId: user.collegeId,
      emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
      collegeAffiliationStatus:
        user.collegeAffiliationStatus,
      teacherApprovalStatus:
        user.teacherApprovalStatus
    }
  };
}


/* =========================================================
   VERIFY EMAIL
========================================================= */

export async function verifyUserEmail(token) {
  let tokenHash;

  try {
    tokenHash = hashEmailVerificationToken(token);
  } catch {
    throw createError(
      "Invalid or expired verification link.",
      400
    );
  }

  const user = await User.findOne({
    emailVerificationTokenHash: tokenHash,
    emailVerificationExpiresAt: {
      $gt: new Date()
    },
    emailVerified: false,
    isActive: true
  }).select(
    "+emailVerificationTokenHash +emailVerificationExpiresAt"
  );

  if (!user) {
    throw createError(
      "Invalid or expired verification link.",
      400
    );
  }

  user.emailVerified = true;

  user.emailVerificationTokenHash = null;
  user.emailVerificationExpiresAt = null;

  await user.save();

  return {
    email: user.email,
    emailVerified: user.emailVerified
  };
}


/* =========================================================
   RESEND VERIFICATION EMAIL
========================================================= */

export async function resendVerificationEmail({ email }) {
  const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

  const genericResponse = {
    message:
      "If an eligible account exists, a verification email will be sent."
  };

  const user = await User.findOne({
    email: normalizedEmail
  });

  if (!user || !user.isActive || user.emailVerified) {
    return genericResponse;
  }

  const {
    token,
    tokenHash,
    expiresAt
  } = generateEmailVerificationToken();

  user.emailVerificationTokenHash = tokenHash;
  user.emailVerificationExpiresAt = expiresAt;

  await user.save();

  try {
    await sendVerificationEmail({
      email: user.email,
      token
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Resend verification email delivery failed:",
      error.message
    );

    throw createError(
      "Unable to send the verification email right now. Please try again later.",
      503
    );
  }

  return genericResponse;
}


/* =========================================================
   REQUEST PASSWORD RESET
========================================================= */

export async function requestPasswordReset({ email }) {
  const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

  const genericResponse = {
    message:
      "If an eligible account exists, password reset instructions will be sent."
  };

  const user = await User.findOne({
    email: normalizedEmail,
    isActive: true,
    authProvider: "local"
  });

  // Do not reveal whether an account exists.
  if (!user) {
    return genericResponse;
  }

  const {
    token,
    tokenHash,
    expiresAt
  } = generatePasswordResetToken();

  user.passwordResetTokenHash = tokenHash;
  user.passwordResetExpiresAt = expiresAt;

  await user.save();

  try {
    await sendPasswordResetEmail({
      email: user.email,
      token
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Password reset email delivery failed:",
      error.message
    );

    user.passwordResetTokenHash = null;
    user.passwordResetExpiresAt = null;

    await user.save();

    throw createError(
      "Unable to send password reset instructions right now. Please try again later.",
      503
    );
  }

  return genericResponse;
}


/* =========================================================
   RESET PASSWORD
========================================================= */

export async function resetPassword({
  token,
  newPassword
}) {
  let tokenHash;

  try {
    tokenHash = hashPasswordResetToken(token);
  } catch {
    throw createError(
      "The password reset link is invalid or has expired.",
      400
    );
  }

  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(newPassword);

  /*
   * Atomic operation:
   *
   * 1. Match the hashed reset token.
   * 2. Check that it has not expired.
   * 3. Check that the account is active and local.
   * 4. Change the password.
   * 5. Remove the reset token.
   * 6. Increment sessionVersion.
   *
   * Only one concurrent request can successfully consume
   * this reset token.
   */

  const updatedUser = await User.findOneAndUpdate(
    {
      passwordResetTokenHash: tokenHash,

      passwordResetExpiresAt: {
        $gt: new Date()
      },

      isActive: true,
      authProvider: "local"
    },
    {
      $set: {
        passwordHash,
        passwordResetTokenHash: null,
        passwordResetExpiresAt: null
      },

      $inc: {
        sessionVersion: 1
      }
    },
    {
      new: true,
      runValidators: true
    }
  ).select("_id");

  if (!updatedUser) {
    throw createError(
      "The password reset link is invalid or has expired.",
      400
    );
  }

  return {
    message:
      "Password reset successfully. All existing sessions have been invalidated. You can now log in."
  };
}


/* =========================================================
   LOGOUT ALL DEVICES
========================================================= */

export async function revokeAllUserSessions(userId) {
  const user = await User.findOneAndUpdate(
    {
      _id: userId,
      isActive: true
    },
    {
      $inc: {
        sessionVersion: 1
      }
    },
    {
      new: true
    }
  ).select("_id");

  if (!user) {
    throw createError(
      "Your account is unavailable. Please log in again.",
      401
    );
  }

  return {
    message:
      "You have been logged out from all devices. Please log in again to continue."
  };
}

========== Backend\src\services\college.service.js ==========
import College from "../models/College.js";

export async function searchColleges(query, limit = 10) {
  const searchTerm = query.trim();

  if (!searchTerm) {
    return [];
  }

  return College.find({
    isActive: true,
    verificationStatus: "verified",
    name: {
      $regex: escapeRegex(searchTerm),
      $options: "i"
    }
  })
    .select("name address website")
    .sort({ name: 1 })
    .limit(limit)
    .lean();
}

export async function getCollegeById(collegeId) {
  return College.findOne({
    _id: collegeId,
    isActive: true,
    verificationStatus: "verified"
  })
    .select("name address website")
    .lean();
}

function escapeRegex(value) {
  return value.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
}


========== Backend\src\services\email.service.js ==========
import nodemailer from "nodemailer";
import env from "../config/env.js";

let transporter;

function getTransporter() {
  if (!env.EMAIL_USER || !env.EMAIL_APP_PASSWORD) {
    throw new Error("Email service is not configured.");
  }

  if (!transporter) {
    transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
      host: env.EMAIL_HOST,
      port: env.EMAIL_PORT,
      secure: env.EMAIL_SECURE,
      auth: {
        user: env.EMAIL_USER,
        pass: env.EMAIL_APP_PASSWORD
      },
      connectionTimeout: 10000,
      greetingTimeout: 10000,
      socketTimeout: 15000
    });
  }

  return transporter;
}

export async function verifyEmailConnection() {
  await getTransporter().verify();
  return true;
}

export async function sendVerificationEmail({ email, token }) {
  if (!email || !token) {
    throw new Error("Recipient email and verification token are required.");
  }

  const verificationUrl = new URL(
    "/verify-email",
    env.FRONTEND_URL
  );

  verificationUrl.searchParams.set("token", token);

  const mailer = getTransporter();

  return mailer.sendMail({
    from: {
      name: "OpenNotes",
      address: env.EMAIL_USER
    },
    to: email,
    subject: "Verify your OpenNotes email address",
    text: [
      "Welcome to OpenNotes!",
      "",
      "Please verify your email address using the link below:",
      verificationUrl.toString(),
      "",
      "This link expires in 30 minutes.",
      "If you did not create this account, you can ignore this email."
    ].join("\n"),
    html: `
      <div style="font-family: Arial, sans-serif; max-width: 560px; margin: auto; line-height: 1.6;">
        <h2>Welcome to OpenNotes!</h2>
        <p>Please verify your email address to continue setting up your account.</p>
        <p>
          <a href="${verificationUrl.toString()}"
             style="display: inline-block; padding: 12px 20px; background: #176b4d; color: white; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px;">
            Verify Email
          </a>
        </p>
        <p>This link expires in 30 minutes.</p>
        <p>If you did not create this account, you can ignore this email.</p>
      </div>
    `
  });
}

export async function sendPasswordResetEmail({ email, token }) {
  const transporter = getTransporter();

  const resetUrl = new URL("/reset-password", env.FRONTEND_URL);
  resetUrl.searchParams.set("token", token);

  const expiresInMinutes = 30;

  await transporter.sendMail({
    from: env.EMAIL_USER,
    to: email,
    subject: "Reset your OpenNotes password",
    text: [
      "Hello,",
      "",
      "We received a request to reset your OpenNotes password.",
      "",
      `Use this link to reset your password: ${resetUrl.toString()}`,
      "",
      `This link expires in ${expiresInMinutes} minutes.`,
      "",
      "If you did not request a password reset, you can ignore this email.",
      "",
      "OpenNotes Team"
    ].join("\n"),
    html: `
      <div style="font-family: Arial, sans-serif; line-height: 1.6;">
        <h2>Reset your OpenNotes password</h2>
        <p>We received a request to reset your OpenNotes password.</p>
        <p>
          Click the button below to choose a new password.
        </p>
        <p>
          <a
            href="${resetUrl.toString()}"
            style="
              display: inline-block;
              padding: 12px 20px;
              background-color: #166534;
              color: #ffffff;
              text-decoration: none;
              border-radius: 6px;
            "
          >
            Reset Password
          </a>
        </p>
        <p>This link expires in ${expiresInMinutes} minutes.</p>
        <p>If you did not request a password reset, you can ignore this email.</p>
        <p>— OpenNotes Team</p>
      </div>
    `
  });
}


========== Backend\src\services\myuploads.service.js ==========

import Note from "../models/Note.js";
import PYQ from "../models/PYQ.js";

const MODELS = {
  notes: Note,
  pyqs: PYQ,
};

export async function getMyUploads(userId, filters = {}) {
  const { type = "all", status, page = 1, limit = 10 } = filters;

  const types = type === "all" ? ["notes", "pyqs"] : [type];

  if (!types.every((item) => MODELS[item])) {
    const error = new Error("Invalid resource type.");
    error.statusCode = 400;
    throw error;
  }

  const safePage = Math.max(1, Number(page) || 1);
  const safeLimit = Math.min(50, Math.max(1, Number(limit) || 10));

  const results = await Promise.all(
    types.map(async (resourceType) => {
      const Model = MODELS[resourceType];

      const query = {
        uploadedBy: userId,
        isDeleted: false,
      };

      if (status && ["pending", "approved", "rejected"].includes(status)) {
        query.approvalStatus = status;
      }

      const [resources, total] = await Promise.all([
        Model.find(query)
          .select("-parts.storageKey")
          .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
          .lean(),
        Model.countDocuments(query),
      ]);

      return resources.map((resource) => ({
        ...resource,
        resourceType,
      }));
    })
  );

  const combined = results
    .flat()
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        new Date(b.createdAt).getTime() -
        new Date(a.createdAt).getTime()
    );

  const total = combined.length;
  const start = (safePage - 1) * safeLimit;

  return {
    resources: combined.slice(start, start + safeLimit),
    pagination: {
      page: safePage,
      limit: safeLimit,
      total,
      totalPages: Math.ceil(total / safeLimit),
    },
  };
}


========== Backend\src\services\resource.service.js ==========
import Note from "../models/Note.js";
import PYQ from "../models/PYQ.js";
import User from "../models/User.js";
import AcademicStructure from "../models/AcademicStructure.js";
import env from "../config/env.js";

import {
  uploadPdfParts,
  deleteStoredFiles,
  createDownloadUrl
} from "./storage.service.js";

function createError(message, statusCode = 400) {
  const error = new Error(message);
  error.statusCode = statusCode;
  return error;
}

function getModel(type) {
  if (type === "notes") return Note;
  if (type === "pyqs") return PYQ;

  throw createError("Invalid resource type.", 400);
}

async function getUploader(userId) {
  const user = await User.findById(userId)
    .select("role collegeId collegeAffiliationStatus emailVerified isActive teacherApprovalStatus");

  if (!user || !user.isActive || !user.emailVerified) {
    throw createError("An active verified account is required.", 403);
  }

  if (!user.collegeId || user.collegeAffiliationStatus !== "verified") {
    throw createError("Your college affiliation must be verified before uploading.", 403);
  }

  if (
    user.role === "teacher" &&
    user.teacherApprovalStatus !== "approved"
  ) {
    throw createError("Your teacher account must be approved before uploading.", 403);
  }

  if (!["student", "teacher"].includes(user.role)) {
    throw createError("Your account cannot upload resources.", 403);
  }

  return user;
}

async function validateAcademicStructure(data, collegeId) {
  const structure = await AcademicStructure.findOne({
    _id: data.academicStructureId,
    collegeId,
    isActive: true
  }).lean();

  if (!structure) {
    throw createError("The selected academic structure is invalid.", 400);
  }

  if (
    structure.stream !== data.stream ||
    structure.branch !== data.branch ||
    !structure.academicYears.includes(data.academicYear) ||
    !structure.semesters.includes(Number(data.semester))
  ) {
    throw createError(
      "The academic details do not match the selected academic structure.",
      400
    );
  }

  const subject = structure.subjects.find(
    (item) =>
      item.subjectCode === data.subjectCode &&
      item.subjectName === data.subjectName &&
      item.semester === Number(data.semester)
  );

  if (!subject) {
    throw createError(
      "The selected subject does not belong to this academic structure and semester.",
      400
    );
  }
}

function validatePartCount(data, files) {
  if (data.hasMultipleParts && files.length < 2) {
    throw createError(
      "Multiple parts were selected, but fewer than two PDF files were uploaded."
    );
  }

  if (!data.hasMultipleParts && files.length !== 1) {
    throw createError(
      "Upload one PDF file or enable multiple parts."
    );
  }
}

export async function createResource({
  type,
  userId,
  data,
  files
}) {
  const Model = getModel(type);

  const uploader = await getUploader(userId);

  validatePartCount(data, files);

  await validateAcademicStructure(data, uploader.collegeId);

  const parts = await uploadPdfParts(files);

  try {
    const resourceData = {
      ...data,
      collegeId: uploader.collegeId,
      academicStructureId: data.academicStructureId,
      semester: Number(data.semester),
      subjectCode: data.subjectCode.toUpperCase(),
      hasMultipleParts: Boolean(data.hasMultipleParts),
      parts,
      uploadedBy: uploader._id,
      approvalStatus: "pending",
      reviewedBy: null,
      reviewedAt: null,
      rejectionReason: "",
      isDeleted: false
    };

    const resource = await Model.create(resourceData);

    return resource;
  } catch (error) {
    await deleteStoredFiles(parts);
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function browseResources(type, filters) {
  const Model = getModel(type);

  const {
    page,
    limit,
    q,
    ...academicFilters
  } = filters;

  const query = {
    ...academicFilters,
    approvalStatus: "approved",
    isDeleted: false
  };

  if (q) {
    query.$or = [
      { title: { $regex: escapeRegex(q), $options: "i" } },
      { subjectName: { $regex: escapeRegex(q), $options: "i" } },
      { subjectCode: { $regex: escapeRegex(q), $options: "i" } }
    ];
  }

  const [resources, total] = await Promise.all([
    Model.find(query)
      .select("-parts.storageKey")
      .populate("uploadedBy", "name")
      .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
      .skip((page - 1) * limit)
      .limit(limit)
      .lean(),

    Model.countDocuments(query)
  ]);

  return {
    resources,
    pagination: {
      page,
      limit,
      total,
      totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
    }
  };
}

export async function getResource(type, resourceId) {
  const Model = getModel(type);

  const resource = await Model.findOne({
    _id: resourceId,
    approvalStatus: "approved",
    isDeleted: false
  })
    .select("-parts.storageKey")
    .populate("uploadedBy", "name")
    .lean();

  if (!resource) {
    throw createError("Resource not found.", 404);
  }

  return resource;
}

export async function getDownloadLink({
  type,
  resourceId,
  partId
}) {
  const Model = getModel(type);

  const resource = await Model.findOne({
    _id: resourceId,
    approvalStatus: "approved",
    isDeleted: false
  }).lean();

  if (!resource) {
    throw createError("Resource not found or not approved.", 404);
  }

  const part = resource.parts.find(
    (item) => item._id.toString() === partId
  );

  if (!part) {
    throw createError("PDF part not found.", 404);
  }

  const url = await createDownloadUrl(part.storageKey);

  return {
    url,
    expiresIn: env.SIGNED_URL_EXPIRY_SECONDS,
    fileName: part.originalFileName
  };
}

export async function getPendingResources(type, page = 1, limit = 20) {
  const Model = getModel(type);

  const query = {
    approvalStatus: "pending",
    isDeleted: false
  };

  const [resources, total] = await Promise.all([
    Model.find(query)
      .select("-parts.storageKey")
      .populate("uploadedBy", "name email")
      .sort({ createdAt: 1 })
      .skip((page - 1) * limit)
      .limit(limit)
      .lean(),

    Model.countDocuments(query)
  ]);

  return {
    resources,
    pagination: {
      page,
      limit,
      total,
      totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
    }
  };
}

export async function moderateResource({
  type,
  resourceId,
  adminId,
  action,
  rejectionReason = ""
}) {
  const Model = getModel(type);

  const resource = await Model.findOne({
    _id: resourceId,
    isDeleted: false
  });

  if (!resource) {
    throw createError("Resource not found.", 404);
  }

  if (resource.approvalStatus !== "pending") {
    throw createError("Only pending resources can be reviewed.", 409);
  }

  if (resource.uploadedBy.toString() === adminId.toString()) {
    throw createError("You cannot review your own upload.", 403);
  }

  resource.approvalStatus = action === "approve" ? "approved" : "rejected";
  resource.reviewedBy = adminId;
  resource.reviewedAt = new Date();
  resource.rejectionReason =
    action === "reject" ? rejectionReason.trim() : "";

  await resource.save();

  return resource;
}

function escapeRegex(value) {
  return value.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
}

========== Backend\src\services\storage.service.js ==========
import {
  PutObjectCommand,
  DeleteObjectCommand,
  GetObjectCommand
} from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";

import { getSignedUrl } from "@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner";

import crypto from "node:crypto";
import path from "node:path";

import {
  storageClient,
  STORAGE_BUCKET
} from "../config/storage.js";

import env from "../config/env.js";

function ensureStorageConfigured() {
  if (!storageClient) {
    throw new Error("Backblaze B2 is not configured.");
  }
}

export async function uploadPdf(file) {
  ensureStorageConfigured();

  const checksum = crypto
    .createHash("sha256")
    .update(file.buffer)
    .digest("hex");

  const storageKey = [
    "resources",
    new Date().getUTCFullYear(),
    crypto.randomUUID() + ".pdf"
  ].join("/");

  await storageClient.send(
    new PutObjectCommand({
      Bucket: STORAGE_BUCKET,
      Key: storageKey,
      Body: file.buffer,
      ContentLength: file.size,
      ContentType: "application/pdf",
      Metadata: {
        sha256: checksum
      }
    })
  );

  return {
    partNumber: 1,
    originalFileName: path.basename(file.originalname).slice(0, 255),
    storageKey,
    contentType: "application/pdf",
    sizeBytes: file.size,
    checksum
  };
}

export async function uploadPdfParts(files) {
  const uploadedParts = [];

  try {
    for (let index = 0; index < files.length; index += 1) {
      const part = await uploadPdf(files[index]);

      uploadedParts.push({
        ...part,
        partNumber: index + 1
      });
    }

    return uploadedParts;
  } catch (error) {
    await Promise.allSettled(
      uploadedParts.map((part) => deleteStoredFile(part.storageKey))
    );

    throw error;
  }
}

export async function deleteStoredFile(storageKey) {
  if (!storageKey) return;

  ensureStorageConfigured();

  await storageClient.send(
    new DeleteObjectCommand({
      Bucket: STORAGE_BUCKET,
      Key: storageKey
    })
  );
}

export async function deleteStoredFiles(parts = []) {
  return Promise.allSettled(
    parts.map((part) => deleteStoredFile(part.storageKey))
  );
}

export async function createDownloadUrl(storageKey) {
  ensureStorageConfigured();

  if (
    typeof storageKey !== "string" ||
    !storageKey.startsWith("resources/")
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid storage key.");
  }

  const command = new GetObjectCommand({
    Bucket: STORAGE_BUCKET,
    Key: storageKey
  });

  return getSignedUrl(storageClient, command, {
    expiresIn: env.SIGNED_URL_EXPIRY_SECONDS
  });
}

========== Backend\src\utils\emailverification.js ==========
import { randomBytes, createHash } from "node:crypto";

const TOKEN_BYTES = 32;
const TOKEN_EXPIRY_MINUTES = 30;

/**
 * Generate a cryptographically secure email verification token.
 * Store the hash in the database, not the raw token.
 */
export function generateEmailVerificationToken() {
  const token = randomBytes(TOKEN_BYTES).toString("hex");

  const tokenHash = createHash("sha256")
    .update(token)
    .digest("hex");

  const expiresAt = new Date(
    Date.now() + TOKEN_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60 * 1000
  );

  return {
    token,
    tokenHash,
    expiresAt
  };
}

/**
 * Hash a verification token received from the user.
 * Compare the resulting hash with the value stored in MongoDB.
 */
export function hashEmailVerificationToken(token) {
  if (typeof token !== "string" || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(token)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid email verification token.");
  }

  return createHash("sha256")
    .update(token)
    .digest("hex");
}


========== Backend\src\utils\password.js ==========
import argon2 from "argon2";

const PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH = 12;
const PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH = 128;

export function validatePassword(password) {
  if (typeof password !== "string") {
    return {
      valid: false,
      message: "Password must be a string."
    };
  }

  if (password.length < PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      message: `Password must contain at least ${PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH} characters.`
    };
  }

  if (password.length > PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      message: `Password cannot exceed ${PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH} characters.`
    };
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

export async function hashPassword(password) {
  const validation = validatePassword(password);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    throw new Error(validation.message);
  }

  return argon2.hash(password, {
    type: argon2.argon2id
  });
}

export async function verifyPassword(password, passwordHash) {
  if (
    typeof password !== "string" ||
    typeof passwordHash !== "string" ||
    !passwordHash.startsWith("$argon2id$")
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  try {
    return await argon2.verify(passwordHash, password);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}


========== Backend\src\utils\passwordReset.js ==========

import { randomBytes, createHash } from "node:crypto";

const TOKEN_BYTES = 32;
const TOKEN_EXPIRY_MINUTES = 30;

export function generatePasswordResetToken() {
  const token = randomBytes(TOKEN_BYTES).toString("hex");

  const tokenHash = createHash("sha256")
    .update(token)
    .digest("hex");

  const expiresAt = new Date(
    Date.now() + TOKEN_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60 * 1000
  );

  return {
    token,
    tokenHash,
    expiresAt
  };
}

export function hashPasswordResetToken(token) {
  if (
    typeof token !== "string" ||
    !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(token)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid password reset token.");
  }

  return createHash("sha256")
    .update(token)
    .digest("hex");
}


========== Backend\src\utils\token.js ==========
import { SignJWT, jwtVerify } from "jose";
import env from "../config/env.js";

function getSecret() {
  if (!env.JWT_SECRET || env.JWT_SECRET.length < 64) {
    throw new Error(
      "JWT_SECRET must contain at least 64 characters before authentication can be used."
    );
  }

  return new TextEncoder().encode(env.JWT_SECRET);
}

/**
 * Generate a short-lived access token (15 minutes).
 *
 * The sessionVersion is embedded so that incrementing it on the User
 * document immediately invalidates all previously issued tokens for
 * that account (logout, password-change, security events).
 */
export async function generateAccessToken(user) {
  return new SignJWT({
    role: user.role,
    sv: user.sessionVersion ?? 0
  })
    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: "HS256", typ: "JWT" })
    .setSubject(user._id.toString())
    .setIssuedAt()
    .setExpirationTime("15m")
    .setIssuer("opennotes-api")
    .setAudience("opennotes-client")
    .sign(getSecret());
}

export async function verifyAccessToken(token) {
  const { payload } = await jwtVerify(token, getSecret(), {
    algorithms: ["HS256"],
    issuer: "opennotes-api",
    audience: "opennotes-client"
  });

  return payload;
}

========== Backend\src\validators\auth.validator.js ==========
import { z } from "zod";

export const registerSchema = z
  .object({
    name: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .min(2, "Name must contain at least 2 characters.")
      .max(100, "Name cannot exceed 100 characters."),

    email: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase()
      .email("Enter a valid email address.")
      .max(254, "Email cannot exceed 254 characters."),

    password: z
      .string()
      .min(12, "Password must contain at least 12 characters.")
      .max(128, "Password cannot exceed 128 characters."),

    collegeId: z
      .string()
      .regex(/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i, "Enter a valid college ID.")
      .optional()
  })
  .strict();

export const loginSchema = z
  .object({
    email: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase()
      .email("Enter a valid email address.")
      .max(254, "Email cannot exceed 254 characters."),

    password: z
      .string()
      .min(1, "Password is required.")
      .max(128, "Password cannot exceed 128 characters.")
  })
  .strict();

========== Backend\src\validators\college.validator.js ==========
import { z } from "zod";

const domainSchema = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .toLowerCase()
  .min(3, "Email domain is required.")
  .max(253, "Email domain is too long.")
  .regex(
    /^(?=.{1,253}$)(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,63}$/,
    "Enter a valid domain, such as college.edu."
  );

export const createCollegeSchema = z
  .object({
    name: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .min(2, "College name must contain at least 2 characters.")
      .max(200, "College name cannot exceed 200 characters."),

    domains: z.array(domainSchema).max(20).default([]),

    address: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .max(500, "Address cannot exceed 500 characters.")
      .default(""),

    website: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .max(500, "Website cannot exceed 500 characters.")
      .default("")
      .refine(
        (value) => {
          if (!value) return true;

          try {
            const url = new URL(value);
            return ["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol);
          } catch {
            return false;
          }
        },
        { message: "Website must be a valid HTTP or HTTPS URL." }
      )
  })
  .strict()
  .superRefine((data, ctx) => {
    const normalizedDomains = data.domains.map((domain) =>
      domain.toLowerCase()
    );

    if (new Set(normalizedDomains).size !== normalizedDomains.length) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["domains"],
        message: "Duplicate email domains are not allowed."
      });
    }
  });

export const collegeSearchSchema = z
  .object({
    q: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100),
    limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(20).default(10)
  })
  .strict();

export const collegeIdSchema = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i, "A valid college ID is required.");

========== Backend\src\validators\note.validator.js ==========
import { z } from "zod";

const objectId = z.string().regex(
  /^[a-f\d]{24}$/i,
  "A valid ID is required."
);

const yearSchema = z.enum([
  "1st Year",
  "2nd Year",
  "3rd Year",
  "4th Year",
  "5th Year"
]);

export const createNoteSchema = z.object({
  academicStructureId: objectId,

  stream: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100),

  branch: z.string().trim().min(1).max(150),

  academicYear: yearSchema,

  semester: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(12),

  subjectName: z.string().trim().min(1).max(150),

  subjectCode: z.string().trim().toUpperCase().min(1).max(30),

  moduleNumber: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(50),

  title: z.string().trim().min(3).max(200),

  description: z.string().trim().max(2000).default(""),

  hasMultipleParts: z.preprocess(
    (value) => value === true || value === "true",
    z.boolean()
  )
}).strict();

export const resourceIdSchema = objectId;

export const browseNoteSchema = z.object({
  collegeId: objectId.optional(),
  academicStructureId: objectId.optional(),
  branch: z.string().trim().max(150).optional(),
  academicYear: yearSchema.optional(),
  semester: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(12).optional(),
  subjectCode: z.string().trim().toUpperCase().max(30).optional(),
  moduleNumber: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(50).optional(),
  q: z.string().trim().max(100).optional(),
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(50).default(20)
}).strict();

========== Backend\src\validators\pyq.validator.js ==========
import { z } from "zod";

const objectId = z.string().regex(
  /^[a-f\d]{24}$/i,
  "A valid ID is required."
);

const yearSchema = z.enum([
  "1st Year",
  "2nd Year",
  "3rd Year",
  "4th Year",
  "5th Year"
]);

export const createPYQSchema = z.object({
  academicStructureId: objectId,

  stream: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100),

  branch: z.string().trim().min(1).max(150),

  academicYear: yearSchema,

  semester: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(12),

  subjectName: z.string().trim().min(1).max(150),

  subjectCode: z.string().trim().toUpperCase().min(1).max(30),

  examType: z.enum(["CIE", "SEE"]),

  examinationYear: z.coerce.number().int().min(1950).max(2200),

  title: z.string().trim().min(3).max(200),

  description: z.string().trim().max(2000).default(""),

  hasMultipleParts: z.preprocess(
    (value) => value === true || value === "true",
    z.boolean()
  )
}).strict();

export const browsePYQSchema = z.object({
  collegeId: objectId.optional(),
  academicStructureId: objectId.optional(),
  branch: z.string().trim().max(150).optional(),
  academicYear: yearSchema.optional(),
  semester: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(12).optional(),
  subjectCode: z.string().trim().toUpperCase().max(30).optional(),
  examType: z.enum(["CIE", "SEE"]).optional(),
  examinationYear: z.coerce.number().int().min(1950).max(2200).optional(),
  q: z.string().trim().max(100).optional(),
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(50).default(20)
}).strict();

export const resourceIdSchema = objectId;

========== Frontend\src\App.jsx ==========

import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";

import MainLayout from "./Components/layout/MainLayout";
import ProtectedRoute from "./Components/auth/ProtectedRoute";
import PermissionRoute from "./Components/auth/PermissionRoute";

import Home from "./Pages/Home/Home";
import BrowseNotes from "./Pages/BrowseNotes/BrowseNotes";
import BrowsePYQs from "./Pages/BrowsePYQs/BrowsePYQs";
import Upload from "./Pages/Upload/Upload";
import MyUploads from "./Pages/MyUploads/MyUploads";
import Profile from "./Pages/Profile/Profile";
import Splash from "./Pages/Splash/Splash";
import Login from "./Pages/Auth/Login";
import Signup from "./Pages/Auth/Signup";


import AdminDashboard from "./Pages/Admin/AdminDashboard";
import TeacherApprovals from "./Pages/Admin/TeacherApprovals";

function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      {/* Public pages without the shared application layout */}
      <Route path="/splash" element={<Splash />} />
      <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
      <Route path="/signup" element={<Signup />} />

      {/* Shared layout: sidebar, topbar, and page content */}
      <Route element={<MainLayout />}>
        {/* Available to guests and signed-in users */}
        <Route path="/home" element={<Home />} />
        <Route path="/browse-notes" element={<BrowseNotes />} />

        {/* Requires the relevant role permission */}
        <Route element={<PermissionRoute permission="viewPYQs" />}>
          <Route path="/browse-pyqs" element={<BrowsePYQs />} />
        </Route>

        <Route element={<PermissionRoute permission="uploadNotes" />}>
          <Route path="/upload" element={<Upload />} />
        </Route>

        {/* Personal pages require an authenticated account */}
        <Route element={<ProtectedRoute />}>
          <Route path="/profile" element={<Profile />} />
          <Route path="/my-uploads" element={<MyUploads />} />
        </Route>


        {/* Admin dashboard and teacher approvals */}
        <Route element={<ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["admin"]} />}>
          <Route
            path="/admin/dashboard"
            element={<AdminDashboard />}
          />
          <Route
            path="/admin/teachers"
            element={<TeacherApprovals />}
          />
        </Route>
      </Route>

      {/* Home is the default landing page */}
      <Route path="/" element={<Navigate to="/home" replace />} />

      {/* Unknown routes return to Home */}
      <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/home" replace />} />
    </Routes>
  );
}

export default App;


========== Frontend\src\Components\auth\PermissionRoute.jsx ==========

import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";

export default function PermissionRoute({ permission }) {
  const { can, isAuthenticated, role } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  // Explicitly block guests from protected resources.
  if (role === "guest" || !can(permission)) {
    if (!isAuthenticated) {
      return (
        <Navigate
          to="/login"
          state={{ from: location }}
          replace
        />
      );
    }

    return <Navigate to="/home" replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}


========== Frontend\src\Components\auth\ProtectedRoute.jsx ==========

import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";

export default function ProtectedRoute({ allowedRoles = [] }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, role } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <Navigate
        to="/login"
        state={{ from: location }}
        replace
      />
    );
  }

  if (allowedRoles.length > 0 && !allowedRoles.includes(role)) {
    const roleHome = {
      student: "/student/dashboard",
      teacher: "/teacher/dashboard",
      admin: "/admin/dashboard",
    };

    return (
      <Navigate
        to={roleHome[role] || "/home"}
        replace
      />
    );
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}


========== Frontend\src\Components\auth\RoleRoute.jsx ==========
import { Navigate, Outlet } from "react-router-dom";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";

function RoleRoute({ allowedRoles }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, role } = useAuth();

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  if (!allowedRoles.includes(role)) {
    return <Navigate to="/home" replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

export default RoleRoute;

import {
  BookOpen,
  FileText,
  Layers,
} from "lucide-react";

function NoteCard({ note }) {
  const hasMultipleParts = note.parts.length > 1;

  return (
    <article className="note-card">

      <div className="note-card-icon">
        <FileText size={24} />
      </div>

      <div className="note-card-content">

        <span className="note-subject">
          {note.subject}
        </span>

        <h3>
          {note.title}
        </h3>

        <div className="note-meta">

          <span>
            <BookOpen size={14} />
            {note.type}
          </span>

          <span>
            <Layers size={14} />

            {hasMultipleParts
              ? `${note.parts.length} Parts`
              : "Single Note"}
          </span>

        </div>

      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        className="note-view-button"
      >
        View
      </button>

    </article>
  );
}

export default NoteCard;

========== Frontend\src\Components\layout\MainLayout.jsx ==========
import { useState } from "react";
import { Outlet } from "react-router-dom";

import Sidebar from "./Sidebar";
import Topbar from "./Topbar";

function MainLayout() {
  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false);

  const handleMenuClick = () => {
    setSidebarOpen(true);
  };

  const handleCloseSidebar = () => {
    setSidebarOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="app-layout">
      <Sidebar
        isOpen={sidebarOpen}
        onClose={handleCloseSidebar}
      />

      <div className="main-area">
        <Topbar onMenuClick={handleMenuClick} />

        <main className="page-content">
          <Outlet />
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default MainLayout;

========== Frontend\src\Components\layout\Sidebar.jsx ==========

import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  Home,
  BookOpen,
  FileQuestion,
  Upload,
  FolderOpen,
  MessageCircle,
  UserRound,
  ShieldCheck,
  X,
  LogIn,
} from "lucide-react";

import Logo from "../common/Logo";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";
import "../../css/sidebar.css";

const publicLinks = [
  { label: "Home", path: "/home", icon: Home },
  { label: "Browse Notes", path: "/browse-notes", icon: BookOpen },
];

const studentLinks = [
  ...publicLinks,
  { label: "Browse PYQs", path: "/browse-pyqs", icon: FileQuestion },
  { label: "Upload", path: "/upload", icon: Upload },
  { label: "My Uploads", path: "/my-uploads", icon: FolderOpen },
  { label: "Discussion Forum", path: "/discussion", icon: MessageCircle },
  { label: "Profile", path: "/profile", icon: UserRound },
];

const teacherLinks = [...studentLinks];

const adminLinks = [
  ...studentLinks,
  { label: "Teacher Approvals", path: "/admin/teachers", icon: ShieldCheck },
];

function Sidebar({ isOpen = false, onClose = () => {} }) {
  const { role } = useAuth();

  const links =
    role === "admin"
      ? adminLinks
      : role === "teacher"
        ? teacherLinks
        : role === "student"
          ? studentLinks
          : publicLinks;

  return (
    <>
      {isOpen && (
        <button
          type="button"
          className="sidebar-overlay"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Close navigation"
        />
      )}

      <aside className={`app-sidebar ${isOpen ? "sidebar-open" : ""}`}>
        <div className="sidebar-brand">
          <div className="sidebar-brand-logo">
            <Logo />
          </div>

          <p className="sidebar-tagline">Learn • Share • Grow</p>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="sidebar-mobile-close"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close sidebar"
          >
            <X size={19} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="sidebar-nav-heading">MENU</div>

        <nav className="sidebar-nav">
          {links.map(({ label, path, icon: Icon }) => (
            <NavLink
              key={path}
              to={path}
              onClick={onClose}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `sidebar-link ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`
              }
            >
              <Icon size={19} />
              <span>{label}</span>
            </NavLink>
          ))}

          {role === "guest" && (
            <NavLink
              to="/login"
              onClick={onClose}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `sidebar-link sidebar-login-link ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`
              }
            >
              <LogIn size={19} />
              <span>Sign in / Create account</span>
            </NavLink>
          )}
        </nav>

        <div className="sidebar-footer">
          <span className="sidebar-footer-title">OpenNotes</span>
          <span className="sidebar-footer-text">
            Learn together. Grow together.
          </span>
        </div>
      </aside>
    </>
  );
}

export default Sidebar;


========== Frontend\src\Components\layout\Topbar.jsx ==========
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  Bell,
  ChevronDown,
  LogIn,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  Moon,
  Settings,
  Sun,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useTheme } from "../../context/useTheme";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";
import Logo from "../common/Logo";

function getInitials(name = "") {
  return (
    name
      .trim()
      .split(/\s+/)
      .filter(Boolean)
      .slice(0, 2)
      .map((part) => part[0].toUpperCase())
      .join("") || "U"
  );
}

function Topbar({ onMenuClick }) {
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const { currentUser, isAuthenticated, role, logout } = useAuth();
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const menuRef = useRef(null);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const displayName = currentUser?.name || "Guest";
  const email = currentUser?.email || "";
  const profileImage = currentUser?.profileImage || "";
  const initials = getInitials(displayName);

  useEffect(() => {
    const closeOnOutsideClick = (event) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(event.target)) {
        setMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const closeOnEscape = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") setMenuOpen(false);
    };

    document.addEventListener("mousedown", closeOnOutsideClick);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", closeOnOutsideClick);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const updateProfile = () => setMenuOpen(false);
    window.addEventListener("openNotesProfileUpdated", updateProfile);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("openNotesProfileUpdated", updateProfile);
  }, []);

  const goToProfile = () => {
    setMenuOpen(false);
    navigate("/profile");
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    setMenuOpen(false);
    logout();
    navigate("/home", { replace: true });
  };

  return (
    <header className="topbar">
      <div className="topbar-left">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="menu-button"
          onClick={onMenuClick}
          aria-label="Open navigation"
        >
          <Menu size={24} />
        </button>

        <Link to="/home" className="mobile-logo" aria-label="OpenNotes home">
          <Logo />
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="topbar-actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="topbar-icon-button"
          aria-label="Notifications"
          title="Notifications"
          onClick={() => {}}
        >
          <Bell size={20} />
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="topbar-icon-button"
          onClick={toggleTheme}
          aria-label="Toggle theme"
          title="Toggle theme"
        >
          {theme === "dark" ? <Sun size={20} /> : <Moon size={20} />}
        </button>

        <div className="topbar-account" ref={menuRef}>
          {isAuthenticated ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="topbar-account-trigger"
              onClick={() => setMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
              aria-expanded={menuOpen}
              aria-haspopup="menu"
              aria-label="Open account menu"
            >
              <span className="topbar-avatar">
                {profileImage ? <img src={profileImage} alt="" /> : initials}
              </span>

              <span className="topbar-account-text">
                <strong>{displayName}</strong>
                <small>{role}</small>
              </span>

              <ChevronDown
                size={16}
                className={menuOpen ? "account-chevron-open" : ""}
              />
            </button>
          ) : (
            <div className="topbar-guest-actions">
              <span className="topbar-guest-label">Guest</span>
              <Link className="topbar-signin-button" to="/login">
                Sign in
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}

          {isAuthenticated && menuOpen && (
            <div className="topbar-account-menu" role="menu">
              <div className="topbar-menu-identity">
                <span className="topbar-avatar topbar-avatar-large">
                  {profileImage ? <img src={profileImage} alt="" /> : initials}
                </span>

                <div>
                  <strong>{displayName}</strong>
                  <span>{email}</span>
                  <small>{role}</small>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="topbar-menu-divider" />

              <button
                type="button"
                className="topbar-menu-item"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={goToProfile}
              >
                <UserRound size={17} />
                My Profile & Workspace
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="topbar-menu-item"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={goToProfile}
              >
                <Settings size={17} />
                Account settings
              </button>

              <div className="topbar-menu-divider" />

              <button
                type="button"
                className="topbar-menu-item topbar-logout-item"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={handleLogout}
              >
                <LogOut size={17} />
                Sign out
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {!isAuthenticated && (
          <Link
            to="/login"
            className="topbar-mobile-signin"
            aria-label="Sign in"
          >
            <LogIn size={19} />
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

export default Topbar;


========== Frontend\src\context\AuthContext.jsx ==========

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  guestUser,
  getRolePermissions,
  ROLES,
} from "../data/authData";

import {
  authApi,
  getAccessToken,
  setAccessToken,
} from "../services/api";

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function restoreSession() {
      const token = getAccessToken();

      if (!token) {
        if (active) setAuthLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const result = await authApi.me();

        if (active) {
          setUser(result?.data || null);
        }
      } catch {
        setAccessToken(null);

        if (active) {
          setUser(null);
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) {
          setAuthLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    restoreSession();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const login = useCallback(async (email, password) => {
    try {
      const result = await authApi.login({
        email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
        password,
      });

      const data = result?.data;

      if (!data?.accessToken || !data?.user) {
        throw new Error("Invalid login response from server.");
      }

      setAccessToken(data.accessToken);
      setUser(data.user);

      return {
        success: true,
        message: result.message || "Signed in successfully.",
        user: data.user,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: error.message || "Unable to sign in.",
      };
    }
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      if (getAccessToken()) {
        await authApi.logoutAll();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn("Logout request failed:", error.message);
    } finally {
      setAccessToken(null);
      setUser(null);
    }
  }, []);

  const updateUser = useCallback((updates) => {
    setUser((current) =>
      current
        ? {
            ...current,
            ...updates,
            id: current.id,
            _id: current._id,
            role: current.role,
          }
        : current
    );
  }, []);

  const role = user?.role || ROLES.GUEST;
  const permissions = getRolePermissions(role) || {};

  const can = useCallback(
    (permission) => Boolean(permissions[permission]),
    [permissions]
  );

  const hasRole = useCallback(
    (...roles) => roles.includes(role),
    [role]
  );

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      user,
      currentUser: user || guestUser,
      isAuthenticated: Boolean(user),
      authLoading,
      role,
      permissions,
      can,
      hasRole,
      isGuest: role === ROLES.GUEST,
      isStudent: role === ROLES.STUDENT,
      isTeacher: role === ROLES.TEACHER,
      isAdmin: role === ROLES.ADMIN,
      login,
      logout,
      updateUser,
    }),
    [
      user,
      authLoading,
      role,
      permissions,
      can,
      hasRole,
      login,
      logout,
      updateUser,
    ]
  );

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export default AuthContext;


========== Frontend\src\context\ThemeContext.jsx ==========
/* eslint-disable react-refresh/only-export-components */

import { createContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

export const ThemeContext = createContext(null);

export function ThemeProvider({ children }) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => {
    const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem("openNotes-theme");

    return savedTheme || "dark";
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute(
      "data-theme",
      theme
    );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "openNotes-theme",
      theme
    );
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme((currentTheme) =>
      currentTheme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"
    );
  };

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider
      value={{
        theme,
        toggleTheme,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

========== Frontend\src\context\useAuth.jsx ==========
import { useContext } from "react";

import AuthContext from "./AuthContext";

function useAuth() {
  const context =
    useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error(
      "useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider."
    );
  }

  return context;
}

export default useAuth;

========== Frontend\src\context\useTheme.jsx ==========
import { useContext } from "react";

import { ThemeContext } from "./ThemeContext";

export function useTheme() {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error(
      "useTheme must be used inside ThemeProvider"
    );
  }

  return context;
}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| OpenNotes - Mock Authentication & Role Data

/* =========================================================================
   ROLE DEFINITIONS
========================================================================= */

export const ROLES = {
  GUEST: "guest",
  STUDENT: "student",
  TEACHER: "teacher",
  ADMIN: "admin",
};

/* =========================================================================
   USER STATUS

/* =========================================================================
   MOCK STUDENTS

export const mockStudents = mockUsers.filter(
  (user) => user.role === ROLES.STUDENT
);

/* =========================================================================
   MOCK TEACHERS
========================================================================= */

export const mockTeachers = mockUsers.filter(
  (user) => user.role === ROLES.TEACHER
);

/* =========================================================================
   MOCK ADMINS
========================================================================= */

export const mockAdmins = mockUsers.filter(
  (user) => user.role === ROLES.ADMIN
);

/* =========================================================================
   FIND USER BY EMAIL
========================================================================= */

export const findUserByEmail = (email) => {
  if (!email) return null;

  return mockUsers.find(
    (user) =>
      user.email.toLowerCase() ===
      email.toLowerCase()
  ) || null;
};

/* =========================================================================
   MOCK LOGIN VALIDATION
========================================================================= */

export const validateMockLogin = (
  email,
  password
) => {
  const user = mockUsers.find(
    (item) =>
      item.email.toLowerCase() ===
        email.toLowerCase() &&
      item.password === password
  );

  if (!user) {
    return {
      success: false,

      message:
        "Invalid email or password.",

      user: null,
    };
  }

  if (
    user.status ===
    USER_STATUS.SUSPENDED
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,

      message:
        "Your account has been suspended. Please contact the administrator.",

      user: null,
    };
  }

  if (
    user.role === ROLES.TEACHER &&
    user.teacherApprovalStatus !==
      TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS.APPROVED
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,

      message:
        "Your teacher account is waiting for administrator approval.",

      user: null,
    };
  }

  return {
    success: true,

    message: "Login successful.",

    user,
  };
};

/* =========================================================================
   GET ROLE PERMISSIONS
========================================================================= */

export const getRolePermissions = (
  role
) => {
  return (
    rolePermissions[role] ||
    rolePermissions[ROLES.GUEST]
  );
};

/* =========================================================================
   CHECK PERMISSION
========================================================================= */

export const hasPermission = (
  role,
  permission
) => {
  const permissions =
    getRolePermissions(role);

  return Boolean(
    permissions[permission]
  );
};

   ROLE DISPLAY NAMES

========== Frontend\src\data\pyqData.js ==========
const createPaper = (id, subjectName, examType, year, pages) => ({
  id,
  year,
  title: `${subjectName} - ${examType} Question Paper`,
  examType,
  fileType: "PDF",
  pages,
  fileUrl: "#",
});

const createSubject = (
  id,
  name,
  code,
  cieYears = ["2025", "2024", "2023"],
  seeYears = ["2025", "2024", "2023"]
) => ({
  id,
  name,
  code,

  cie: cieYears.map((year) =>
    createPaper(
      `${id}-cie-${year}`,
      name,
      "CIE",
      year,
      4
    )
  ),

  see: seeYears.map((year) =>
    createPaper(
      `${id}-see-${year}`,
      name,
      "SEE",
      year,
      7
    )
  ),
});
