========== Frontend\src\Pages\Admin\AdminDashboard.jsx ==========

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  BookOpen,
  Clock3,
  FileQuestion,
  FileText,
  GraduationCap,
  ShieldCheck,
  Users,
  UserCheck,
} from "lucide-react";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";
import { getAdminDashboardStats } from "../../services/dashboardService";
import "../../css/admin-dashboard.css";

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const { currentUser } = useAuth();

  const name = useMemo(
    () => currentUser?.name?.trim() || "Administrator",
    [currentUser?.name]
  );

  const stats = useMemo(() => getAdminDashboardStats(), []);

  const cards = [
    {
      label: "Total Students",
      value: stats.totalStudents,
      description: "Registered student accounts",
      icon: GraduationCap,
    },
    {
      label: "Total Teachers",
      value: stats.totalTeachers,
      description: "Registered teacher accounts",
      icon: Users,
    },
    {
      label: "Pending Approvals",
      value: stats.pendingTeacherApprovals,
      description: "Teacher requests to review",
      icon: Clock3,
    },
    {
      label: "Study Resources",
      value: stats.resourceCount,
      description: `${stats.notesCount} notes · ${stats.pyqsCount} PYQs`,
      icon: BookOpen,
    },
  ];

  const actions = [
    {
      title: "Teacher Approvals",
      description: "Approve or reject teacher registration requests.",
      path: "/admin/teachers",
      label: "Review requests",
      icon: UserCheck,
    },
    {
      title: "Browse Notes",
      description: "Check the notes available on the platform.",
      path: "/browse-notes",
      label: "Browse notes",
      icon: FileText,
    },
    {
      title: "Browse PYQs",
      description: "Explore previous-year question papers.",
      path: "/browse-pyqs",
      label: "Browse PYQs",
      icon: FileQuestion,
    },
    {
      title: "Upload Resources",
      description: "Open the resource upload workflow.",
      path: "/upload",
      label: "Open uploads",
      icon: BookOpen,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <main className="admin-dashboard dashboard-page">
      <section className="admin-welcome dashboard-welcome">
        <div>
          <span className="admin-eyebrow">
            <ShieldCheck size={15} />
            ADMINISTRATION
          </span>
          <h1>Welcome back, {name}</h1>
          <p>
            Review account approvals and keep OpenNotes resources organised.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="admin-welcome-icon" aria-hidden="true">
          <ShieldCheck size={34} />
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="admin-section">
        <div className="admin-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Platform overview</h2>
            <p>Counts based on this browser's demo data.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="admin-stats-grid">
          {cards.map(({ label, value, description, icon: Icon }) => (
            <article className="admin-stat-card" key={label}>
              <div className="admin-stat-top">
                <span>{label}</span>
                <span className="admin-stat-icon">
                  <Icon size={20} />
                </span>
              </div>
              <strong>{value}</strong>
              <p>{description}</p>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>

        <p className="admin-data-note">
          These are browser-demo totals, not shared production database counts.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="admin-section">
        <div className="admin-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Administration tools</h2>
            <p>Open a section to continue managing resources.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="admin-actions-grid">
          {actions.map(({ title, description, path, label, icon: Icon }) => (
            <article className="admin-action-card" key={title}>
              <span className="admin-action-icon">
                <Icon size={22} />
              </span>
              <h3>{title}</h3>
              <p>{description}</p>
              <Link to={path} className="admin-action-link">
                {label}
                <ArrowRight size={16} />
              </Link>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="admin-notice">
        <Clock3 size={21} />
        <div>
          <h3>Teacher approval queue</h3>
          <p>
            {stats.pendingTeacherApprovals === 0
              ? "There are no pending teacher approvals in the current demo data."
              : `${stats.pendingTeacherApprovals} teacher request(s) need review.`}
          </p>
          <Link to="/admin/teachers" className="admin-action-link">
            Open teacher approvals
            <ArrowRight size={16} />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}


========== Frontend\src\Pages\Admin\TeacherApprovals.jsx ==========

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Check,
  Clock3,
  RefreshCw,
  ShieldCheck,
  UserRound,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  getTeacherApprovalRequests,
  updateTeacherApproval,
} from "../../services/authService";

import "../../css/teacher-approvals.css";

function loadInitialRequests() {
  try {
    return getTeacherApprovalRequests();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to load teacher requests:", error);
    return [];
  }
}

export default function TeacherApprovals() {
  const [requests, setRequests] = useState(loadInitialRequests);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [processingEmail, setProcessingEmail] = useState("");
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState(null);
  const [confirmRequest, setConfirmRequest] = useState(null);

  // Refresh pending teacher requests.
  const loadRequests = () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setFeedback(null);

    try {
      const result = getTeacherApprovalRequests();

      setRequests(Array.isArray(result) ? result : []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Failed to refresh teacher requests:", error);

      setFeedback({
        type: "error",
        message: "Unable to load teacher requests. Please try again.",
      });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Approve or reject a teacher registration.
  const handleDecision = () => {
    if (!confirmRequest || processingEmail) return;

    const { email, decision } = confirmRequest;

    setProcessingEmail(email);
    setFeedback(null);

    try {
      const result = updateTeacherApproval(email, decision);

      if (!result || !result.success) {
        setFeedback({
          type: "error",
          message: result?.message || "Unable to process this request.",
        });
        return;
      }

      setRequests((previousRequests) =>
        previousRequests.filter(
          (request) => request.email !== email
        )
      );

      setFeedback({
        type: "success",
        message: result.message,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Teacher approval action failed:", error);

      setFeedback({
        type: "error",
        message: "Something went wrong. Please try again.",
      });
    } finally {
      setProcessingEmail("");
      setConfirmRequest(null);
    }
  };

  const openConfirmation = (request, decision) => {
    setConfirmRequest({
      email: request.email,
      name: request.name || "this applicant",
      decision,
    });
  };

  return (
    <main className="teacher-approvals">
      {/* Page header */}
      <header className="ta-page-header">
        <div>
          <span className="ta-eyebrow">
            <ShieldCheck size={15} />
            ADMINISTRATION
          </span>

          <h1>Teacher Approvals</h1>

          <p>
            Review teacher registration requests before granting
            access to teaching features.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="ta-refresh-button"
          type="button"
          onClick={loadRequests}
          disabled={loading || Boolean(processingEmail)}
        >
          <RefreshCw
            size={16}
            className={loading ? "ta-spin" : ""}
          />
          Refresh
        </button>
      </header>

      {/* Pending request count */}
      <section className="ta-summary">
        <div className="ta-summary-icon">
          <Clock3 size={23} />
        </div>

        <div>
          <span>Pending requests</span>
          <strong>{loading ? "…" : requests.length}</strong>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Feedback message */}
      {feedback && (
        <div
          className={`ta-feedback ta-feedback-${feedback.type}`}
          role="status"
        >
          <span>{feedback.message}</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Dismiss message"
            onClick={() => setFeedback(null)}
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Requests section */}
      <section className="ta-requests-section">
        <div className="ta-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Registration requests</h2>
            <p>
              Review each request before making a decision.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="ta-state">
            <RefreshCw
              className="ta-spin"
              size={24}
            />
            <p>Loading requests...</p>
          </div>
        ) : requests.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="ta-state">
            <div className="ta-empty-icon">
              <Check size={26} />
            </div>

            <h3>You're all caught up!</h3>

            <p>
              There are no pending teacher requests right now.
            </p>

            <button
              className="ta-secondary-button"
              type="button"
              onClick={loadRequests}
              disabled={loading}
            >
              Check again
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="ta-request-list">
            {requests.map((request) => (
              <article
                className="ta-request-card"
                key={request.email}
              >
                {/* Applicant details */}
                <div className="ta-applicant">
                  <div className="ta-avatar">
                    <UserRound size={23} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="ta-applicant-details">
                    <h3>
                      {request.name || "Unnamed applicant"}
                    </h3>

                    <p>{request.email}</p>

                    <span className="ta-pending-badge">
                      <Clock3 size={13} />
                      Pending review
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Account information */}
                <div className="ta-request-meta">
                  <span>Account type</span>
                  <strong>Teacher</strong>

                  <span>Registered</span>
                  <strong>
                    {request.createdAt &&
                    !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(request.createdAt))
                      ? new Date(
                          request.createdAt
                        ).toLocaleDateString()
                      : "Date unavailable"}
                  </strong>
                </div>

                {/* Approval actions */}
                <div className="ta-request-actions">
                  <button
                    className="ta-reject-button"
                    type="button"
                    disabled={Boolean(processingEmail)}
                    onClick={() =>
                      openConfirmation(request, "reject")
                    }
                  >
                    <X size={16} />
                    Reject
                  </button>

                  <button
                    className="ta-approve-button"
                    type="button"
                    disabled={Boolean(processingEmail)}
                    onClick={() =>
                      openConfirmation(request, "approve")
                    }
                  >
                    <Check size={16} />
                    Approve
                  </button>
                </div>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Confirmation dialog */}
      {confirmRequest && (
        <div
          className="ta-modal-backdrop"
          onClick={(event) => {
            if (
              event.target === event.currentTarget &&
              !processingEmail
            ) {
              setConfirmRequest(null);
            }
          }}
        >
          <section
            className="ta-confirm-modal"
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="ta-modal-title"
          >
            <div className="ta-modal-icon">
              {confirmRequest.decision === "approve" ? (
                <Check size={24} />
              ) : (
                <X size={24} />
              )}
            </div>

            <h2 id="ta-modal-title">
              {confirmRequest.decision === "approve"
                ? "Approve teacher?"
                : "Reject registration?"}
            </h2>

            <p>
              {confirmRequest.decision === "approve"
                ? `${confirmRequest.name} will be allowed to sign in as an approved teacher in this browser demo.`
                : `The registration for ${confirmRequest.name} will be rejected in this browser demo.`}
            </p>

            <div className="ta-modal-actions">
              <button
                className="ta-secondary-button"
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setConfirmRequest(null)}
                disabled={Boolean(processingEmail)}
              >
                Cancel
              </button>

              <button
                className={
                  confirmRequest.decision === "approve"
                    ? "ta-approve-button"
                    : "ta-reject-button"
                }
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDecision}
                disabled={Boolean(processingEmail)}
              >
                {processingEmail ? (
                  "Processing..."
                ) : (
                  "Confirm"
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

========== Frontend\src\Pages\Auth\Login.jsx ==========

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";
import "./Login.css";
import Logo from "../../Components/common/Logo";

const demoAccounts = [
  {
    label: "Student",
    email: "rahul@student.com",
    password: "Student@123",
  },
  {
    label: "Teacher",
    email: "arun@opennotes.com",
    password: "Teacher@123",
  },
  {
    label: "Admin",
    email: "admin@opennotes.com",
    password: "Admin@123",
  },
];

const destinationByRole = {
  student: "/student/dashboard",
  teacher: "/teacher/dashboard",
  admin: "/admin/dashboard",
};

function getSafeDestination(requestedPath, role) {
  const defaultPath = destinationByRole[role] || "/home";

  if (!requestedPath || !requestedPath.startsWith("/")) {
    return defaultPath;
  }

  // Never allow a login redirect to another role's dashboard.
  if (
    requestedPath.startsWith("/student/") &&
    role !== "student"
  ) {
    return defaultPath;
  }

  if (
    requestedPath.startsWith("/teacher/") &&
    role !== "teacher"
  ) {
    return defaultPath;
  }

  if (
    requestedPath.startsWith("/admin/") &&
    role !== "admin"
  ) {
    return defaultPath;
  }

  if (
    ["/login", "/signup", "/splash"].includes(requestedPath)
  ) {
    return defaultPath;
  }

  return requestedPath;
}

function Login() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const { login, isAuthenticated, role } = useAuth();

  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  if (isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <main className="login-redirect">
        <p>You are already signed in.</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() =>
            navigate(destinationByRole[role] || "/home", {
              replace: true,
            })
          }
        >
          Continue to OpenNotes
        </button>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!cleanEmail || !password) {
      setError("Please enter both your email and password.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const result = await login(cleanEmail, password);

      if (!result?.success) {
        setError(
          result?.message || "Unable to sign in. Please try again."
        );
        return;
      }

      const requestedPath = location.state?.from?.pathname;
      const destination = getSafeDestination(
        requestedPath,
        result.user?.role
      );

      navigate(destination, { replace: true });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Login failed:", error);
      setError("Something went wrong. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const fillDemoAccount = (account) => {
    setEmail(account.email);
    setPassword(account.password);
    setError("");
  };

  return (
    <main className="login-page">
      <section className="login-brand-panel">
        <Link
            to="/home"
            className="login-brand"
            aria-label="OpenNotes home"
            >
            <Logo className="login-logo" />
        </Link>

        <div className="login-brand-content">
          <span className="login-eyebrow">YOUR LEARNING SPACE</span>

          <h1>
            Learn smarter.
            <br />
            <span>Move forward.</span>
          </h1>

          <p>
            Your notes, previous-year questions, and study resources
            — together in one place.
          </p>

          <div className="login-feature">
            <span className="login-feature-icon">01</span>
            <div>
              <strong>Organised resources</strong>
              <p>Find study material by stream, semester, and subject.</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="login-feature">
            <span className="login-feature-icon">02</span>
            <div>
              <strong>Built for students</strong>
              <p>Spend less time searching and more time learning.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="login-brand-footer">
          Every semester, every subject, right at your fingertips.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="login-form-panel">
        <div className="login-form-wrapper">
          <Link to="/home" className="login-mobile-brand">
            <Logo className="login-logo" />
          </Link>

          <div className="login-heading">
            <span className="login-eyebrow">WELCOME BACK</span>
            <h2>Sign in to OpenNotes</h2>
            <p>Enter your account details to continue.</p>
          </div>

          <form className="login-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div className="login-field">
              <label htmlFor="login-email">Email address</label>
              <input
                id="login-email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="username"
                placeholder="you@example.com"
                value={email}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setEmail(event.target.value);
                  setError("");
                }}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="login-field">
              <label htmlFor="login-password">Password</label>

              <div className="login-password-wrap">
                <input
                  id="login-password"
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  autoComplete="current-password"
                  placeholder="Enter your password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    setPassword(event.target.value);
                    setError("");
                  }}
                  required
                />

                <button
                  className="login-password-toggle"
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setShowPassword((current) => !current)
                  }
                  aria-label={
                    showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"
                  }
                >
                  {showPassword ? "Hide" : "Show"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="login-error" role="alert">
                <span aria-hidden="true">!</span>
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              className="login-submit"
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
            >
              {loading ? "Signing in..." : "Sign in"}
              {!loading && <span aria-hidden="true">→</span>}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="login-divider">
            <span>DEMO ACCESS</span>
          </div>

          <p className="login-demo-help">
            Testing the frontend? Select a demo role to fill in its
            credentials.
          </p>

          <div className="login-demo-buttons">
            {demoAccounts.map((account) => (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={account.label}
                onClick={() => fillDemoAccount(account)}
              >
                {account.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <p className="login-register-link">
            New to OpenNotes? <Link to="/signup">Create an account</Link>
          </p>

          <p className="login-register-link">
            Want to explore first? <Link to="/home">Browse OpenNotes</Link>
          </p>

          <p className="login-security-note">
            Demo authentication only. Production accounts will use
            secure backend authentication.
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

export default Login;

========== Frontend\src\Pages\Auth\Signup.jsx ==========

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Eye, EyeOff, GraduationCap, BookOpen, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { registerMockUser } from "../../services/authService";
import "./Signup.css";
import Logo from "../../Components/common/Logo";

function Signup() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    password: "",
    confirmPassword: "",
    role: "student",
    acceptTerms: false,
  });

  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [showConfirmPassword, setShowConfirmPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const updateField = (event) => {
    const { name, value, type, checked } = event.target;

    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [name]: type === "checkbox" ? checked : value,
    }));

    setError("");
    setSuccess("");
  };

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    const name = form.name.trim();
    const email = form.email.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (name.length < 2) {
      setError("Please enter your full name.");
      return;
    }

    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
      setError("Please enter a valid email address.");
      return;
    }

    if (form.password.length < 8) {
      setError("Your password must contain at least 8 characters.");
      return;
    }

    if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(form.password) || !/\d/.test(form.password)) {
      setError("Use at least one letter and one number in your password.");
      return;
    }

    if (form.password !== form.confirmPassword) {
      setError("Your passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    if (!form.acceptTerms) {
      setError("Please accept the terms to continue.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const result = registerMockUser({
        name,
        email,
        password: form.password,
        role: form.role,
      });

      if (!result.success) {
        setError(result.message);
        return;
      }

      if (result.requiresApproval) {
        setSuccess(
          "Your teacher account has been submitted. An administrator must approve it before you can sign in."
        );
      } else {
        setSuccess("Your account has been created. You can now sign in.");
      }

      setTimeout(() => navigate("/login", { replace: true }), 1800);
    } catch {
      setError("We couldn't create your account. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="signup-page">
      <section className="signup-aside">
        <Link
            to="/home"
            className="signup-brand"
            aria-label="OpenNotes home"
            >
            <Logo className="signup-logo" />
        </Link>

        <div className="signup-aside-content">
          <span className="signup-eyebrow">JOIN THE COMMUNITY</span>
          <h1>
            Your next step
            <br />
            <span>starts here.</span>
          </h1>
          <p>
            Discover resources, organise your learning, and make every
            semester count.
          </p>

          <div className="signup-benefit">
            <CheckCircle2 size={19} />
            <span>Study resources organised in one place</span>
          </div>
          <div className="signup-benefit">
            <CheckCircle2 size={19} />
            <span>A profile that keeps your learning connected</span>
          </div>
          <div className="signup-benefit">
            <CheckCircle2 size={19} />
            <span>Different tools for students and teachers</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="signup-aside-footer">
          Every semester, every subject, right at your fingertips.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="signup-main">
        <div className="signup-card">
          <div className="signup-mobile-brand">
            <Logo className="signup-logo" />
          </div>


          <header className="signup-heading">
            <span className="signup-eyebrow">CREATE YOUR ACCOUNT</span>
            <h2>Join OpenNotes</h2>
            <p>Fill in your details to get started.</p>
          </header>

          <form className="signup-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div className="signup-field">
              <label htmlFor="signup-name">Full name</label>
              <input
                id="signup-name"
                name="name"
                type="text"
                autoComplete="name"
                placeholder="Enter your full name"
                value={form.name}
                onChange={updateField}
                maxLength={100}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="signup-field">
              <label htmlFor="signup-email">Email address</label>
              <input
                id="signup-email"
                name="email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                placeholder="you@example.com"
                value={form.email}
                onChange={updateField}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <fieldset className="signup-role-fieldset">
              <legend>I want to join as</legend>

              <div className="signup-role-options">
                <label
                  className={`signup-role-option ${
                    form.role === "student" ? "selected" : ""
                  }`}
                >
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    name="role"
                    value="student"
                    checked={form.role === "student"}
                    onChange={updateField}
                  />
                  <GraduationCap size={21} />
                  <span>
                    <strong>Student</strong>
                    <small>Learn and access resources</small>
                  </span>
                </label>

                <label
                  className={`signup-role-option ${
                    form.role === "teacher" ? "selected" : ""
                  }`}
                >
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    name="role"
                    value="teacher"
                    checked={form.role === "teacher"}
                    onChange={updateField}
                  />
                  <BookOpen size={21} />
                  <span>
                    <strong>Teacher</strong>
                    <small>Contribute learning materials</small>
                  </span>
                </label>
              </div>

              {form.role === "teacher" && (
                <p className="signup-approval-note">
                  Teacher accounts require administrator approval before
                  access is granted.
                </p>
              )}
            </fieldset>

            <div className="signup-field">
              <label htmlFor="signup-password">Password</label>
              <div className="signup-password-wrap">
                <input
                  id="signup-password"
                  name="password"
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  autoComplete="new-password"
                  placeholder="At least 8 characters"
                  value={form.password}
                  onChange={updateField}
                  minLength={8}
                  required
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="signup-password-toggle"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword((value) => !value)}
                  aria-label={showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
                </button>
              </div>
              <small className="signup-field-hint">
                Use at least 8 characters, including a letter and a number.
              </small>
            </div>

            <div className="signup-field">
              <label htmlFor="signup-confirm-password">Confirm password</label>
              <div className="signup-password-wrap">
                <input
                  id="signup-confirm-password"
                  name="confirmPassword"
                  type={showConfirmPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  autoComplete="new-password"
                  placeholder="Enter your password again"
                  value={form.confirmPassword}
                  onChange={updateField}
                  required
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="signup-password-toggle"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setShowConfirmPassword((value) => !value)
                  }
                  aria-label={
                    showConfirmPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"
                  }
                >
                  {showConfirmPassword ? (
                    <EyeOff size={18} />
                  ) : (
                    <Eye size={18} />
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <label className="signup-terms">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                name="acceptTerms"
                checked={form.acceptTerms}
                onChange={updateField}
              />
              <span>
                I agree to the terms of use and privacy policy.
              </span>
            </label>

            {error && (
              <p className="signup-message signup-error" role="alert">
                {error}
              </p>
            )}

            {success && (
              <p className="signup-message signup-success" role="status">
                {success}
              </p>
            )}

            <button
              className="signup-submit"
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || Boolean(success)}
            >
              {loading ? "Creating account..." : "Create account"}
              {!loading && <span aria-hidden="true">→</span>}
            </button>
          </form>

          <p className="signup-login-link">
            Already have an account? <Link to="/login">Sign in</Link>
          </p>

          <p className="signup-security-note">
            Account creation is currently running in demo mode. Secure
            production registration will be handled by the backend.
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

export default Signup;

========== Frontend\src\Pages\BrowseNotes\BrowseNotes.jsx ==========
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  BookOpen,
  Building2,
  BriefcaseBusiness,
  ChevronRight,
  FileText,
  FlaskConical,
  GraduationCap,
  Scale,
  Search
} from "lucide-react";

import notesData from "../../data/notesData";
import "./BrowseNotes.css";

function BrowseNotes() {
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  const [selectedStream, setSelectedStream] = useState(null);
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState(null);
  const [selectedSemester, setSelectedSemester] = useState(null);
  const [selectedSubject, setSelectedSubject] = useState(null);
  const [selectedModule, setSelectedModule] = useState(null);

  const streams = [
    {
      id: "engineering",
      title: "Engineering",
      description: "Engineering notes and study materials",
      icon: GraduationCap
    },
    {
      id: "law",
      title: "Law",
      description: "Law courses and legal study materials",
      icon: Scale
    },
    {
      id: "architecture",
      title: "Architecture",
      description: "Architecture and design resources",
      icon: Building2
    },
    {
      id: "management-research",
      title: "Management & Research",
      description: "Management and research materials",
      icon: BriefcaseBusiness
    }
  ];

  const recentNotes = [
    {
      title: "Data Structures",
      stream: "Engineering",
      semester: "1st Semester"
    },
    {
      title: "Arrays and Strings",
      stream: "Engineering",
      semester: "1st Semester"
    },
    {
      title: "DBMS",
      stream: "Engineering",
      semester: "2nd Semester"
    }
  ];

  const filteredNotes = useMemo(() => {
    const query = searchTerm.toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!query) return recentNotes;

    return recentNotes.filter((note) =>
      `${note.title} ${note.stream} ${note.semester}`
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(query)
    );
  }, [searchTerm]);

  const currentStreamData = selectedStream
    ? notesData[selectedStream]
    : null;

  const currentYearData =
    currentStreamData && selectedYear
      ? currentStreamData[selectedYear]
      : null;

  const currentSemesterData =
    currentYearData && selectedSemester
      ? currentYearData.semesters[selectedSemester]
      : null;

  const selectedSubjectData =
    currentSemesterData && selectedSubject
      ? currentSemesterData.subjects.find(
          (subject) => subject.id === selectedSubject
        )
      : null;

  const handleStreamClick = (streamId) => {
    setSelectedStream(streamId);
    setSelectedYear(null);
    setSelectedSemester(null);
    setSelectedSubject(null);
    setSelectedModule(null);
  };

  const handleYearClick = (yearId) => {
    setSelectedYear(yearId);
    setSelectedSemester(null);
    setSelectedSubject(null);
    setSelectedModule(null);
  };

  const handleSemesterClick = (semesterId) => {
    setSelectedSemester(semesterId);
    setSelectedSubject(null);
    setSelectedModule(null);
  };

  const handleSubjectClick = (subjectId) => {
    setSelectedSubject(subjectId);
    setSelectedModule(null);
  };

  const handleModuleClick = (module) => {
    setSelectedModule(module);
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    if (selectedModule) {
      setSelectedModule(null);
    } else if (selectedSubject) {
      setSelectedSubject(null);
    } else if (selectedSemester) {
      setSelectedSemester(null);
    } else if (selectedYear) {
      setSelectedYear(null);
    } else if (selectedStream) {
      setSelectedStream(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section className="browse-notes-page">

      {/* HEADER */}
      <header className="browse-notes-header">
        <div className="section-label">BROWSE NOTES</div>

        <h1>
          Find the notes
          <span> you need.</span>
        </h1>

        <p className="browse-notes-description">
          Explore notes organized by stream, year, semester, subject and
          module.
        </p>

        <div className="browse-notes-header-icon">
          <BookOpen size={30} />
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* SEARCH */}
      {!selectedModule && (
        <div className="browse-search-wrapper">
          <Search className="browse-search-icon" size={21} />

          <input
            type="text"
            className="browse-search-input"
            placeholder="Search notes, subjects or streams..."
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(event) => setSearchTerm(event.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* RECENT NOTES */}
      {!selectedStream && (
        <section className="browse-section">
          <div className="browse-section-heading">
            <div>
              <div className="section-label">RECENT NOTES</div>
              <h2>
                Recently added
                <span className="note-count">
                  {filteredNotes.length}
                </span>
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>

          {filteredNotes.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="recent-notes-grid">
              {filteredNotes.map((note) => (
                <div className="recent-note-card" key={note.title}>
                  <div className="note-card-icon">
                    <FileText size={22} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="note-card-content">
                    <h3 className="note-subject">
                      {note.title}
                    </h3>

                    <p className="note-meta">
                      {note.stream} • {note.semester}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <ChevronRight
                    className="note-card-arrow"
                    size={20}
                  />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="no-notes">No notes found.</p>
          )}
        </section>
      )}

      {/* STREAM SELECTION */}
      {!selectedStream && (
        <section className="browse-section">
          <div className="browse-section-heading">
            <div>
              <div className="section-label">EXPLORE</div>
              <h2>Choose your stream</h2>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="stream-grid">
            {streams.map((stream) => {
              const Icon = stream.icon;

              return (
                <button
                  className="stream-card"
                  key={stream.id}
                  onClick={() => handleStreamClick(stream.id)}
                >
                  <div className="stream-card-icon">
                    <Icon size={25} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="stream-card-content">
                    <h3>{stream.title}</h3>
                    <p>{stream.description}</p>
                  </div>

                  <ChevronRight
                    className="stream-card-arrow"
                    size={21}
                  />
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* ENGINEERING HIERARCHY */}
      {selectedStream === "engineering" && (
        <>
          <button className="browse-back-button" onClick={handleBack}>
            <ArrowLeft size={18} />
            Back
          </button>

          {!selectedYear && (
            <section className="hierarchy-section">
              <div className="hierarchy-heading">
                <div className="section-label">ENGINEERING</div>
                <h2>Choose your year</h2>
              </div>

              <div className="year-grid">
                {[
                  {
                    id: "1st-year",
                    number: "01",
                    title: "1st Year",
                    subtitle: "1st & 2nd Semester"
                  },
                  {
                    id: "2nd-year",
                    number: "02",
                    title: "2nd Year",
                    subtitle: "3rd & 4th Semester"
                  },
                  {
                    id: "3rd-year",
                    number: "03",
                    title: "3rd Year",
                    subtitle: "5th & 6th Semester"
                  },
                  {
                    id: "4th-year",
                    number: "04",
                    title: "4th Year",
                    subtitle: "7th & 8th Semester"
                  }
                ].map((year) => (
                  <button
                    className="year-card"
                    key={year.id}
                    onClick={() => handleYearClick(year.id)}
                  >
                    <span className="year-number">
                      {year.number}
                    </span>

                    <span className="year-card-content">
                      <strong>{year.title}</strong>
                      <small>{year.subtitle}</small>
                    </span>

                    <ChevronRight size={20} />
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}

          {selectedYear && !selectedSemester && (
            <section className="hierarchy-section">
              <div className="hierarchy-heading">
                <div className="section-label">
                  {currentYearData.title}
                </div>

                <h2>Choose your semester</h2>
              </div>

              <div className="semester-grid">
                {Object.entries(currentYearData.semesters).map(
                  ([semesterId, semester]) => (
                    <button
                      className="semester-card"
                      key={semesterId}
                      onClick={() =>
                        handleSemesterClick(semesterId)
                      }
                    >
                      <div className="semester-card-icon">
                        <BookOpen size={24} />
                      </div>

                      <div className="semester-card-content">
                        <strong>{semester.title}</strong>
                        <small>
                          {semester.subjects.length} subjects
                        </small>
                      </div>

                      <ChevronRight size={20} />
                    </button>
                  )
                )}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}

          {selectedSemester && !selectedSubject && (
            <section className="hierarchy-section">
              <div className="hierarchy-heading">
                <div className="section-label">
                  {currentSemesterData.title}
                </div>

                <h2>Choose your subject</h2>
              </div>

              <div className="program-grid">
                {currentSemesterData.subjects.map((subject) => (
                  <button
                    className="program-card"
                    key={subject.id}
                    onClick={() =>
                      handleSubjectClick(subject.id)
                    }
                  >
                    <div className="program-card-icon">
                      <FlaskConical size={23} />
                    </div>

                    <div className="program-card-content">
                      <strong>{subject.name}</strong>
                      <small>{subject.code}</small>
                    </div>

                    <ChevronRight size={20} />
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}

          {selectedSubject && !selectedModule && (
            <section className="hierarchy-section">
              <div className="hierarchy-heading">
                <div className="section-label">
                  {selectedSubjectData.name}
                </div>

                <h2>Choose your module</h2>
              </div>

              <div className="module-grid">
                {selectedSubjectData.modules.map((module, index) => (
                  <button
                    className="module-card"
                    key={module.id}
                    onClick={() => handleModuleClick(module)}
                  >
                    <span className="module-number">
                      {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
                    </span>

                    <span className="module-card-content">
                      <strong>{module.name}</strong>

                      <small>
                        {module.teacherNotes.length +
                          module.studentNotes.length}{" "}
                        notes available
                      </small>
                    </span>

                    <ChevronRight size={20} />
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}

          {selectedModule && (
            <section className="module-notes-section">
              <div className="hierarchy-heading">
                <div className="section-label">
                  {selectedSubjectData.name}
                </div>

                <h2>{selectedModule.name}</h2>
              </div>

              {/* TEACHER NOTES */}
              <div className="notes-category">
                <div className="notes-category-heading">
                  <div>
                    <span className="category-label">
                      TEACHER NOTES
                    </span>

                    <h3>Teacher Notes</h3>
                  </div>

                  <span className="category-count">
                    {selectedModule.teacherNotes.length}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {selectedModule.teacherNotes.length > 0 ? (
                  <div className="module-notes-grid">
                    {selectedModule.teacherNotes.map((note) => (
                      <article
                        className="module-note-card"
                        key={note.id}
                      >
                        <div className="module-note-icon">
                          <FileText size={23} />
                        </div>

                        <div className="module-note-content">
                          <h4>{note.title}</h4>

                          <p>{note.description}</p>

                          <div className="module-note-meta">
                            <span>{note.fileType}</span>
                            <span>{note.pages} pages</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <a
                          href={note.fileUrl}
                          className="note-open-button"
                        >
                          Open
                        </a>
                      </article>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="empty-notes">
                    No teacher notes available yet.
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* STUDENT NOTES */}
              <div className="notes-category">
                <div className="notes-category-heading">
                  <div>
                    <span className="category-label">
                      STUDENT NOTES
                    </span>

                    <h3>Student Notes</h3>
                  </div>

                  <span className="category-count">
                    {selectedModule.studentNotes.length}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {selectedModule.studentNotes.length > 0 ? (
                  <div className="module-notes-grid">
                    {selectedModule.studentNotes.map((note) => (
                      <article
                        className="module-note-card"
                        key={note.id}
                      >
                        <div className="module-note-icon">
                          <FileText size={23} />
                        </div>

                        <div className="module-note-content">
                          <h4>{note.title}</h4>

                          <p>{note.description}</p>

                          <div className="module-note-meta">
                            <span>{note.fileType}</span>
                            <span>{note.pages} pages</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <a
                          href={note.fileUrl}
                          className="note-open-button"
                        >
                          Open
                        </a>
                      </article>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="empty-notes">
                    No student notes available yet.
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {/* OTHER STREAMS */}
      {selectedStream &&
        selectedStream !== "engineering" && (
          <section className="hierarchy-section">
            <button
              className="browse-back-button"
              onClick={handleBack}
            >
              <ArrowLeft size={18} />
              Back
            </button>

            <div className="hierarchy-heading">
              <div className="section-label">
                {streams.find(
                  (stream) => stream.id === selectedStream
                )?.title}
              </div>

              <h2>Coming soon</h2>
            </div>

            <div className="empty-notes">
              Notes for this stream will be added here.
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
    </section>
  );
}

export default BrowseNotes;

    margin-bottom: 30px;
  }

  .browse-pyqs-header-content {
    margin-bottom: 22px;
  }

  .browse-pyqs-header-content h1 {
    font-size: 25px;
    letter-spacing: -0.4px;
  }

  .browse-pyqs-description {
    font-size: 13px;
  }

  .browse-pyqs-header-icon {
    width: 48px;
    height: 48px;
    border-radius: 12px;
  }

  .browse-pyqs-section {
    margin-bottom: 34px;
  }

  .browse-pyqs-section-heading {
    align-items: flex-start;
    margin-bottom: 15px;
  }

  .browse-pyqs-section-heading h2 {
    font-size: 18px;
  }

  .pyq-stream-grid,
  .pyq-semester-grid,
  .pyq-paper-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .pyq-subject-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .pyq-year-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .pyq-stream-card {
    min-height: 100px;
    padding: 17px;
  }

  .pyq-year-card {
    min-height: 140px;
    padding: 18px;
  }

  .pyq-paper-card {
    padding: 17px;
  }

  .pyq-exam-heading {
    align-items: flex-start;
  }
}
