========== Frontend\src\Pages\BrowsePYQs\BrowsePYQs.jsx ==========
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  BookOpen,
  Building2,
  BriefcaseBusiness,
  ChevronRight,
  FileQuestion,
  FileText,
  GraduationCap,
  Scale,
  Search,
} from "lucide-react";

import pyqData from "../../data/pyqdata";
import "./BrowsePYQs.css";

const UPLOADED_PYQS_KEY =
  "openNotes-uploaded-pyqs";

const DB_NAME = "openNotesDB";
const DB_VERSION = 1;
const FILE_STORE_NAME = "pyqFiles";

/* =========================================================
   HELPERS
========================================================= */

function getUploadedPYQs() {
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(
      UPLOADED_PYQS_KEY
    );

    if (!stored) {
      return [];
    }

    const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);

    return Array.isArray(parsed)
      ? parsed
      : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function openFileDatabase() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (!window.indexedDB) {
      reject(
        new Error(
          "IndexedDB is not supported by this browser."
        )
      );

      return;
    }

    const request = window.indexedDB.open(
      DB_NAME,
      DB_VERSION
    );

    request.onerror = () => {
      reject(
        new Error(
          "Unable to open local file storage."
        )
      );
    };

    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      const database = request.result;

      if (
        !database.objectStoreNames.contains(
          FILE_STORE_NAME
        )
      ) {
        database.createObjectStore(
          FILE_STORE_NAME,
          {
            keyPath: "id",
          }
        );
      }
    };

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      resolve(request.result);
    };
  });
}

function getFileFromDatabase(fileId) {
  return openFileDatabase().then(
    (database) =>
      new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const transaction =
          database.transaction(
            FILE_STORE_NAME,
            "readonly"
          );

        const store =
          transaction.objectStore(
            FILE_STORE_NAME
          );

        const request =
          store.get(fileId);

        request.onsuccess = () => {
          database.close();

          resolve(
            request.result?.file || null
          );
        };

        request.onerror = () => {
          database.close();

          reject(
            new Error(
              "Unable to retrieve the PDF."
            )
          );
        };
      })
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   COMPONENT
========================================================= */

function BrowsePYQs() {
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] =
    useState("");

  const [selectedStream, setSelectedStream] =
    useState(null);

  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] =
    useState(null);

  const [selectedSemester, setSelectedSemester] =
    useState(null);

  const [selectedSubject, setSelectedSubject] =
    useState(null);

  const [uploadedPYQs, setUploadedPYQs] =
    useState(getUploadedPYQs);

  const [openingFileId, setOpeningFileId] =
    useState(null);

  /* =======================================================
     STREAMS
  ======================================================= */

  const streams = [
    {
      id: "engineering",
      name: "Engineering",
      description:
        "Engineering question papers",
      icon: Building2,
    },
    {
      id: "law",
      name: "Law",
      description:
        "Law examination papers",
      icon: Scale,
    },
    {
      id: "architecture",
      name: "Architecture",
      description:
        "Architecture question papers",
      icon: BookOpen,
    },
    {
      id: "management",
      name: "Management",
      description:
        "Management examination papers",
      icon: BriefcaseBusiness,
    },
    {
      id: "research",
      name: "Research",
      description:
        "Research examination papers",
      icon: GraduationCap,
    },
  ];

  /* =======================================================
     LISTEN FOR NEW UPLOADS
  ======================================================= */

  useEffect(() => {
    const refreshUploadedPYQs =
      () => {
        setUploadedPYQs(
          getUploadedPYQs()
        );
      };

    window.addEventListener(
      "openNotesPYQUpdated",
      refreshUploadedPYQs
    );

    window.addEventListener(
      "storage",
      refreshUploadedPYQs
    );

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(
        "openNotesPYQUpdated",
        refreshUploadedPYQs
      );

      window.removeEventListener(
        "storage",
        refreshUploadedPYQs
      );
    };
  }, []);

  /* =======================================================
     CURRENT DATA
  ======================================================= */

  const currentStreamData =
    selectedStream
      ? pyqData[selectedStream]
      : null;

  const currentYearData =
    currentStreamData && selectedYear
      ? currentStreamData[selectedYear]
      : null;

  const currentSemesterData =
    currentYearData && selectedSemester
      ? currentYearData.semesters[
          selectedSemester
        ]
      : null;

  const selectedSubjectData =
    currentSemesterData &&
    selectedSubject
      ? currentSemesterData.subjects.find(
          (subject) =>
            subject.id ===
            selectedSubject
        )
      : null;

  /* =======================================================
     MERGE STATIC + UPLOADED PYQS
  ======================================================= */

  const combinedPYQs = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedSubjectData) {
      return [];
    }

    const staticPYQs =
      Array.isArray(
        selectedSubjectData.pyqs
      )
        ? selectedSubjectData.pyqs
        : [];

    const matchingUploadedPYQs =
      uploadedPYQs.filter((pyq) => {
        return (
          pyq.stream ===
            selectedStream &&
          pyq.yearId ===
            selectedYear &&
          pyq.semester ===
            selectedSemester &&
          pyq.subjectId ===
            selectedSubject
        );
      });

    return [
      ...staticPYQs,
      ...matchingUploadedPYQs,
    ];
  }, [
    selectedSubjectData,
    uploadedPYQs,
    selectedStream,
    selectedYear,
    selectedSemester,
    selectedSubject,
  ]);

  /* =======================================================
     SEARCH
  ======================================================= */

  const filteredPYQs = useMemo(() => {
    const query =
      searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!query) {
      return combinedPYQs;
    }

    return combinedPYQs.filter(
      (pyq) => {
        const searchableText = [
          pyq.title,
          pyq.year,
          pyq.examType,
          pyq.fileType,
          pyq.description,
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join(" ")
          .toLowerCase();

        return searchableText.includes(
          query
        );
      }
    );
  }, [
    combinedPYQs,
    searchTerm,
  ]);

  /* =======================================================
     GROUP BY CIE / SEE
  ======================================================= */

  const groupedPYQs = useMemo(() => {
    const cie = {};
    const see = {};

    filteredPYQs.forEach((pyq) => {
      const examType =
        String(
          pyq.examType || ""
        ).toUpperCase();

      const year = String(
        pyq.year || "Unknown"
      );

      const target =
        examType === "CIE"
          ? cie
          : examType === "SEE"
          ? see
          : null;

      if (!target) {
        return;
      }

      if (!target[year]) {
        target[year] = [];
      }

      target[year].push(pyq);
    });

    const sortYears = (
      yearObject
    ) => {
      return Object.entries(
        yearObject
      ).sort(
        ([yearA], [yearB]) =>
          Number(yearB) -
          Number(yearA)
      );
    };

    return {
      cie: sortYears(cie),
      see: sortYears(see),
    };
  }, [filteredPYQs]);

  /* =======================================================
     NAVIGATION
  ======================================================= */

  const handleStreamClick = (
    streamId
  ) => {
    setSelectedStream(streamId);
    setSelectedYear(null);
    setSelectedSemester(null);
    setSelectedSubject(null);
    setSearchTerm("");
  };

  const handleYearClick = (
    yearId
  ) => {
    setSelectedYear(yearId);
    setSelectedSemester(null);
    setSelectedSubject(null);
    setSearchTerm("");
  };

  const handleSemesterClick = (
    semesterId
  ) => {
    setSelectedSemester(
      semesterId
    );
    setSelectedSubject(null);
    setSearchTerm("");
  };

  const handleSubjectClick = (
    subjectId
  ) => {
    setSelectedSubject(
      subjectId
    );
    setSearchTerm("");
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    setSearchTerm("");

    if (selectedSubject) {
      setSelectedSubject(null);
      return;
    }

    if (selectedSemester) {
      setSelectedSemester(null);
      return;
    }

    if (selectedYear) {
      setSelectedYear(null);
      return;
    }

    if (selectedStream) {
      setSelectedStream(null);
    }
  };

  /* =======================================================
     OPEN PAPER
  ======================================================= */

  const handleOpenPaper = async (
    pyq
  ) => {
    /*
      Existing static PYQ
      -------------------
      If fileUrl exists, open it directly.
    */

    if (pyq.fileUrl) {
      window.open(
        pyq.fileUrl,
        "_blank",
        "noopener,noreferrer"
      );

      return;
    }

    /*
      Uploaded PYQ
      ------------
      Retrieve PDF from IndexedDB.
    */

    const fileId =
      pyq.files?.[0]?.id ||
      pyq.storageId;

    if (!fileId) {
      window.alert(
        "This question paper does not have a valid file."
      );

      return;
    }

    let newWindow = null;

    try {
      /*
        Open tab immediately so popup
        blockers don't stop it.
      */

      newWindow = window.open(
        "",
        "_blank"
      );

      if (newWindow) {
        newWindow.document.title =
          "OpenNotes - Question Paper";

        newWindow.document.body.innerHTML =
          `
            <div style="
              min-height:100vh;
              display:flex;
              align-items:center;
              justify-content:center;
              background:#050505;
              color:#f5b900;
              font-family:Arial,sans-serif;
              font-size:16px;
            ">
              Opening question paper...
            </div>
          `;
      }

      setOpeningFileId(pyq.id);

      const file =
        await getFileFromDatabase(
          fileId
        );

      if (!file) {
        throw new Error(
          "PDF file could not be found."
        );
      }

      const fileUrl =
        URL.createObjectURL(file);

      if (newWindow) {
        newWindow.location.href =
          fileUrl;
      } else {
        window.open(
          fileUrl,
          "_blank"
        );
      }

      /*
        Give the browser enough time to
        start loading the PDF before
        releasing the object URL.
      */

      setTimeout(() => {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(
          fileUrl
        );
      }, 60000);
    } catch (error) {
      if (newWindow) {
        newWindow.close();
      }

      window.alert(
        error?.message ||
          "Unable to open this PDF."
      );
    } finally {
      setOpeningFileId(null);
    }
  };

  /* =======================================================
     PAPER CARD
  ======================================================= */

  const renderPaperCard = (
    pyq
  ) => {
    const isUploaded =
      pyq.source ===
      "user-upload";

    const isOpening =
      openingFileId === pyq.id;

    return (
      <article
        className="pyq-paper-card"
        key={pyq.id}
      >
        <div className="pyq-paper-icon">
          <FileText
            size={24}
            strokeWidth={1.8}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="pyq-paper-content">
          <span className="pyq-paper-year">
            {pyq.year}
          </span>

          <h3>
            {pyq.title}
          </h3>

          <div className="pyq-paper-meta">
            <span>
              {pyq.examType}
            </span>

            <span className="pyq-meta-dot">
              •
            </span>

            <span>
              {pyq.fileType ||
                "PDF"}
            </span>

            {pyq.pages && (
              <>
                <span className="pyq-meta-dot">
                  •
                </span>

                <span>
                  {pyq.pages} pages
                </span>
              </>
            )}

            {isUploaded && (
              <>
                <span className="pyq-meta-dot">
                  •
                </span>

                <span className="pyq-uploaded-label">
                  Uploaded
                </span>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="pyq-open-button"
          onClick={() =>
            handleOpenPaper(pyq)
          }
          disabled={isOpening}
        >
          {isOpening
            ? "Opening..."
            : "Open"}
        </button>
      </article>
    );
  };

  /* =======================================================
     YEAR GROUP
  ======================================================= */

  const renderExamGroups = (
    groups,
    emptyMessage
  ) => {
    if (!groups.length) {
      return (
        <div className="pyq-empty-small">
          <FileQuestion
            size={25}
            strokeWidth={1.6}
          />

          <span>
            {emptyMessage}
          </span>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return (
      <div className="pyq-year-groups">
        {groups.map(
          ([
            year,
            papers,
          ]) => (
            <div
              className="pyq-year-group"
              key={year}
            >
              <div className="pyq-year-group-heading">
                <div>
                  <span className="section-label">
                    EXAMINATION YEAR
                  </span>

                  <h3>
                    {year}
                  </h3>
                </div>

                <span className="pyq-year-count">
                  {papers.length}{" "}
                  {papers.length ===
                  1
                    ? "paper"
                    : "papers"}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="pyq-paper-grid">
                {papers.map(
                  renderPaperCard
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  /* =======================================================
     RENDER
  ======================================================= */

  return (
    <section className="browse-pyqs-page">
      {/* =================================================
          HEADER
      ================================================= */}

      <header className="browse-pyqs-header">
        <div className="browse-pyqs-header-content">
          <div className="browse-pyqs-header-text">
            <span className="section-label">
              OPENNOTES PYQ LIBRARY
            </span>

            <h1>
              Browse previous
              year papers.
            </h1>

            <p className="browse-pyqs-description">
              Find question papers organized
              by stream, year, semester,
              subject and examination type.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="browse-pyqs-header-icon">
            <FileQuestion
              size={31}
              strokeWidth={1.8}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* =================================================
          SEARCH
      ================================================= */}

      {!selectedSubject && (
        <div className="browse-pyqs-search-wrapper">
          <Search
            size={21}
            className="browse-pyqs-search-icon"
          />

          <input
            type="search"
            className="browse-pyqs-search-input"
            placeholder="Search question papers..."
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setSearchTerm(
                event.target.value
              )
            }
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =================================================
          BACK
      ================================================= */}

      {selectedStream && (
        <button
          type="button"
          className="browse-pyqs-back-button"
          onClick={handleBack}
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={17} />
          Back
        </button>
      )}

      {/* =================================================
          STREAMS
      ================================================= */}

      {!selectedStream && (
        <section className="browse-pyqs-section">
          <div className="browse-pyqs-section-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="section-label">
                EXPLORE
              </span>

              <h2>
                Browse by stream
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pyq-stream-grid">
            {streams.map(
              (stream) => {
                const Icon =
                  stream.icon;

                return (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="pyq-stream-card"
                    key={stream.id}
                    onClick={() =>
                      handleStreamClick(
                        stream.id
                      )
                    }
                  >
                    <div className="pyq-stream-card-icon">
                      <Icon
                        size={27}
                        strokeWidth={1.8}
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="pyq-stream-card-content">
                      <h3>
                        {stream.name}
                      </h3>

                      <p>
                        {
                          stream.description
                        }
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <ChevronRight
                      size={20}
                    />
                  </button>
                );
              }
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* =================================================
          YEAR
      ================================================= */}

      {selectedStream &&
        !selectedYear &&
        currentStreamData && (
          <section className="browse-pyqs-section">
            <div className="browse-pyqs-section-heading">
              <div>
                <span className="section-label">
                  {
                    streams.find(
                      (stream) =>
                        stream.id ===
                        selectedStream
                    )?.name
                  }
                </span>

                <h2>
                  Choose your year
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="pyq-year-grid">
              {Object.entries(
                currentStreamData
              ).map(
                ([
                  yearId,
                  yearData,
                ]) => (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="pyq-year-card"
                    key={yearId}
                    onClick={() =>
                      handleYearClick(
                        yearId
                      )
                    }
                  >
                    <span className="pyq-year-number">
                      {yearData.title.replace(
                        " Year",
                        ""
                      )}
                    </span>

                    <div className="pyq-year-card-content">
                      <h3>
                        {
                          yearData.title
                        }
                      </h3>

                      <p>
                        View semesters
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <ChevronRight
                      size={19}
                    />
                  </button>
                )
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

      {/* =================================================
          SEMESTERS
      ================================================= */}

      {selectedYear &&
        !selectedSemester &&
        currentYearData && (
          <section className="browse-pyqs-section">
            <div className="browse-pyqs-section-heading">
              <div>
                <span className="section-label">
                  {
                    currentYearData.title
                  }
                </span>

                <h2>
                  Choose semester
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="pyq-semester-grid">
              {Object.entries(
                currentYearData.semesters
              ).map(
                ([
                  semesterId,
                  semesterData,
                ]) => (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="pyq-semester-card"
                    key={semesterId}
                    onClick={() =>
                      handleSemesterClick(
                        semesterId
                      )
                    }
                  >
                    <div className="pyq-semester-card-icon">
                      <GraduationCap
                        size={24}
                        strokeWidth={1.8}
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="pyq-semester-card-content">
                      <h3>
                        {
                          semesterData.title
                        }
                      </h3>

                      <p>
                        {
                          semesterData
                            .subjects
                            .length
                        }{" "}
                        subjects
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <ChevronRight
                      size={19}
                    />
                  </button>
                )
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

      {/* =================================================
          SUBJECTS
      ================================================= */}

      {selectedSemester &&
        !selectedSubject &&
        currentSemesterData && (
          <section className="browse-pyqs-section">
            <div className="browse-pyqs-section-heading">
              <div>
                <span className="section-label">
                  {
                    currentSemesterData.title
                  }
                </span>

                <h2>
                  Choose subject
                </h2>
              </div>

              <span className="pyq-count">
                {
                  currentSemesterData
                    .subjects.length
                }{" "}
                subjects
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="pyq-subject-grid">
              {currentSemesterData.subjects.map(
                (subject) => (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="pyq-subject-card"
                    key={subject.id}
                    onClick={() =>
                      handleSubjectClick(
                        subject.id
                      )
                    }
                  >
                    <div className="pyq-subject-card-icon">
                      <BookOpen
                        size={23}
                        strokeWidth={1.8}
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="pyq-subject-card-content">
                      {subject.code && (
                        <span className="pyq-subject-code">
                          {
                            subject.code
                          }
                        </span>
                      )}

                      <h3>
                        {
                          subject.name
                        }
                      </h3>

                      <p>
                        {
                          subject.pyqs
                            ?.length ||
                          0
                        }{" "}
                        papers
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <ChevronRight
                      size={19}
                    />
                  </button>
                )
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

      {/* =================================================
          PAPERS
      ================================================= */}

      {selectedSubjectData && (
        <section className="browse-pyqs-section">
          <div className="browse-pyqs-section-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="section-label">
                PREVIOUS YEAR PAPERS
              </span>

              <h2>
                {
                  selectedSubjectData.name
                }
              </h2>
            </div>

            <span className="pyq-count">
              {filteredPYQs.length}{" "}
              {filteredPYQs.length ===
              1
                ? "paper"
                : "papers"}
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* CIE */}

          <div className="pyq-exam-section">
            <div className="pyq-exam-heading">
              <div className="pyq-exam-title-group">
                <span className="pyq-exam-badge">
                  CIE
                </span>

                <div>
                  <h3>
                    Continuous Internal
                    Evaluation
                  </h3>

                  <p>
                    Internal examination
                    papers
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <span className="pyq-exam-count">
                {groupedPYQs.cie.reduce(
                  (
                    total,
                    [, papers]
                  ) =>
                    total +
                    papers.length,
                  0
                )}{" "}
                papers
              </span>
            </div>

            {renderExamGroups(
              groupedPYQs.cie,
              "No CIE papers available."
            )}
          </div>

          {/* SEE */}

          <div className="pyq-exam-section">
            <div className="pyq-exam-heading">
              <div className="pyq-exam-title-group">
                <span className="pyq-exam-badge">
                  SEE
                </span>

                <div>
                  <h3>
                    Semester End
                    Examination
                  </h3>

                  <p>
                    Semester-end
                    examination papers
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <span className="pyq-exam-count">
                {groupedPYQs.see.reduce(
                  (
                    total,
                    [, papers]
                  ) =>
                    total +
                    papers.length,
                  0
                )}{" "}
                papers
              </span>
            </div>

            {renderExamGroups(
              groupedPYQs.see,
              "No SEE papers available."
            )}
          </div>

          {/* NO RESULTS */}

          {filteredPYQs.length ===
            0 && (
            <div className="pyq-empty-state">
              <FileQuestion
                size={30}
                strokeWidth={1.6}
              />

              <h3>
                No question papers
                found
              </h3>

              <p>
                There are currently no
                papers available for
                this subject.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      )}

      {/* =================================================
          UNSUPPORTED STREAM
      ================================================= */}

      {selectedStream &&
        !currentStreamData && (
          <div className="pyq-empty-state">
            <FileQuestion
              size={30}
              strokeWidth={1.6}
            />

            <h3>
              Question papers
              coming soon
            </h3>

            <p>
              PYQs for this stream
              will be available soon.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
    </section>
  );
}

export default BrowsePYQs;

========== Frontend\src\Pages\Home\Home.jsx ==========
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  BookOpen,
  Search,
  Sparkles,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import books from "../../data/books.js";
import BookCard from "../../Components/common/BookCard.jsx";

import "./Home.css";

function Home() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  const [selectedDepartment, setSelectedDepartment] =
    useState("All");

  const departments = [
    "All",
    "Engineering",
    "Law",
    "Architecture",
    "Management & Research",
  ];

  const filteredBooks = useMemo(() => {
    const search = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    return books.filter((book) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        book.title.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
        book.author.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
        book.category.toLowerCase().includes(search);

      const matchesDepartment =
        selectedDepartment === "All" ||
        book.department === selectedDepartment;

      return matchesSearch && matchesDepartment;
    });
  }, [searchTerm, selectedDepartment]);

  const handleBrowseNotes = () => {
    navigate("/browse-notes");
  };

  return (
    <div className="home-page">

      {/* =================================
          HERO SECTION
      ================================= */}

      <section className="home-hero">

        <div className="hero-content">

          <div className="hero-badge">
            <Sparkles size={16} />

            <span>
              YOUR ACADEMIC KNOWLEDGE HUB
            </span>
          </div>

          <h1>
            Every semester,
            <span> every subject.</span>
          </h1>

          <p className="hero-description">
            Discover books, notes and academic resources
            from students, teachers and authors across
            different streams.
          </p>

          {/* SEARCH */}

          <div className="home-search">

            <Search size={21} />

            <input
              type="search"
              value={searchTerm}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setSearchTerm(event.target.value)
              }
              placeholder="Search books, subjects or authors..."
              aria-label="Search books, subjects or authors"
            />

            {searchTerm && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="clear-search"
                onClick={() => setSearchTerm("")}
              >
                Clear
              </button>
            )}

          </div>

          <div className="hero-actions">

            <button
              type="button"
              className="primary-button"
              onClick={handleBrowseNotes}
            >
              Browse Notes

              <ArrowRight size={18} />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="secondary-button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/browse-pyqs")}
            >
              Browse PYQs
            </button>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="hero-decoration">

          <div className="hero-circle hero-circle-one" />
          <div className="hero-circle hero-circle-two" />

          <div className="hero-book-icon">
            <BookOpen size={90} />
          </div>

        </div>

      </section>


      {/* =================================
          STREAM FILTER
      ================================= */}

      <section className="home-section">

        <div className="section-heading">

          <div>
            <p className="section-label">
              EXPLORE
            </p>

            <h2>
              Browse by stream
            </h2>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="view-all-button"
            onClick={handleBrowseNotes}
          >
            View all
            <ArrowRight size={17} />
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="department-list">

          {departments.map((department) => (
            <button
              key={department}
              type="button"
              className={`department-button ${
                selectedDepartment === department
                  ? "department-button-active"
                  : ""
              }`}
              onClick={() =>
                setSelectedDepartment(department)
              }
            >
              {department}
            </button>
          ))}

        </div>

      </section>


      {/* =================================
          BOOKS
      ================================= */}

      <section className="home-section books-section">

        <div className="section-heading">

          <div>
            <p className="section-label">
              OPENNOTES LIBRARY
            </p>

            <h2>
              Featured books
            </h2>
          </div>

          <span className="book-count">
            {filteredBooks.length}{" "}
            {filteredBooks.length === 1
              ? "book"
              : "books"}
          </span>

        </div>


        {filteredBooks.length > 0 ? (

          <div className="books-grid">

            {filteredBooks.map((book) => (
              <BookCard
                key={book.id}
                book={book}
              />
            ))}

          </div>

        ) : (

          <div className="no-books">

            <BookOpen size={40} />

            <h3>
              No books found
            </h3>

            <p>
              Try searching for another subject,
              author or stream.
            </p>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setSearchTerm("");
                setSelectedDepartment("All");
              }}
            >
              Clear filters
            </button>

          </div>

        )}

      </section>

    </div>
  );
}

export default Home;

========== Frontend\src\Pages\MyUploads\MyUploads.css ==========
/* =========================================================
   MY UPLOADS
   OpenNotes
   Black + Golden Yellow
========================================================= */

.my-uploads-page {

/* =========================================================
   HEADER
========================================================= */

}

.my-uploads-header-content {

  padding: 34px 36px;

  background: var(--color-surface);

  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);

  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.my-uploads-header-text {
  max-width: 760px;
}

.section-label {
  display: inline-block;

  margin-bottom: 8px;

  color: var(--color-primary);

  font-size: 0.72rem;
  font-weight: 800;

  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.my-uploads-header h1 {
  margin: 0;

  color: var(--color-text);

  font-size: clamp(
    2rem,
    4vw,

      20px 16px;
  }

  .my-uploads-header h1 {
    font-size: 1.85rem;
  }

  .my-uploads-header-icon {
    display: none;
  }

  .upload-stats-grid {
    gap: 8px;
  }

  .upload-stat-card {
    padding: 11px;
  }

  .my-uploads-filters {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .my-uploads-filters button {
    flex: 1;

    padding:
      0 7px;
  }

  .my-upload-card-top,
  .my-upload-card-body {
    padding-left: 16px;
    padding-right: 16px;
  }

  .my-upload-card-footer {
    padding-left: 16px;
    padding-right: 16px;
  }

  .download-wrapper {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .upload-download-button {
    flex: 1;
  }

  .delete-modal-actions {
    flex-direction: column-reverse;
  }

  .modal-cancel-button,
  .modal-submit-button {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

/* =========================================================
   REDUCED MOTION
========================================================= */

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .my-upload-card,
  .upload-stat-card,
  .modal-submit-button {
    transition: none;
  }
}

========== Frontend\src\Pages\MyUploads\MyUploads.jsx ==========
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
  BookOpen,
  CalendarDays,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock3,
  Download,
  FileQuestion,
  FileText,
  FolderOpen,
  Search,
  Trash2,
  UploadCloud,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";

import "./MyUploads.css";

/* =========================================================
   STORAGE KEYS
========================================================= */

const NOTES_STORAGE_KEY = "openNotes-uploaded-notes";
const PYQ_STORAGE_KEY = "openNotes-uploaded-pyqs";
const DELETE_REQUESTS_KEY =
  "openNotes-deletion-requests";

/* =========================================================
   INDEXED DB CONFIGURATION
========================================================= */

const DB_NAME = "openNotesDB";
const DB_VERSION = 2;

const NOTES_STORE = "noteFiles";
const PYQ_STORE = "pyqFiles";

/* =========================================================
   LOCAL STORAGE HELPERS
========================================================= */

function getLocalStorageArray(key) {
  try {
    const storedData = localStorage.getItem(key);

    if (!storedData) {
      return [];
    }

    const parsedData = JSON.parse(storedData);

    return Array.isArray(parsedData)
      ? parsedData
      : [];
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      `Error reading localStorage key: ${key}`,
      error
    );

    return [];
  }
}

function saveLocalStorageArray(key, data) {
  localStorage.setItem(
    key,
    JSON.stringify(data)
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   DATE FORMATTER
========================================================= */

function formatDate(dateValue) {
  if (!dateValue) {
    return "Date unavailable";
  }

  const date = new Date(dateValue);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return "Date unavailable";
  }

  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

/* =========================================================
   STATUS HELPERS
========================================================= */

function getStatusClass(status) {
  if (status === "approved") {
    return "status-approved";
  }

  if (status === "rejected") {
    return "status-rejected";
  }

  return "status-pending";
}

function getStatusLabel(status) {
  if (status === "approved") {
    return "Approved";
  }

  if (status === "rejected") {
    return "Rejected";
  }

  return "Pending";
}

/* =========================================================
   INDEXED DB
========================================================= */

function openDatabase() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (!window.indexedDB) {
      reject(
        new Error(
          "IndexedDB is not supported by this browser."
        )
      );

      return;
    }

    const request = window.indexedDB.open(
      DB_NAME,
      DB_VERSION
    );

    request.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
      const db = event.target.result;

      /*
       * Create Notes store if missing.
       */
      if (
        !db.objectStoreNames.contains(
          NOTES_STORE
        )
      ) {
        db.createObjectStore(NOTES_STORE, {
          keyPath: "id",
        });
      }

      /*
       * Create PYQ store if missing.
       */
      if (
        !db.objectStoreNames.contains(
          PYQ_STORE
        )
      ) {
        db.createObjectStore(PYQ_STORE, {
          keyPath: "id",
        });
      }
    };

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const db = request.result;

      /*
       * Verify both stores exist before allowing
       * the component to use the database.
       */
      if (
        !db.objectStoreNames.contains(
          NOTES_STORE
        ) ||
        !db.objectStoreNames.contains(
          PYQ_STORE
        )
      ) {
        db.close();

        reject(
          new Error(
            "OpenNotes storage is incomplete. Please refresh the page."
          )
        );

        return;
      }

      resolve(db);
    };

    request.onerror = () => {
      reject(
        request.error ||
          new Error(
            "Unable to open OpenNotes storage."
          )
      );
    };

    request.onblocked = () => {
      reject(
        new Error(
          "OpenNotes storage upgrade is blocked. Please close other OpenNotes tabs and try again."
        )
      );
    };
  });
}

/* =========================================================
   GET PDF FROM INDEXED DB
========================================================= */

async function getFileFromDatabase(
  storeName,
  fileId
) {
  const db = await openDatabase();

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(
      storeName,
      "readonly"
    );

    const store =
      transaction.objectStore(storeName);

    const request = store.get(fileId);

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      resolve(request.result || null);
    };

    request.onerror = () => {
      reject(request.error);
    };
  });
}

/* =========================================================
   MAIN COMPONENT
========================================================= */

function MyUploads() {
  const [uploads, setUploads] = useState([]);

  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] =
    useState("all");

  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] =
    useState("");

  const [deleteRequests, setDeleteRequests] =
    useState([]);

  const [selectedUpload, setSelectedUpload] =
    useState(null);

  const [showDeleteModal, setShowDeleteModal] =
    useState(false);

  const [deleteReason, setDeleteReason] =
    useState("");

  const [deleteError, setDeleteError] =
    useState("");

  const [deleteSuccess, setDeleteSuccess] =
    useState("");

  const [downloadingFile, setDownloadingFile] =
    useState("");

  /* =======================================================
     LOAD DATA
  ======================================================= */

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadData = () => {
      const notes =
        getLocalStorageArray(
          NOTES_STORAGE_KEY
        );

      const pyqs =
        getLocalStorageArray(
          PYQ_STORAGE_KEY
        );

      const formattedNotes =
        notes.map((note) => ({
          ...note,
          uploadType: "notes",
          typeLabel: "Notes",
        }));

      const formattedPYQs =
        pyqs.map((pyq) => ({
          ...pyq,
          uploadType: "pyq",
          typeLabel: "PYQ",
        }));

      const combinedUploads = [
        ...formattedNotes,
        ...formattedPYQs,
      ];

      combinedUploads.sort((a, b) => {
        const dateA = new Date(
          a.uploadedAt || 0
        ).getTime();

        const dateB = new Date(
          b.uploadedAt || 0
        ).getTime();

        return dateB - dateA;
      });

      setUploads(combinedUploads);

      setDeleteRequests(
        getLocalStorageArray(
          DELETE_REQUESTS_KEY
        )
      );
    };

    loadData();

    const handleNotesUpdated = () => {
      loadData();
    };

    const handlePYQsUpdated = () => {
      loadData();
    };

    window.addEventListener(
      "openNotesNotesUpdated",
      handleNotesUpdated
    );

    window.addEventListener(
      "openNotesPYQUpdated",
      handlePYQsUpdated
    );

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(
        "openNotesNotesUpdated",
        handleNotesUpdated
      );

      window.removeEventListener(
        "openNotesPYQUpdated",
        handlePYQsUpdated
      );
    };
  }, []);

  /* =======================================================
     CHECK DELETE REQUEST
  ======================================================= */

  const hasPendingDeleteRequest = (
    uploadId
  ) => {
    return deleteRequests.some(
      (request) =>
        request.uploadId === uploadId &&
        request.status === "pending"
    );
  };

  /* =======================================================
     FILTERED UPLOADS
  ======================================================= */

  const filteredUploads = useMemo(() => {
    const search =
      searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    return uploads.filter((upload) => {
      const matchesFilter =
        activeFilter === "all" ||
        upload.uploadType ===
          activeFilter;

      if (!matchesFilter) {
        return false;
      }

      if (!search) {
        return true;
      }

      const searchableText = [
        upload.title,
        upload.description,
        upload.subject,
        upload.module,
        upload.code,
        upload.stream,
        upload.yearId,
        upload.semester,
        upload.typeLabel,
        upload.examType,
        upload.year,
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")
        .toLowerCase();

      return searchableText.includes(
        search
      );
    });
  }, [
    uploads,
    activeFilter,
    searchTerm,
  ]);

  /* =======================================================
     STATISTICS
  ======================================================= */

  const totalUploads =
    uploads.length;

  const totalNotes =
    uploads.filter(
      (upload) =>
        upload.uploadType === "notes"
    ).length;

  const totalPYQs =
    uploads.filter(
      (upload) =>
        upload.uploadType === "pyq"
    ).length;

  const approvedUploads =
    uploads.filter(
      (upload) =>
        upload.status === "approved"
    ).length;

  const pendingUploads =
    uploads.filter(
      (upload) =>
        upload.status !== "approved"
    ).length;

  /* =======================================================
     DOWNLOAD PDF
  ======================================================= */

  const handleDownload = async (
    upload,
    file
  ) => {
    if (!file || !file.id) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setDownloadingFile(file.id);

      const storeName =
        upload.uploadType === "pyq"
          ? PYQ_STORE
          : NOTES_STORE;

      const storedFile =
        await getFileFromDatabase(
          storeName,
          file.id
        );

      if (
        !storedFile ||
        !storedFile.blob
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          "PDF file could not be found."
        );
      }

      const blobUrl =
        URL.createObjectURL(
          storedFile.blob
        );

      const link =
        document.createElement("a");

      link.href = blobUrl;

      link.download =
        storedFile.name ||
        file.name ||
        "OpenNotes.pdf";

      document.body.appendChild(link);

      link.click();

      document.body.removeChild(link);

      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "Download failed:",
        error
      );

      window.alert(
        "Unable to download the PDF. Please try again."
      );
    } finally {
      setDownloadingFile("");
    }
  };

  /* =======================================================
     OPEN DELETE MODAL
  ======================================================= */

  const handleOpenDeleteModal = (
    upload
  ) => {
    setSelectedUpload(upload);
    setDeleteReason("");
    setDeleteError("");
    setDeleteSuccess("");
    setShowDeleteModal(true);
  };

  /* =======================================================
     CLOSE DELETE MODAL
  ======================================================= */

  const handleCloseDeleteModal = () => {
    setShowDeleteModal(false);
    setSelectedUpload(null);
    setDeleteReason("");
    setDeleteError("");
    setDeleteSuccess("");
  };

  /* =======================================================
     SUBMIT DELETE REQUEST
  ======================================================= */

  const handleDeleteRequest = (
    event
  ) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    setDeleteError("");
    setDeleteSuccess("");

    if (!selectedUpload) {
      return;
    }

    const reason =
      deleteReason.trim();

    if (reason.length < 10) {
      setDeleteError(
        "Please provide a proper reason with at least 10 characters."
      );

      return;
    }

    if (
      hasPendingDeleteRequest(
        selectedUpload.id
      )
    ) {
      setDeleteError(
        "A deletion request is already pending for this upload."
      );

      return;
    }

    const newRequest = {
      id: `delete-${Date.now()}`,

      uploadId:
        selectedUpload.id,

      uploadType:
        selectedUpload.uploadType,

      title:
        selectedUpload.title,

      reason,

      requestedAt:
        new Date().toISOString(),

      status: "pending",
    };

    const existingRequests =
      getLocalStorageArray(
        DELETE_REQUESTS_KEY
      );

    const updatedRequests = [
      ...existingRequests,
      newRequest,
    ];

    saveLocalStorageArray(
      DELETE_REQUESTS_KEY,
      updatedRequests
    );

    setDeleteRequests(
      updatedRequests
    );

    setDeleteSuccess(
      "Deletion request sent successfully."
    );

    setTimeout(() => {
      handleCloseDeleteModal();
    }, 1200);
  };

  /* =======================================================
     EMPTY STATE
  ======================================================= */

  const renderEmptyState = () => {
    const hasUploads =
      uploads.length > 0;

    return (
      <div className="my-uploads-empty">
        <div className="my-uploads-empty-icon">
          {hasUploads ? (
            <Search size={30} />
          ) : (
            <FolderOpen size={30} />
          )}
        </div>

        <h3>
          {hasUploads
            ? "No uploads found"
            : "No uploads yet"}
        </h3>

        <p>
          {hasUploads
            ? "Try changing your search or filter."
            : "Your uploaded notes and PYQs will appear here."}
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  };

  /* =======================================================
     UPLOAD CARD
  ======================================================= */

  const renderUploadCard = (
    upload
  ) => {
    const isPYQ =
      upload.uploadType === "pyq";

    const fileCount =
      Array.isArray(upload.files)
        ? upload.files.length
        : 0;

    const deletePending =
      hasPendingDeleteRequest(
        upload.id
      );

    return (
      <article
        className="my-upload-card"
        key={`${upload.uploadType}-${upload.id}`}
      >
        {/* CARD HEADER */}

        <div className="my-upload-card-top">
          <div
            className={`my-upload-type-icon ${
              isPYQ
                ? "pyq-icon"
                : "notes-icon"
            }`}
          >
            {isPYQ ? (
              <FileQuestion size={22} />
            ) : (
              <FileText size={22} />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="my-upload-card-meta">
            <span className="my-upload-type">
              {upload.typeLabel}
            </span>

            <span
              className={`my-upload-status ${getStatusClass(
                upload.status
              )}`}
            >
              {upload.status ===
              "approved" ? (
                <CheckCircle2
                  size={14}
                />
              ) : (
                <Clock3 size={14} />
              )}

              {getStatusLabel(
                upload.status
              )}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CARD CONTENT */}

        <div className="my-upload-card-body">
          <h3>
            {upload.title ||
              "Untitled upload"}
          </h3>

          {upload.description && (
            <p className="my-upload-description">
              {upload.description}
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="my-upload-details">
            {upload.subject && (
              <div className="my-upload-detail">
                <BookOpen size={15} />

                <span>
                  {upload.subject}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}

            {upload.code && (
              <div className="my-upload-detail">
                <span className="detail-code">
                  {upload.code}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}

            {upload.semester && (
              <div className="my-upload-detail">
                <CalendarDays
                  size={15}
                />

                <span>
                  {upload.semester}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}

            {upload.module && (
              <div className="my-upload-detail">
                <FolderOpen size={15} />

                <span>
                  {upload.module}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}

            {isPYQ &&
              upload.examType && (
                <div className="my-upload-detail">
                  <span className="exam-type-badge">
                    {upload.examType}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

            {isPYQ &&
              upload.year && (
                <div className="my-upload-detail">
                  <span>
                    Exam {upload.year}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CARD FOOTER */}

        <div className="my-upload-card-footer">
          <div className="my-upload-file-info">
            <span>
              {fileCount}{" "}
              {fileCount === 1
                ? "PDF"
                : "PDFs"}
            </span>

            <span className="footer-dot">
              •
            </span>

            <span>
              {formatDate(
                upload.uploadedAt
              )}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="my-upload-actions">
            {fileCount > 0 && (
              <div className="download-wrapper">
                {upload.files.map(
                  (file, index) => (
                    <button
                      key={file.id}
                      type="button"
                      className="upload-download-button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        handleDownload(
                          upload,
                          file
                        )
                      }
                      disabled={
                        downloadingFile ===
                        file.id
                      }
                    >
                      <Download
                        size={16}
                      />

                      <span>
                        {upload.multipleParts
                          ? `Part ${
                              index + 1
                            }`
                          : "PDF"}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  )
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              className={`delete-request-button ${
                deletePending
                  ? "request-pending"
                  : ""
              }`}
              onClick={() =>
                handleOpenDeleteModal(
                  upload
                )
              }
              disabled={deletePending}
            >
              {deletePending ? (
                <>
                  <Clock3 size={16} />
                  Request Pending
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Trash2 size={16} />
                  Request Deletion
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </article>
    );
  };

  /* =======================================================
     MAIN RETURN
  ======================================================= */

  return (
    <section className="my-uploads-page">
      {/* =================================================
          HEADER
      ================================================= */}

      <header className="my-uploads-header">
        <div className="my-uploads-header-content">
          <div className="my-uploads-header-text">
            <span className="section-label">
              YOUR CONTRIBUTIONS
            </span>

            <h1>
              My{" "}
              <span>uploads.</span>
            </h1>

            <p>
              Manage the notes and previous year
              question papers you have contributed
              to OpenNotes.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="my-uploads-header-icon">
            <UploadCloud size={32} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* =================================================
          STATISTICS
      ================================================= */}

      <section className="upload-stats-grid">
        <div className="upload-stat-card">
          <div className="upload-stat-icon">
            <FolderOpen size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Total uploads</span>
            <strong>
              {totalUploads}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="upload-stat-card">
          <div className="upload-stat-icon">
            <FileText size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Notes</span>
            <strong>
              {totalNotes}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="upload-stat-card">
          <div className="upload-stat-icon">
            <FileQuestion size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>PYQs</span>
            <strong>
              {totalPYQs}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="upload-stat-card">
          <div className="upload-stat-icon">
            <CheckCircle2 size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Approved</span>
            <strong>
              {approvedUploads}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        {pendingUploads > 0 && (
          <div className="upload-stat-card">
            <div className="upload-stat-icon">
              <Clock3 size={21} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Pending</span>
              <strong>
                {pendingUploads}
              </strong>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* =================================================
          SEARCH + FILTER
      ================================================= */}

      <section className="my-uploads-controls">
        <div className="my-uploads-search">
          <Search size={19} />

          <input
            type="search"
            value={searchTerm}
            placeholder="Search your uploads..."
            onChange={(event) =>
              setSearchTerm(
                event.target.value
              )
            }
          />

          {searchTerm && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="clear-search-button"
              onClick={() =>
                setSearchTerm("")
              }
              aria-label="Clear search"
            >
              <X size={17} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="my-uploads-filters">
          <button
            type="button"
            className={
              activeFilter === "all"
                ? "active"
                : ""
            }
            onClick={() =>
              setActiveFilter("all")
            }
          >
            All
            <span>
              {totalUploads}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={
              activeFilter === "notes"
                ? "active"
                : ""
            }
            onClick={() =>
              setActiveFilter("notes")
            }
          >
            Notes
            <span>
              {totalNotes}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={
              activeFilter === "pyq"
                ? "active"
                : ""
            }
            onClick={() =>
              setActiveFilter("pyq")
            }
          >
            PYQs
            <span>
              {totalPYQs}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =================================================
          RESULTS HEADER
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="my-uploads-results-header">
        <div>
          <span className="section-label">
            UPLOAD LIBRARY
          </span>

          <h2>
            {activeFilter === "all"
              ? "All your uploads"
              : activeFilter === "notes"
              ? "Your notes"
              : "Your PYQs"}
          </h2>
        </div>

        <span className="results-count">
          {filteredUploads.length}{" "}
          {filteredUploads.length === 1
            ? "upload"
            : "uploads"}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          UPLOADS
      ================================================= */}

      {filteredUploads.length > 0 ? (
        <section className="my-uploads-grid">
          {filteredUploads.map(
            renderUploadCard
          )}
        </section>
      ) : (
        renderEmptyState()
      )}

      {/* =================================================
          DELETE MODAL
      ================================================= */}

      {showDeleteModal &&
        selectedUpload && (
          <div
            className="delete-modal-overlay"
            onMouseDown={(event) => {
              if (
                event.target ===
                event.currentTarget
              ) {
                handleCloseDeleteModal();
              }
            }}
          >
            <div
              className="delete-modal"
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              aria-labelledby="delete-modal-title"
            >
              {/* MODAL HEADER */}

              <div className="delete-modal-header">
                <div>
                  <span className="section-label">
                    DELETION REQUEST
                  </span>

                  <h2 id="delete-modal-title">
                    Request upload removal
                  </h2>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="modal-close-button"
                  onClick={
                    handleCloseDeleteModal
                  }
                  aria-label="Close"
                >
                  <X size={20} />
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* MODAL BODY */}

              <div className="delete-modal-content">
                <div className="delete-upload-preview">
                  <div className="delete-preview-icon">
                    {selectedUpload.uploadType ===
                    "pyq" ? (
                      <FileQuestion
                        size={20}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <FileText
                        size={20}
                      />
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <strong>
                      {
                        selectedUpload.title
                      }
                    </strong>

                    <span>
                      {
                        selectedUpload.subject
                      }
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <p className="delete-modal-note">
                  Your upload will not be deleted
                  immediately. A deletion request
                  will be sent to the responsible
                  teacher or admin for review.
                </p>

                <form
                  onSubmit={
                    handleDeleteRequest
                  }
                >
                  <div className="delete-reason-group">
                    <label htmlFor="deleteReason">
                      Reason for deletion
                      <span>*</span>
                    </label>

                    <textarea
                      id="deleteReason"
                      value={deleteReason}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setDeleteReason(
                          event.target.value
                        )
                      }
                      placeholder="Explain why you want this upload to be removed..."
                      rows={5}
                      maxLength={500}
                    />

                    <div className="delete-reason-footer">
                      <span>
                        Please provide a clear
                        reason.
                      </span>

                      <span>
                        {
                          deleteReason.length
                        }
                        /500
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {deleteError && (
                    <div className="delete-message delete-error">
                      <X size={17} />

                      <span>
                        {deleteError}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {deleteSuccess && (
                    <div className="delete-message delete-success">
                      <CheckCircle2
                        size={17}
                      />

                      <span>
                        {deleteSuccess}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="delete-modal-actions">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="modal-cancel-button"
                      onClick={
                        handleCloseDeleteModal
                      }
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="modal-submit-button"
                      disabled={
                        Boolean(
                          deleteSuccess
                        )
                      }
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={17} />

                      Send Request
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
    </section>
  );
}

export default MyUploads;

  .profile-detail-row:nth-child(odd) {
    border-right: 0;
  }

  .profile-form-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .profile-section-nav {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .profile-section-nav button {
    justify-content: flex-start;

    padding: 0 11px;

    text-align: left;
  }

  .profile-form-actions {
    flex-direction: column-reverse;
  }

  .profile-cancel-button,
  .profile-save-button {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

/* =========================================================
   VERY SMALL
========================================================= */

@media (max-width: 380px) {
  .profile-header-icon {
    display: none;
  }

  .profile-header h1 {
    font-size: 1.85rem;
  }

  .profile-main {
    padding: 20px 15px;
  }
}

/* =========================================================
   REDUCED MOTION
========================================================= */

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .profile-camera-button,
  .profile-edit-button,
  .profile-resource-card {
    transition: none;
  }
