========== Frontend\src\Pages\Profile\Profile.jsx ==========
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, Navigate, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  BookOpen,
  Camera,
  CheckCircle2,
  Download,
  Edit3,
  FileText,
  GraduationCap,
  LayoutDashboard,
  LogOut,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  Save,
  ShieldCheck,
  Upload,
  User,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";

import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";
import { roleLabels, ROLES } from "../../data/authData";
import "./Profile.css";

const PROFILE_KEY = "openNotes-profile";
const SAVED_KEY = "openNotes-saved-notes";
const DOWNLOADED_KEY = "openNotes-downloaded-notes";
const NOTES_KEY = "openNotes-uploaded-notes";
const PYQS_KEY = "openNotes-uploaded-pyqs";

const STREAMS = [
  { value: "engineering", label: "Engineering" },
  { value: "law", label: "Law" },
  { value: "architecture", label: "Architecture" },
  { value: "management", label: "Management" },
  { value: "research", label: "Research" },
];

const YEARS = ["1st Year", "2nd Year", "3rd Year", "4th Year"];

function readArray(key) {
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || "[]");
    return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function readProfile(user) {
  try {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PROFILE_KEY) || "{}");

    return {
      name: user?.name || saved.name || "OpenNotes User",
      email: user?.email || saved.email || "",
      phone: user?.phone || saved.phone || "",
      stream: saved.stream || "",
      course: saved.course || user?.department || "",
      year: saved.year || user?.year || "",
      about: saved.about || "",
      profileImage: user?.profileImage || saved.profileImage || "",
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      name: user?.name || "OpenNotes User",
      email: user?.email || "",
      phone: user?.phone || "",
      stream: "",
      course: user?.department || "",
      year: user?.year || "",
      about: "",
      profileImage: user?.profileImage || "",
    };
  }
}

function Profile() {
  const { user, currentUser, role, isGuest, logout, updateUser } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const fileRef = useRef(null);

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(() =>
    readProfile(user || currentUser)
  );
  const [form, setForm] = useState(() =>
    readProfile(user || currentUser)
  );
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [section, setSection] = useState("workspace");
  const [savedNotes, setSavedNotes] = useState(() =>
    readArray(SAVED_KEY)
  );
  const [downloadedNotes, setDownloadedNotes] = useState(() =>
    readArray(DOWNLOADED_KEY)
  );
  const [uploadedNotes, setUploadedNotes] = useState(() =>
    readArray(NOTES_KEY)
  );
  const [uploadedPYQs, setUploadedPYQs] = useState(() =>
    readArray(PYQS_KEY)
  );
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const refreshResources = () => {
      setSavedNotes(readArray(SAVED_KEY));
      setDownloadedNotes(readArray(DOWNLOADED_KEY));
      setUploadedNotes(readArray(NOTES_KEY));
      setUploadedPYQs(readArray(PYQS_KEY));
    };

    window.addEventListener("openNotesSavedNotesUpdated", refreshResources);
    window.addEventListener(
      "openNotesDownloadedNotesUpdated",
      refreshResources
    );
    window.addEventListener("storage", refreshResources);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(
        "openNotesSavedNotesUpdated",
        refreshResources
      );
      window.removeEventListener(
        "openNotesDownloadedNotesUpdated",
        refreshResources
      );
      window.removeEventListener("storage", refreshResources);
    };
  }, []);



  const initials = useMemo(
    () =>
      (profile.name || "U")
        .trim()
        .split(/\s+/)
        .slice(0, 2)
        .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
        .join(""),
    [profile.name]
  );

  const myUploads = useMemo(() => {
    const email = (user?.email || "").toLowerCase();

    return [...uploadedNotes, ...uploadedPYQs].filter((item) => {
      const owner = (
        item.email ||
        item.uploaderEmail ||
        item.uploadedByEmail ||
        ""
      ).toLowerCase();

      return email !== "" && owner === email;
    });
  }, [uploadedNotes, uploadedPYQs, user?.email]);

  const pendingUploads = myUploads.filter(
    (item) => String(item.status || "").toLowerCase() === "pending"
  ).length;

  const handleChange = (event) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;
    setForm((previous) => ({ ...previous, [name]: value }));
    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  const handleSave = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    const name = form.name.trim();

    if (!name) {
      setError("Please enter your full name.");
      return;
    }

    const updated = {
      ...form,
      name,
      email: user?.email || form.email.trim(),
      phone: form.phone.trim(),
      course: form.course.trim(),
      about: form.about.trim(),
    };

    try {
      localStorage.setItem(PROFILE_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));

      updateUser({
        name: updated.name,
        phone: updated.phone,
        profileImage: updated.profileImage,
        department: updated.course,
        year: updated.year,
      });

      setProfile(updated);
      setForm(updated);
      setEditing(false);
      setMessage("Profile updated successfully.");

      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("openNotesProfileUpdated"));
    } catch {
      setError("Unable to save your profile. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  const handlePhotoChange = (event) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];

    if (!file) return;

    if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
      setError("Please select a valid image.");
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    if (file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setError("Your image must be smaller than 2 MB.");
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    const reader = new FileReader();

    reader.onload = () => {
      setForm((previous) => ({
        ...previous,
        profileImage: String(reader.result || ""),
      }));
    };

    reader.onerror = () => {
      setError("Unable to read the selected image.");
    };

    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    if (!window.confirm("Are you sure you want to sign out?")) return;

    logout();
    navigate("/home", { replace: true });
  };

  if (isGuest || !user) {
    return <Navigate to="/home" replace />;
  }

  const isAdmin = role === ROLES.ADMIN;
  const isTeacher = role === ROLES.TEACHER;

  const shortcuts = [
    {
      label: "Browse Notes",
      description: "Explore study materials",
      path: "/browse-notes",
      icon: BookOpen,
    },
    {
      label: "Browse PYQs",
      description: "Explore previous-year questions",
      path: "/browse-pyqs",
      icon: FileText,
    },
    {
      label: "Upload Resource",
      description: "Share your learning materials",
      path: "/upload",
      icon: Upload,
    },
    {
      label: "My Uploads",
      description: "View your uploaded resources",
      path: "/my-uploads",
      icon: Download,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="profile-page">
      <header className="profile-header">
        <div className="profile-header-content">
          <div className="profile-header-text">
            <span className="profile-section-label">YOUR ACCOUNT</span>
            <h1>
              Your <span>profile.</span>
            </h1>
            <p>Manage your account and role-specific OpenNotes workspace.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="profile-header-icon">
            <User size={32} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {message && (
        <div className="profile-message profile-success">
          <CheckCircle2 size={18} />
          <span>{message}</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMessage("")}
            aria-label="Dismiss message"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="profile-message profile-error">
          <X size={18} />
          <span>{error}</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setError("")}
            aria-label="Dismiss error"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="profile-layout">
        <aside className="profile-sidebar-card">
          <div className="profile-avatar-wrapper">
            {profile.profileImage ? (
              <img
                src={profile.profileImage}
                alt="Profile"
                className="profile-avatar-image"
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="profile-avatar-placeholder">{initials}</div>
            )}

            {editing && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="profile-camera-button"
                onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
                aria-label="Change profile picture"
              >
                <Camera size={17} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {editing && (
            <>
              <input
                ref={fileRef}
                type="file"
                accept="image/*"
                className="profile-file-input"
                onChange={handlePhotoChange}
              />

              {form.profileImage && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="remove-photo-button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setForm((previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      profileImage: "",
                    }))
                  }
                >
                  Remove photo
                </button>
              )}
            </>
          )}

          <h2>{profile.name}</h2>
          <p className="profile-sidebar-email">{user.email}</p>

          <div className="profile-role-badge">
            <User size={14} />
            {roleLabels[role] || role}
          </div>

          <div className="profile-sidebar-divider" />

          <nav className="profile-section-nav">
            <button
              type="button"
              className={section === "workspace" ? "active" : ""}
              onClick={() => setSection("workspace")}
            >
              <LayoutDashboard size={17} />
              <span className="profile-nav-label">My Workspace</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={section === "overview" ? "active" : ""}
              onClick={() => setSection("overview")}
            >
              <User size={17} />
              <span className="profile-nav-label">Profile Overview</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={section === "saved" ? "active" : ""}
              onClick={() => setSection("saved")}
            >
              <BookOpen size={17} />
              <span className="profile-nav-label">Saved Notes</span>
              <span className="profile-nav-count">{savedNotes.length}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={section === "downloaded" ? "active" : ""}
              onClick={() => setSection("downloaded")}
            >
              <Download size={17} />
              <span className="profile-nav-label">Downloaded</span>
              <span className="profile-nav-count">
                {downloadedNotes.length}
              </span>
            </button>
          </nav>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="profile-logout-button"
            onClick={handleLogout}
          >
            <LogOut size={17} />
            Sign out
          </button>
        </aside>

        <main className="profile-main">
          {section === "workspace" && (
            <section className="profile-resource-section">
              <div className="profile-content-header">
                <div>
                  <span className="profile-section-label">YOUR WORKSPACE</span>
                  <h2>
                    {isAdmin
                      ? "Admin Workspace"
                      : isTeacher
                        ? "Teacher Workspace"
                        : "Student Workspace"}
                  </h2>
                  <p>Your account overview and quick actions.</p>
                </div>
                {isAdmin ? (
                  <ShieldCheck size={28} />
                ) : (
                  <LayoutDashboard size={28} />
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="profile-resource-grid">
                <article className="profile-resource-card">
                  <div className="profile-resource-icon">
                    <BookOpen size={21} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="profile-resource-content">
                    <span className="profile-resource-type">SAVED NOTES</span>
                    <h3>{savedNotes.length}</h3>
                    <p>Resources saved for later</p>
                  </div>
                </article>

                <article className="profile-resource-card">
                  <div className="profile-resource-icon">
                    <Download size={21} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="profile-resource-content">
                    <span className="profile-resource-type">DOWNLOADS</span>
                    <h3>{downloadedNotes.length}</h3>
                    <p>Your download history</p>
                  </div>
                </article>

                {!isAdmin && (
                  <article className="profile-resource-card">
                    <div className="profile-resource-icon">
                      <Upload size={21} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="profile-resource-content">
                      <span className="profile-resource-type">MY UPLOADS</span>
                      <h3>{myUploads.length}</h3>
                      <p>{pendingUploads} pending review</p>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                )}

                {isTeacher && (
                  <article className="profile-resource-card">
                    <div className="profile-resource-icon">
                      <GraduationCap size={21} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="profile-resource-content">
                      <span className="profile-resource-type">TEACHER ACCOUNT</span>
                      <h3>
                        {user.teacherApprovalStatus === "approved"
                          ? "Approved"
                          : "Pending"}
                      </h3>
                      <p>Account approval status</p>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                )}

                {isAdmin && (
                  <article className="profile-resource-card">
                    <div className="profile-resource-icon">
                      <ShieldCheck size={21} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="profile-resource-content">
                      <span className="profile-resource-type">ADMIN TOOLS</span>
                      <h3>Manage</h3>
                      <p>Open the administration workspace</p>
                      <Link to="/admin/dashboard">Open admin tools</Link>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="profile-content-header" style={{ marginTop: 28 }}>
                <div>
                  <span className="profile-section-label">QUICK ACCESS</span>
                  <h2>Continue learning</h2>
                  <p>Open the sections available to your account.</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="profile-resource-grid">
                {shortcuts.map(({ label, description, path, icon: Icon }) => (
                  <Link
                    key={path}
                    to={path}
                    className="profile-resource-card"
                    style={{ textDecoration: "none" }}
                  >
                    <div className="profile-resource-icon">
                      <Icon size={21} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="profile-resource-content">
                      <h3>{label}</h3>
                      <p>{description}</p>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>

              {isAdmin && (
                <div className="profile-about-card">
                  <span>ADMINISTRATION</span>
                  <p>
                    Teacher approval and resource management must use
                    admin-protected routes and permissions.
                  </p>
                  <Link to="/admin/teachers">Teacher approvals</Link>
                </div>
              )}
            </section>
          )}

          {section === "overview" && (
            <>
              <div className="profile-content-header">
                <div>
                  <span className="profile-section-label">
                    PROFILE INFORMATION
                  </span>
                  <h2>Personal details</h2>
                  <p>Keep your account information up to date.</p>
                </div>

                {!editing && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="profile-edit-button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setForm({ ...profile });
                      setEditing(true);
                      setMessage("");
                      setError("");
                    }}
                  >
                    <Edit3 size={17} />
                    Edit Profile
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              {editing ? (
                <form className="profile-form" onSubmit={handleSave}>
                  <section className="profile-form-section">
                    <div className="profile-form-section-heading">
                      <span>BASIC INFORMATION</span>
                      <h3>About you</h3>
                    </div>

                    <div className="profile-form-grid">
                      <div className="profile-form-group">
                        <label htmlFor="profile-name">Full name *</label>
                        <div className="profile-input-wrapper">
                          <User size={17} />
                          <input
                            id="profile-name"
                            name="name"
                            value={form.name}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            required
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="profile-form-group">
                        <label htmlFor="profile-email">Email</label>
                        <div className="profile-input-wrapper">
                          <Mail size={17} />
                          <input
                            id="profile-email"
                            value={user.email || ""}
                            readOnly
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="profile-form-group">
                        <label htmlFor="profile-phone">Phone</label>
                        <div className="profile-input-wrapper">
                          <Phone size={17} />
                          <input
                            id="profile-phone"
                            name="phone"
                            value={form.phone}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="profile-form-group">
                        <label htmlFor="profile-stream">Stream</label>
                        <div className="profile-input-wrapper">
                          <GraduationCap size={17} />
                          <select
                            id="profile-stream"
                            name="stream"
                            value={form.stream}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                          >
                            <option value="">Select stream</option>
                            {STREAMS.map((item) => (
                              <option key={item.value} value={item.value}>
                                {item.label}
                              </option>
                            ))}
                          </select>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="profile-form-group">
                        <label htmlFor="profile-course">
                          Course / Department
                        </label>
                        <div className="profile-input-wrapper">
                          <BookOpen size={17} />
                          <input
                            id="profile-course"
                            name="course"
                            value={form.course}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="profile-form-group">
                        <label htmlFor="profile-year">Academic year</label>
                        <div className="profile-input-wrapper">
                          <GraduationCap size={17} />
                          <select
                            id="profile-year"
                            name="year"
                            value={form.year}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                          >
                            <option value="">Select year</option>
                            {YEARS.map((year) => (
                              <option key={year} value={year}>
                                {year}
                              </option>
                            ))}
                          </select>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="profile-form-group profile-about-group">
                      <label htmlFor="profile-about">About</label>
                      <textarea
                        id="profile-about"
                        name="about"
                        value={form.about}
                        onChange={handleChange}
                        rows={5}
                        maxLength={500}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </section>

                  <div className="profile-form-actions">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="profile-cancel-button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setForm({ ...profile });
                        setEditing(false);
                      }}
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                    <button type="submit" className="profile-save-button">
                      <Save size={17} />
                      Save Changes
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              ) : (
                <section className="profile-details-card">
                  {[
                    ["Full name", profile.name, User],
                    ["Email", user.email, Mail],
                    ["Phone", profile.phone, Phone],
                    [
                      "Stream",
                      STREAMS.find((item) => item.value === profile.stream)
                        ?.label || "Not added",
                      GraduationCap,
                    ],
                    ["Course / Department", profile.course, BookOpen],
                    ["Academic year", profile.year, GraduationCap],
                  ].map(([label, value, Icon]) => (
                    <div className="profile-detail-row" key={label}>
                      <div className="profile-detail-icon">
                        <Icon size={18} />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>{label}</span>
                        <strong>{value || "Not added"}</strong>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}

                  <div className="profile-about-card">
                    <span>ABOUT</span>
                    <p>{profile.about || "No information added yet."}</p>
                  </div>
                </section>
              )}
            </>
          )}

          {section === "saved" && (
            <section className="profile-resource-section">
              <div className="profile-content-header">
                <div>
                  <span className="profile-section-label">SAVED RESOURCES</span>
                  <h2>Saved Notes</h2>
                  <p>Resources saved for quick access.</p>
                </div>
                <BookOpen size={24} />
              </div>

              {savedNotes.length ? (
                <div className="profile-resource-grid">
                  {savedNotes.map((item, index) => (
                    <article
                      className="profile-resource-card"
                      key={item.id || `${item.title}-${index}`}
                    >
                      <div className="profile-resource-icon">
                        <FileText size={21} />
                      </div>
                      <div className="profile-resource-content">
                        <span className="profile-resource-type">SAVED</span>
                        <h3>{item.title || "Saved resource"}</h3>
                        <p>
                          {item.subject || item.description || "Study resource"}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </article>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="profile-resource-empty">
                  <BookOpen size={28} />
                  <h3>No saved notes yet</h3>
                  <p>Notes you save will appear here.</p>
                  <Link to="/browse-notes">Browse Notes</Link>
                </div>
              )}
            </section>
          )}

          {section === "downloaded" && (
            <section className="profile-resource-section">
              <div className="profile-content-header">
                <div>
                  <span className="profile-section-label">DOWNLOAD HISTORY</span>
                  <h2>Downloaded Resources</h2>
                  <p>Resources you have downloaded.</p>
                </div>
                <Download size={24} />
              </div>

              {downloadedNotes.length ? (
                <div className="profile-resource-grid">
                  {downloadedNotes.map((item, index) => (
                    <article
                      className="profile-resource-card"
                      key={item.id || `${item.title}-${index}`}
                    >
                      <div className="profile-resource-icon">
                        <Download size={21} />
                      </div>
                      <div className="profile-resource-content">
                        <span className="profile-resource-type">DOWNLOADED</span>
                        <h3>{item.title || "Downloaded resource"}</h3>
                        <p>
                          {item.subject || item.description || "Study resource"}
                        </p>
                        {item.downloadedAt && (
                          <span className="profile-resource-date">
                            Downloaded{" "}
                            {new Date(item.downloadedAt).toLocaleDateString(
                              "en-IN"
                            )}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </article>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="profile-resource-empty">
                  <Download size={28} />
                  <h3>No downloads yet</h3>
                  <p>Your download history will appear here.</p>
                  <Link to="/browse-notes">Browse Notes</Link>
                </div>
              )}
            </section>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export default Profile;

========== Frontend\src\Pages\Splash\Splash.css ==========
.splash-page {
  position: relative;

  min-height: 100vh;

  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;

  overflow: hidden;

========== Frontend\src\Pages\Splash\Splash.jsx ==========
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import "./Splash.css";

function Splash() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      navigate("/home", { replace: true });
    }, 3500);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [navigate]);

  return (
    <main className="splash-page">
      <div className="splash-background-glow"></div>

      <section className="splash-content">
        <div className="splash-logo-wrapper">
          <img
            src="/logo.png"
            alt="OpenNotes logo"
            className="splash-logo"
          />
        </div>

        

        <p className="splash-slogan">
          Every semester, every subject,
          <br />
          right at your fingertips.
        </p>

        <div className="splash-loader">
          <span></span>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

export default Splash;

========== Frontend\src\Pages\Student\StudentDashboard.jsx ==========

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  BookOpen,
  FileQuestion,
  FileText,
  FolderOpen,
  UploadCloud,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";
import { getUserDashboardStats } from "../../services/dashboardService";
import "../../css/dashboards.css";

export default function StudentDashboard() {
  const { currentUser } = useAuth();
  const stats = getUserDashboardStats(currentUser?.email);

  const shortcuts = [
    {
      title: "Browse Notes",
      description: "Find notes organised by course and semester.",
      path: "/browse-notes",
      icon: BookOpen,
    },
    {
      title: "Browse PYQs",
      description: "Practise with previous-year question papers.",
      path: "/browse-pyqs",
      icon: FileQuestion,
    },
    {
      title: "Upload Resources",
      description: "Share your notes or question papers.",
      path: "/upload",
      icon: UploadCloud,
    },
    {
      title: "My Uploads",
      description: "Review resources you've submitted.",
      path: "/my-uploads",
      icon: FolderOpen,
    },
    {
      title: "My Profile",
      description: "View or update your profile information.",
      path: "/profile",
      icon: UserRound,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <main className="dashboard-page">
      <section className="dashboard-welcome">
        <span className="dashboard-eyebrow">STUDENT SPACE</span>
        <h1>Welcome back, {currentUser?.name || "Student"}!</h1>
        <p>Your study resources and learning shortcuts, all in one place.</p>
      </section>

      <section className="dashboard-metrics">
        <article className="dashboard-metric">
          <FileText size={22} />
          <span>Notes uploaded</span>
          <strong>{stats.notesUploaded}</strong>
        </article>
        <article className="dashboard-metric">
          <FileQuestion size={22} />
          <span>PYQs uploaded</span>
          <strong>{stats.pyqsUploaded}</strong>
        </article>
        <article className="dashboard-metric">
          <UploadCloud size={22} />
          <span>Pending uploads</span>
          <strong>{stats.pendingUploads}</strong>
        </article>
      </section>

      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="dashboard-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Your learning space</h2>
            <p>Choose what you want to work on next.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="dashboard-shortcuts">
          {shortcuts.map(({ title, description, path, icon: Icon }) => (
            <article className="dashboard-shortcut" key={title}>
              <span className="dashboard-shortcut-icon">
                <Icon size={22} />
              </span>
              <h3>{title}</h3>
              <p>{description}</p>
              <Link to={path}>
                Open section <ArrowRight size={15} />
              </Link>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <p className="dashboard-data-note">
        Upload counts use metadata available in this browser. They may not
        include older uploads that don't record your account email.
      </p>
    </main>
  );
}


========== Frontend\src\Pages\Teacher\TeacherDashboard.jsx ==========

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  BookOpen,
  FileQuestion,
  FileText,
  FolderOpen,
  UploadCloud,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";
import useAuth from "../../context/useAuth";
import { getUserDashboardStats } from "../../services/dashboardService";
import "../../css/dashboards.css";

export default function TeacherDashboard() {
  const { currentUser } = useAuth();
  const stats = getUserDashboardStats(currentUser?.email);

  const shortcuts = [
    {
      title: "Browse Notes",
      description: "Explore available notes and learning resources.",
      path: "/browse-notes",
      icon: BookOpen,
    },
    {
      title: "Browse PYQs",
      description: "Review previous-year question papers.",
      path: "/browse-pyqs",
      icon: FileQuestion,
    },
    {
      title: "Upload Resources",
      description: "Share teaching notes and question papers.",
      path: "/upload",
      icon: UploadCloud,
    },
    {
      title: "My Uploads",
      description: "Review your submitted resources.",
      path: "/my-uploads",
      icon: FolderOpen,
    },
    {
      title: "My Profile",
      description: "Keep your account information up to date.",
      path: "/profile",
      icon: UserRound,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <main className="dashboard-page">
      <section className="dashboard-welcome">
        <span className="dashboard-eyebrow">TEACHER SPACE</span>
        <h1>Welcome back, {currentUser?.name || "Teacher"}!</h1>
        <p>Manage your teaching resources and share useful study material.</p>
      </section>

      <section className="dashboard-metrics">
        <article className="dashboard-metric">
          <FileText size={22} />
          <span>Notes uploaded</span>
          <strong>{stats.notesUploaded}</strong>
        </article>
        <article className="dashboard-metric">
          <FileQuestion size={22} />
          <span>PYQs uploaded</span>
          <strong>{stats.pyqsUploaded}</strong>
        </article>
        <article className="dashboard-metric">
          <UploadCloud size={22} />
          <span>Pending uploads</span>
          <strong>{stats.pendingUploads}</strong>
        </article>
      </section>

      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="dashboard-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Teaching tools</h2>
            <p>Access your resources and profile.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="dashboard-shortcuts">
          {shortcuts.map(({ title, description, path, icon: Icon }) => (
            <article className="dashboard-shortcut" key={title}>
              <span className="dashboard-shortcut-icon">
                <Icon size={22} />
              </span>
              <h3>{title}</h3>
              <p>{description}</p>
              <Link to={path}>
                Open section <ArrowRight size={15} />
              </Link>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <p className="dashboard-data-note">
        Teacher approvals are managed by the administrator. Upload moderation
        and publication rules must also be enforced by the backend before
        production deployment.
      </p>
    </main>
  );
}

========== Frontend\src\Pages\Upload\Upload.jsx ==========
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  BookOpen,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronRight,
  FileQuestion,
  FileText,
  GraduationCap,
  UploadCloud,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";

import pyqData from "../../data/pyqdata";
import notesData from "../../data/notesData";

import "./Upload.css";

const PYQ_STORAGE_KEY = "openNotes-uploaded-pyqs";
const NOTES_STORAGE_KEY = "openNotes-uploaded-notes";

const DB_NAME = "openNotesDB";
const DB_VERSION = 2;
const PYQ_STORE = "pyqFiles";
const NOTES_STORE = "noteFiles";

const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 25 * 1024 * 1024;

const noteStreams = [
  {
    id: "engineering",
    title: "Engineering",
    description: "Engineering notes and study materials",
    icon: GraduationCap,
  },
  {
    id: "law",
    title: "Law",
    description: "Law courses and study materials",
    icon: BookOpen,
  },
  {
    id: "architecture",
    title: "Architecture",
    description: "Architecture and design resources",
    icon: BookOpen,
  },
  {
    id: "management-research",
    title: "Management & Research",
    description: "Management and research materials",
    icon: GraduationCap,
  },
];

const pyqStreams = [
  {
    id: "engineering",
    title: "Engineering",
    description: "Engineering previous year papers",
    icon: GraduationCap,
  },
  {
    id: "law",
    title: "Law",
    description: "Law examination papers",
    icon: BookOpen,
  },
  {
    id: "architecture",
    title: "Architecture",
    description: "Architecture examination papers",
    icon: BookOpen,
  },
  {
    id: "management",
    title: "Management",
    description: "Management examination papers",
    icon: BookOpen,
  },
  {
    id: "research",
    title: "Research",
    description: "Research examination papers",
    icon: GraduationCap,
  },
];

const createInitialForm = () => ({
  stream: "",
  year: "",
  semester: "",
  subject: "",
  module: "",

  examType: "",
  examYear: "",

  title: "",
  description: "",

  multipleParts: false,

  files: [],
});

function openDatabase() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (!window.indexedDB) {
      reject(
        new Error(
          "IndexedDB is not supported by this browser."
        )
      );

      return;
    }

    const request = window.indexedDB.open(
      DB_NAME,
      DB_VERSION
    );

    request.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
      const db = event.target.result;

      /*
       * Create PYQ file store if it does not exist.
       */
      if (
        !db.objectStoreNames.contains(PYQ_STORE)
      ) {
        db.createObjectStore(PYQ_STORE, {
          keyPath: "id",
        });
      }

      /*
       * Create Notes file store if it does not exist.
       */
      if (
        !db.objectStoreNames.contains(
          NOTES_STORE
        )
      ) {
        db.createObjectStore(NOTES_STORE, {
          keyPath: "id",
        });
      }
    };

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const db = request.result;

      /*
       * Safety check.
       *
       * If the database somehow still does not
       * contain the required stores, close it and
       * return a clear error instead of allowing
       * transaction() to fail later.
       */
      if (
        !db.objectStoreNames.contains(
          PYQ_STORE
        ) ||
        !db.objectStoreNames.contains(
          NOTES_STORE
        )
      ) {
        db.close();

        reject(
          new Error(
            "OpenNotes storage is incomplete. Please refresh the page."
          )
        );

        return;
      }

      resolve(db);
    };

    request.onerror = () => {
      reject(
        request.error ||
          new Error(
            "Unable to open OpenNotes storage."
          )
      );
    };

    request.onblocked = () => {
      reject(
        new Error(
          "OpenNotes storage upgrade is blocked. Please close other OpenNotes tabs and try again."
        )
      );
    };
  });
}

async function saveFilesToDatabase(files, storeName, uploadId) {
  if (!files || files.length === 0) {
    return [];
  }

  const db = await openDatabase();

  const savedFiles = [];

  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(storeName, "readwrite");
    const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);

    files.forEach((file, index) => {
      const fileId = `${uploadId}-${index}`;

      store.put({
        id: fileId,
        uploadId,
        fileIndex: index,
        name: file.name,
        type: file.type,
        size: file.size,
        blob: file,
      });

      savedFiles.push({
        id: fileId,
        name: file.name,
        type: file.type,
        size: file.size,
      });
    });

    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
      resolve();
    };

    transaction.onerror = () => {
      reject(transaction.error);
    };
  });

  db.close();

  return savedFiles;
}

function getLocalStorageArray(key) {
  try {
    const value = localStorage.getItem(key);

    if (!value) {
      return [];
    }

    const parsed = JSON.parse(value);

    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function saveLocalStorageArray(key, data) {
  localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(data));
}

function Upload() {
  const [uploadType, setUploadType] = useState(null);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState(createInitialForm);

  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);

  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * CURRENT DATA
   * =========================================================
   */

  const activeData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!uploadType || !formData.stream) {
      return null;
    }

    if (uploadType === "notes") {
      return notesData[formData.stream] || null;
    }

    return pyqData[formData.stream] || null;
  }, [uploadType, formData.stream]);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * YEARS
   * =========================================================
   */

  const availableYears = useMemo(() => {
    if (!activeData || typeof activeData !== "object") {
      return [];
    }

    return Object.entries(activeData).map(([id, data]) => ({
      id,
      ...data,
    }));
  }, [activeData]);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * CURRENT YEAR
   * =========================================================
   */

  const currentYearData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!activeData || !formData.year) {
      return null;
    }

    return activeData[formData.year] || null;
  }, [activeData, formData.year]);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * SEMESTERS
   * =========================================================
   */

  const availableSemesters = useMemo(() => {
    if (!currentYearData?.semesters) {
      return [];
    }

    return Object.entries(currentYearData.semesters).map(
      ([id, data]) => ({
        id,
        ...data,
      })
    );
  }, [currentYearData]);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * CURRENT SEMESTER
   * =========================================================
   */

  const currentSemesterData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!currentYearData?.semesters || !formData.semester) {
      return null;
    }

    return currentYearData.semesters[formData.semester] || null;
  }, [currentYearData, formData.semester]);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * SUBJECTS
   * =========================================================
   */

  const availableSubjects = useMemo(() => {
    return currentSemesterData?.subjects || [];
  }, [currentSemesterData]);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * CURRENT SUBJECT
   * =========================================================
   */

  const currentSubjectData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!formData.subject) {
      return null;
    }

    return (
      availableSubjects.find(
        (subject) => subject.id === formData.subject
      ) || null
    );
  }, [availableSubjects, formData.subject]);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * MODULES
   *
   * IMPORTANT:
   * Modules come from notesData.js.
   * PYQ upload does not use modules.
   * =========================================================
   */

  const availableModules = useMemo(() => {
    if (uploadType !== "notes") {
      return [];
    }

    return currentSubjectData?.modules || [];
  }, [uploadType, currentSubjectData]);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * EXAM YEARS
   * =========================================================
   */

  const examYears = useMemo(() => {
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();

    return Array.from(
      { length: 10 },
      (_, index) => String(currentYear - index)
    );
  }, []);

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * COMMON FORM UPDATE
   * =========================================================
   */

  const updateForm = (field, value) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [field]: value,
    }));

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * SELECT UPLOAD TYPE
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleUploadTypeSelect = (type) => {
    setUploadType(type);

    setFormData(createInitialForm());

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * STREAM CHANGE
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleStreamChange = (event) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,

      stream: event.target.value,

      year: "",
      semester: "",
      subject: "",
      module: "",
    }));

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * YEAR CHANGE
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleYearChange = (event) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,

      year: event.target.value,

      semester: "",
      subject: "",
      module: "",
    }));

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * SEMESTER CHANGE
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleSemesterChange = (event) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,

      semester: event.target.value,

      subject: "",
      module: "",
    }));

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * SUBJECT CHANGE
   *
   * IMPORTANT:
   * Whenever subject changes, module is reset.
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleSubjectChange = (event) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,

      subject: event.target.value,

      module: "",
    }));

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * FILE VALIDATION
   * =========================================================
   */

  const validateFiles = (files) => {
    const validFiles = [];

    for (const file of files) {
      if (file.type !== "application/pdf") {
        setError(
          `Only PDF files are allowed. "${file.name}" is not a PDF.`
        );

        return [];
      }

      if (file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
        setError(
          `"${file.name}" is larger than 25 MB.`
        );

        return [];
      }

      validFiles.push(file);
    }

    return validFiles;
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * ADD FILES
   * =========================================================
   */

  const addFiles = (fileList) => {
    const selectedFiles = Array.from(fileList || []);

    if (selectedFiles.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    const validFiles = validateFiles(selectedFiles);

    if (validFiles.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    setFormData((previous) => {
      const files = previous.multipleParts
        ? [...previous.files, ...validFiles]
        : [validFiles[0]];

      return {
        ...previous,
        files,
      };
    });

    setError("");
    setMessage("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * FILE INPUT
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleFileChange = (event) => {
    addFiles(event.target.files);

    event.target.value = "";
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * DRAG EVENTS
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleDragOver = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    setIsDragging(true);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    setIsDragging(false);
  };

  const handleDrop = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    setIsDragging(false);

    addFiles(event.dataTransfer.files);
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * REMOVE FILE
   * =========================================================
   */

  const removeFile = (index) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,

      files: previous.files.filter(
        (_, fileIndex) => fileIndex !== index
      ),
    }));
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * MULTIPLE PARTS TOGGLE
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleMultiplePartsChange = (event) => {
    const checked = event.target.checked;

    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,

      multipleParts: checked,

      files: checked
        ? previous.files
        : previous.files.slice(0, 1),
    }));
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * VALIDATION
   * =========================================================
   */

  const validateForm = () => {
    if (!uploadType) {
      return "Please select Notes or PYQs.";
    }

    if (!formData.stream) {
      return "Please select a stream.";
    }

    if (!formData.year) {
      return "Please select a year.";
    }

    if (!formData.semester) {
      return "Please select a semester.";
    }

    if (!formData.subject) {
      return "Please select a subject.";
    }

    if (
      uploadType === "notes" &&
      !formData.module
    ) {
      return "Please select a module / unit.";
    }

    if (
      uploadType === "pyq" &&
      !formData.examType
    ) {
      return "Please select CIE or SEE.";
    }

    if (
      uploadType === "pyq" &&
      !formData.examYear
    ) {
      return "Please select the examination year.";
    }

    if (!formData.title.trim()) {
      return "Please enter a title.";
    }

    if (formData.files.length === 0) {
      return "Please upload at least one PDF file.";
    }

    return "";
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * SUBMIT
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    const validationError = validateForm();

    if (validationError) {
      setError(validationError);

      return;
    }

    setIsUploading(true);

    try {
      const uploadId = `${uploadType}-${Date.now()}`;

      const storeName =
        uploadType === "pyq"
          ? PYQ_STORE
          : NOTES_STORE;

      const savedFiles = await saveFilesToDatabase(
        formData.files,
        storeName,
        uploadId
      );

      /*
       * =====================================================
       * PYQ UPLOAD
       * =====================================================
       */

      if (uploadType === "pyq") {
        const uploadedPYQ = {
          id: uploadId,

          stream: formData.stream,

          yearId: formData.year,

          semester: formData.semester,

          subjectId: formData.subject,

          subject:
            currentSubjectData?.name || "",

          code:
            currentSubjectData?.code || "",

          title: formData.title.trim(),

          description:
            formData.description.trim(),

          examType: formData.examType,

          year: formData.examYear,

          multipleParts:
            formData.multipleParts,

          files: savedFiles,

          storageId: uploadId,

          uploadedAt:
            new Date().toISOString(),

          status: "approved",
        };

        const existingPYQs =
          getLocalStorageArray(
            PYQ_STORAGE_KEY
          );

        existingPYQs.push(uploadedPYQ);

        saveLocalStorageArray(
          PYQ_STORAGE_KEY,
          existingPYQs
        );

        window.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent(
            "openNotesPYQUpdated"
          )
        );
      }

      /*
       * =====================================================
       * NOTES UPLOAD
       * =====================================================
       */

      if (uploadType === "notes") {
        const uploadedNote = {
          id: uploadId,

          stream: formData.stream,

          yearId: formData.year,

          semester: formData.semester,

          subjectId: formData.subject,

          subject:
            currentSubjectData?.name || "",

          moduleId: formData.module,

          module:
            availableModules.find(
              (module) =>
                module.id === formData.module
            )?.name || "",

          title: formData.title.trim(),

          description:
            formData.description.trim(),

          multipleParts:
            formData.multipleParts,

          files: savedFiles,

          storageId: uploadId,

          uploadedAt:
            new Date().toISOString(),

          status: "approved",
        };

        const existingNotes =
          getLocalStorageArray(
            NOTES_STORAGE_KEY
          );

        existingNotes.push(uploadedNote);

        saveLocalStorageArray(
          NOTES_STORAGE_KEY,
          existingNotes
        );

        window.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent(
            "openNotesNotesUpdated"
          )
        );
      }

      setMessage(
        uploadType === "pyq"
          ? "PYQ uploaded successfully."
          : "Notes uploaded successfully."
      );

      setFormData(createInitialForm());
    } catch (uploadError) {
      console.error(uploadError);

      setError(
        uploadError?.message ||
          "Something went wrong while uploading."
      );
    } finally {
      setIsUploading(false);
    }
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * RESET
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleReset = () => {
    setFormData(createInitialForm());

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * GO BACK TO TYPE SELECTION
   * =========================================================
   */

  const handleChangeUploadType = () => {
    setUploadType(null);

    setFormData(createInitialForm());

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  };

  /*
   * =========================================================
   * RENDER
   * =========================================================
   */

  return (
    <section className="upload-page">

      {/* =====================================================
          HEADER
      ===================================================== */}

      <header className="upload-header">
        <div className="upload-header-content">

          <div className="upload-header-text">

            <span className="section-label">
              OPENNOTES CONTRIBUTION
            </span>

            <h1>
              Share your
              <span> knowledge.</span>
            </h1>

            <p className="upload-description">
              Upload notes and previous year question
              papers to help students learn better.
            </p>

          </div>

          <div className="upload-header-icon">
            <UploadCloud size={32} />
          </div>

        </div>
      </header>


      {/* =====================================================
          TYPE SELECTION
      ===================================================== */}

      {!uploadType && (
        <section className="upload-type-section">

          <div className="upload-type-heading">

            <span className="section-label">
              UPLOAD TYPE
            </span>

            <h2>
              What do you want to upload?
            </h2>

            <p>
              Select the type of academic material
              you want to contribute.
            </p>

          </div>


          <div className="upload-type-grid">

            {/* NOTES */}

            <button
              type="button"
              className="upload-type-card"
              onClick={() =>
                handleUploadTypeSelect("notes")
              }
            >

              <div className="upload-type-icon">
                <FileText size={28} />
              </div>

              <div className="upload-type-content">

                <strong>
                  Notes
                </strong>

                <span>
                  Upload teacher or student
                  notes organized by module.
                </span>

              </div>

              <ChevronRight
                className="upload-type-arrow"
                size={22}
              />

            </button>


            {/* PYQs */}

            <button
              type="button"
              className="upload-type-card"
              onClick={() =>
                handleUploadTypeSelect("pyq")
              }
            >

              <div className="upload-type-icon">
                <FileQuestion size={28} />
              </div>

              <div className="upload-type-content">

                <strong>
                  Previous Year Questions
                </strong>

                <span>
                  Upload CIE or SEE question
                  papers organized by exam year.
                </span>

              </div>

              <ChevronRight
                className="upload-type-arrow"
                size={22}
              />

            </button>

          </div>

        </section>
      )}


      {/* =====================================================
          UPLOAD FORM
      ===================================================== */}

      {uploadType && (
        <form
          className="upload-form"
          onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        >

          {/* CHANGE TYPE */}

          <button
            type="button"
            className="change-upload-type"
            onClick={handleChangeUploadType}
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={17} />
            Change upload type
          </button>


          {/* =================================================
              ACADEMIC DETAILS
          ================================================= */}

          <section className="upload-section">

            <div className="upload-section-heading">

              <div>
                <span className="section-label">
                  {uploadType === "notes"
                    ? "NOTES DETAILS"
                    : "PYQ DETAILS"}
                </span>

                <h2>
                  Academic information
                </h2>
              </div>

            </div>


            <div className="upload-form-grid">

              {/* STREAM */}

              <div className="form-group">

                <label htmlFor="stream">
                  Stream <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <select
                  id="stream"
                  value={formData.stream}
                  onChange={handleStreamChange}
                  required
                >

                  <option value="">
                    Select stream
                  </option>

                  {(uploadType === "notes"
                    ? noteStreams
                    : pyqStreams
                  ).map((stream) => (
                    <option
                      key={stream.id}
                      value={stream.id}
                    >
                      {stream.title}
                    </option>
                  ))}

                </select>

              </div>


              {/* YEAR */}

              <div className="form-group">

                <label htmlFor="year">
                  Year <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <select
                  id="year"
                  value={formData.year}
                  onChange={handleYearChange}
                  disabled={
                    !formData.stream ||
                    availableYears.length === 0
                  }
                  required
                >

                  <option value="">
                    {!formData.stream
                      ? "Select stream first"
                      : availableYears.length === 0
                      ? "Coming soon"
                      : "Select year"}
                  </option>

                  {availableYears.map(
                    (year) => (
                      <option
                        key={year.id}
                        value={year.id}
                      >
                        {year.title}
                      </option>
                    )
                  )}

                </select>

              </div>


              {/* SEMESTER */}

              <div className="form-group">

                <label htmlFor="semester">
                  Semester <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <select
                  id="semester"
                  value={formData.semester}
                  onChange={handleSemesterChange}
                  disabled={
                    !formData.year ||
                    availableSemesters.length === 0
                  }
                  required
                >

                  <option value="">
                    {!formData.year
                      ? "Select year first"
                      : availableSemesters.length === 0
                      ? "Coming soon"
                      : "Select semester"}
                  </option>

                  {availableSemesters.map(
                    (semester) => (
                      <option
                        key={semester.id}
                        value={semester.id}
                      >
                        {semester.title}
                      </option>
                    )
                  )}

                </select>

              </div>


              {/* SUBJECT */}

              <div className="form-group">

                <label htmlFor="subject">
                  Subject <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <select
                  id="subject"
                  value={formData.subject}
                  onChange={handleSubjectChange}
                  disabled={
                    !formData.semester ||
                    availableSubjects.length === 0
                  }
                  required
                >

                  <option value="">
                    {!formData.semester
                      ? "Select semester first"
                      : availableSubjects.length === 0
                      ? "No subjects available"
                      : "Select subject"}
                  </option>

                  {availableSubjects.map(
                    (subject) => (
                      <option
                        key={subject.id}
                        value={subject.id}
                      >
                        {subject.code
                          ? `${subject.code} — ${subject.name}`
                          : subject.name}
                      </option>
                    )
                  )}

                </select>

              </div>


              {/* =================================================
                  MODULE
                  NOTES ONLY
              ================================================= */}

              {uploadType === "notes" && (
                <div className="form-group">

                  <label htmlFor="module">
                    Module / Unit <span>*</span>
                  </label>

                  <select
                    id="module"
                    name="module"
                    value={formData.module}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateForm(
                        "module",
                        event.target.value
                      )
                    }
                    disabled={
                      !formData.subject ||
                      availableModules.length === 0
                    }
                    required
                  >

                    <option value="">
                      {!formData.subject
                        ? "Select subject first"
                        : availableModules.length === 0
                        ? "No modules available"
                        : "Select module"}
                    </option>

                    {availableModules.map(
                      (module) => (
                        <option
                          key={module.id}
                          value={module.id}
                        >
                          {module.name}
                        </option>
                      )
                    )}

                  </select>

                </div>
              )}


              {/* =================================================
                  PYQ EXAM TYPE
              ================================================= */}

              {uploadType === "pyq" && (
                <div className="form-group">

                  <label>
                    Examination Type <span>*</span>
                  </label>

                  <div className="choice-grid">

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={`choice-card ${
                        formData.examType === "CIE"
                          ? "choice-card-selected"
                          : ""
                      }`}
                      onClick={() =>
                        updateForm(
                          "examType",
                          "CIE"
                        )
                      }
                    >
                      <strong>
                        CIE
                      </strong>

                      <small>
                        Continuous Internal
                        Evaluation
                      </small>
                    </button>


                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={`choice-card ${
                        formData.examType === "SEE"
                          ? "choice-card-selected"
                          : ""
                      }`}
                      onClick={() =>
                        updateForm(
                          "examType",
                          "SEE"
                        )
                      }
                    >
                      <strong>
                        SEE
                      </strong>

                      <small>
                        Semester End
                        Examination
                      </small>
                    </button>

                  </div>

                </div>
              )}


              {/* =================================================
                  EXAM YEAR
              ================================================= */}

              {uploadType === "pyq" && (
                <div className="form-group">

                  <label htmlFor="examYear">
                    Examination Year <span>*</span>
                  </label>

                  <select
                    id="examYear"
                    value={formData.examYear}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateForm(
                        "examYear",
                        event.target.value
                      )
                    }
                    required
                  >

                    <option value="">
                      Select exam year
                    </option>

                    {examYears.map(
                      (year) => (
                        <option
                          key={year}
                          value={year}
                        >
                          {year}
                        </option>
                      )
                    )}

                  </select>

                </div>
              )}

            </div>

          </section>


          {/* =================================================
              TITLE + DESCRIPTION
          ================================================= */}

          <section className="upload-section">

            <div className="upload-section-heading">

              <div>

                <span className="section-label">
                  CONTENT
                </span>

                <h2>
                  {uploadType === "notes"
                    ? "Note information"
                    : "Paper information"}
                </h2>

              </div>

            </div>


            <div className="upload-form-grid">

              {/* TITLE */}

              <div className="form-group form-group-full">

                <label htmlFor="title">
                  Title <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <input
                  id="title"
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.title}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateForm(
                      "title",
                      event.target.value
                    )
                  }
                  placeholder={
                    uploadType === "notes"
                      ? "Enter note title"
                      : "Enter question paper title"
                  }
                  required
                />

              </div>


              {/* DESCRIPTION */}

              <div className="form-group form-group-full">

                <label htmlFor="description">
                  Description
                </label>

                <textarea
                  id="description"
                  rows="5"
                  value={formData.description}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateForm(
                      "description",
                      event.target.value
                    )
                  }
                  placeholder={
                    uploadType === "notes"
                      ? "Write a short description about these notes..."
                      : "Write a short description about this question paper..."
                  }
                />

              </div>

            </div>

          </section>


          {/* =================================================
              MULTIPLE PARTS
          ================================================= */}

          <section className="upload-section">

            <div className="checkbox-wrapper">

              <input
                id="multipleParts"
                type="checkbox"
                className="custom-checkbox"
                checked={
                  formData.multipleParts
                }
                onChange={
                  handleMultiplePartsChange
                }
              />

              <label
                htmlFor="multipleParts"
                className="checkbox-text"
              >
                <strong>
                  This upload has multiple parts
                </strong>

                <span>
                  Enable this if the material is
                  divided into multiple PDF files.
                </span>
              </label>

            </div>

          </section>


          {/* =================================================
              FILE UPLOAD
          ================================================= */}

          <section className="upload-section">

            <div className="upload-section-heading">

              <div>

                <span className="section-label">
                  DOCUMENT
                </span>

                <h2>
                  Upload PDF
                </h2>

              </div>

            </div>


            <label
              className={`upload-drop-zone ${
                isDragging
                  ? "drag-active"
                  : ""
              }`}
              onDragOver={handleDragOver}
              onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
              onDrop={handleDrop}
            >

              <input
                type="file"
                accept="application/pdf,.pdf"
                multiple={
                  formData.multipleParts
                }
                onChange={handleFileChange}
                hidden
              />

              <div className="upload-drop-icon">
                <UploadCloud size={34} />
              </div>

              <strong>
                Drag & drop your PDF here
              </strong>

              <span>
                or click to browse
              </span>

              <small>
                PDF only • Maximum 25 MB per file
              </small>

            </label>


            {/* SELECTED FILES */}

            {formData.files.length > 0 && (
              <div className="selected-files">

                <div className="selected-files-header">

                  <strong>
                    Selected files
                  </strong>

                  <span className="file-count">
                    {formData.files.length}
                    {formData.files.length === 1
                      ? " file"
                      : " files"}
                  </span>

                </div>


                <div className="selected-file-list">

                  {formData.files.map(
                    (file, index) => (
                      <div
                        className="selected-file"
                        key={`${file.name}-${index}`}
                      >

                        <div className="selected-file-icon">
                          <FileText size={21} />
                        </div>


                        <div className="selected-file-info">

                          <strong>
                            {file.name}
                          </strong>

                          <span>
                            {(
                              file.size /
                              (1024 * 1024)
                            ).toFixed(2)}{" "}
                            MB
                          </span>

                        </div>


                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="remove-file-button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            removeFile(index)
                          }
                          aria-label={`Remove ${file.name}`}
                        >
                          <X size={17} />
                        </button>

                      </div>
                    )
                  )}

                </div>

              </div>
            )}

          </section>


          {/* =================================================
              MESSAGES
          ================================================= */}

          {error && (
            <div className="upload-message upload-error">
              <X size={19} />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}


          {message && (
            <div className="upload-message upload-success">
              <CheckCircle2 size={19} />
              <span>{message}</span>
            </div>
          )}


          {/* =================================================
              ACTIONS
          ================================================= */}

          <div className="upload-actions">

            <button
              type="button"
              className="upload-reset-button"
              onClick={handleReset}
              disabled={isUploading}
            >
              Reset
            </button>


            <button
              type="submit"
              className="upload-submit-button"
              disabled={isUploading}
            >

              {isUploading ? (
                <>
                  <span className="upload-spinner" />
                  Uploading...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <UploadCloud size={19} />
                  Upload{" "}
                  {uploadType === "notes"
                    ? "Notes"
                    : "PYQ"}
                </>
              )}

            </button>

          </div>

        </form>
      )}

    </section>
  );
}

export default Upload;
