========== Frontend\src\services\api.js ==========

const API_BASE_URL = (
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || "http://localhost:5000/api/v1"
).replace(/\/$/, "");

const TOKEN_KEY = "openNotes-access-token";

export function getAccessToken() {
  return sessionStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
}

export function setAccessToken(token) {
  if (token) {
    sessionStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);
  } else {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  }
}

export async function apiRequest(path, options = {}) {
  const { headers = {}, body, ...rest } = options;
  const token = getAccessToken();

  const isFormData =
    typeof FormData !== "undefined" && body instanceof FormData;

  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_BASE_URL}${path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`}`,
    {
      ...rest,
      body,
      headers: {
        ...(body && !isFormData
          ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
          : {}),
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
        ...headers,
      },
    }
  );

  let payload;

  try {
    payload = await response.json();
  } catch {
    payload = null;
  }

  if (!response.ok || payload?.success === false) {
    const error = new Error(
      payload?.message || `Request failed (${response.status}).`
    );

    error.status = response.status;
    error.payload = payload;

    throw error;
  }

  return payload;
}

function toQuery(params = {}) {
  const query = new URLSearchParams();

  Object.entries(params).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== "") {
      query.set(key, String(value));
    }
  });

  const result = query.toString();
  return result ? `?${result}` : "";
}

export const authApi = {
  register: (data) =>
    apiRequest("/auth/register", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }),

  login: (data) =>
    apiRequest("/auth/login", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }),

  me: () => apiRequest("/auth/me"),

  logoutAll: () =>
    apiRequest("/auth/logout-all", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    }),
};

export const collegeApi = {
  search: (q, limit = 10) =>
    apiRequest(`/colleges/search${toQuery({ q, limit })}`),

  get: (id) =>
    apiRequest(`/colleges/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
};

export const academicApi = {
  list: (collegeId, filters = {}) =>
    apiRequest(
      `/academic-structures${toQuery({
        collegeId,
        ...filters,
      })}`
    ),
};

export const resourceApi = {
  notes: (params = {}) =>
    apiRequest(`/notes${toQuery(params)}`),

  pyqs: (params = {}) =>
    apiRequest(`/pyqs${toQuery(params)}`),

  getNote: (id) =>
    apiRequest(`/notes/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),

  getPYQ: (id) =>
    apiRequest(`/pyqs/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),

  uploadNotes: (formData) =>
    apiRequest("/notes", {
      method: "POST",
      body: formData,
    }),

  uploadPYQ: (formData) =>
    apiRequest("/pyqs", {
      method: "POST",
      body: formData,
    }),

  myUploads: (params = {}) =>
    apiRequest(`/resources/mine${toQuery(params)}`),

  download: (type, id, partId) =>
    apiRequest(
      `/${type}/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/download/${encodeURIComponent(partId)}`
    ),

  pending: (type, params = {}) =>
    apiRequest(`/admin/resources/pending${toQuery({ type, ...params })}`),

  approve: (type, id) =>
    apiRequest(`/admin/resources/${type}/${id}/approve`, {
      method: "PATCH",
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    }),

  reject: (type, id, rejectionReason) =>
    apiRequest(`/admin/resources/${type}/${id}/reject`, {
      method: "PATCH",
      body: JSON.stringify({ rejectionReason }),
    }),
};


========== Frontend\src\services\authService.js ==========

import {
  mockUsers,
  ROLES,
  USER_STATUS,
  TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS,
} from "../data/authData";

const REGISTERED_USERS_KEY = "openNotes-registered-users";
const USER_OVERRIDES_KEY = "openNotes-user-overrides";

function readJSON(key, fallback = []) {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (!raw) return fallback;

    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed ?? fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function readRegisteredUsers() {
  const users = readJSON(REGISTERED_USERS_KEY, []);
  return Array.isArray(users) ? users : [];
}

function readOverrides() {
  const overrides = readJSON(USER_OVERRIDES_KEY, []);
  return Array.isArray(overrides) ? overrides : [];
}

function writeJSON(key, value) {
  localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
}

function safeUser(user) {
  if (!user) return null;

  const { password, ...publicUser } = user;
  return publicUser;
}

function getAllUsers() {
  const allUsers = [...mockUsers, ...readRegisteredUsers()];
  const overrides = readOverrides();

  return allUsers.map((user) => {
    const override = overrides.find(
      (item) => item.email === user.email
    );

    return override
      ? { ...user, ...override }
      : user;
  });
}

function saveUserOverride(email, updates) {
  const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  const overrides = readOverrides();

  const existingIndex = overrides.findIndex(
    (item) => item.email === normalizedEmail
  );

  const updatedOverride = {
    ...(existingIndex >= 0 ? overrides[existingIndex] : {}),
    email: normalizedEmail,
    ...updates,
  };

  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    overrides[existingIndex] = updatedOverride;
  } else {
    overrides.push(updatedOverride);
  }

  writeJSON(USER_OVERRIDES_KEY, overrides);
}

export function registerMockUser({
  name,
  email,
  password,
  role,
}) {
  const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

  if (![ROLES.STUDENT, ROLES.TEACHER].includes(role)) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "This account type cannot be created through public signup.",
    };
  }

  if (
    getAllUsers().some(
      (user) => user.email.trim().toLowerCase() === normalizedEmail
    )
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "An account with this email already exists.",
    };
  }

  const isTeacher = role === ROLES.TEACHER;

  const newUser = {
    id:
      typeof crypto !== "undefined" &&
      typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function"
        ? crypto.randomUUID()
        : `user-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`,
    name: name.trim(),
    email: normalizedEmail,
    password,
    role,
    status: isTeacher ? USER_STATUS.PENDING : USER_STATUS.ACTIVE,
    teacherApprovalStatus: isTeacher
      ? TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS.PENDING
      : TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS.APPROVED,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  try {
    writeJSON(REGISTERED_USERS_KEY, [
      ...readRegisteredUsers(),
      newUser,
    ]);
  } catch {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to save the demo account in this browser.",
    };
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    message: isTeacher
      ? "Teacher registration submitted for approval."
      : "Account created successfully.",
    requiresApproval: isTeacher,
    user: safeUser(newUser),
  };
}

export function loginMockUser(email, password) {
  const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

  const user = getAllUsers().find(
    (item) => item.email.trim().toLowerCase() === normalizedEmail
  );

  if (!user || user.password !== password) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Incorrect email or password.",
    };
  }

  if (user.status === USER_STATUS.SUSPENDED) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "This account has been suspended. Contact an administrator.",
    };
  }

  if (user.status === USER_STATUS.REJECTED) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "This account registration was rejected.",
    };
  }

  if (
    user.role === ROLES.TEACHER &&
    user.teacherApprovalStatus !== TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS.APPROVED
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Your teacher account is awaiting administrator approval.",
    };
  }

  if (user.status === USER_STATUS.PENDING) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Your account is awaiting approval.",
    };
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Signed in successfully.",
    user: safeUser(user),
  };
}

export function getTeacherApprovalRequests() {
  return getAllUsers()
    .filter(
      (user) =>
        user.role === ROLES.TEACHER &&
        user.teacherApprovalStatus === TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS.PENDING &&
        user.status !== USER_STATUS.REJECTED
    )
    .map(safeUser);
}

export function updateTeacherApproval(email, decision) {
  const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();

  if (!["approve", "reject"].includes(decision)) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid approval decision.",
    };
  }

  const user = getAllUsers().find(
    (item) => item.email.trim().toLowerCase() === normalizedEmail
  );

  if (!user || user.role !== ROLES.TEACHER) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Teacher account not found.",
    };
  }

  if (
    user.teacherApprovalStatus !== TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS.PENDING
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "This teacher request has already been processed.",
    };
  }

  try {
    const approved = decision === "approve";

    saveUserOverride(normalizedEmail, {
      status: approved ? USER_STATUS.ACTIVE : USER_STATUS.REJECTED,
      teacherApprovalStatus: approved
        ? TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS.APPROVED
        : TEACHER_APPROVAL_STATUS.REJECTED,
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      message: approved
        ? "Teacher approved successfully."
        : "Teacher registration rejected.",
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Unable to save the decision in this browser.",
    };
  }
}


========== Frontend\src\services\dashboardService.js ==========

import { mockUsers, ROLES } from "../data/authData";

const REGISTERED_USERS_KEY = "openNotes-registered-users";
const USER_OVERRIDES_KEY = "openNotes-user-overrides";
const NOTES_KEY = "openNotes-uploaded-notes";
const PYQS_KEY = "openNotes-uploaded-pyqs";

function readArray(key) {
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || "[]");
    return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function getUsers() {
  const registeredUsers = readArray(REGISTERED_USERS_KEY);
  const overrides = readArray(USER_OVERRIDES_KEY);

  const users = [...mockUsers, ...registeredUsers];

  return users.map((user) => {
    const override = overrides.find(
      (item) =>
        item.email?.toLowerCase() === user.email?.toLowerCase()
    );

    return override ? { ...user, ...override } : user;
  });
}

function getUploads() {
  return {
    notes: readArray(NOTES_KEY),
    pyqs: readArray(PYQS_KEY),
  };
}

export function getAdminDashboardStats() {
  const users = getUsers();
  const { notes, pyqs } = getUploads();

  return {
    totalStudents: users.filter(
      (user) =>
        user.role === ROLES.STUDENT &&
        user.status !== "rejected"
    ).length,
    totalTeachers: users.filter(
      (user) =>
        user.role === ROLES.TEACHER &&
        user.status !== "rejected"
    ).length,
    pendingTeacherApprovals: users.filter(
      (user) =>
        user.role === ROLES.TEACHER &&
        user.teacherApprovalStatus === "pending" &&
        user.status !== "rejected"
    ).length,
    notesCount: notes.length,
    pyqsCount: pyqs.length,
    resourceCount: notes.length + pyqs.length,
  };
}

export function getUserDashboardStats(userEmail) {
  const email = userEmail?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const { notes, pyqs } = getUploads();

  const matchesUser = (item) => {
    const uploaderEmail = (
      item.email ||
      item.uploaderEmail ||
      item.uploadedByEmail ||
      ""
    ).toLowerCase();

    return Boolean(email && uploaderEmail === email);
  };

  const userNotes = notes.filter(matchesUser);
  const userPYQs = pyqs.filter(matchesUser);

  return {
    notesUploaded: userNotes.length,
    pyqsUploaded: userPYQs.length,
    totalUploaded: userNotes.length + userPYQs.length,
    pendingUploads: [...userNotes, ...userPYQs].filter(
      (item) => item.status === "pending"
    ).length,
  };
}
